## ⭐⭐ FUTURE FEATURE, RULED — PHI: EXPOSE THE FULL REGULAR-EXPRESSION ENGINE TO SNOBOL4 (Lon, 2026-08-23 s264, in-chat to CEO)
Verbatim in substance: *"we'll want to add the same feature that I did for snobol4python through the Φ and φ functions, where we expose the entire REGULAR EXPRESSION engine to SNOBOL4."* ⛔ The names are the GREEK LETTERS `Φ` and `φ` — corrected by Lon s264 after CEO first wrote them as ASCII "PHI/phi" — and they are TWO pattern primitives, not one, mapping regex named groups onto SNOBOL4's two assignment operators. Precedent (the PY code, read at `snobol4python/src/SNOBOL4python/_backend_pure.py`): **`Φ` (:626) = regex match with IMMEDIATE assignments** — every named group `(?P<name>…)` assigns into globals the moment the regex matches (SNOBOL4 `$` semantics, `_env._g[N] = STRING(V)`); **`φ` (:649) = regex match with CONDITIONAL assignments** — named groups assign only on overall pattern success (SNOBOL4 `.` semantics). Both compile-and-cache the regex (`_rexs`), anchor at the cursor, yield the matched slice, restore position on backtrack — i.e. each is already a well-behaved Byrd box. In SCRIP this lands as two boxes with the same Greek names. ⛔ NOT scheduled — recorded so it is not lost; it becomes rungs when Lon opens it. Routed by CEO s264.

## ⭐⭐ FUTURE FEATURE, RULED — Λ/λ: THE LAMBDA, CODE EMBEDDED IN THE PATTERN (Lon, 2026-08-23 s264, in-chat to CEO)
Verbatim in substance: *"Here is a feature we really need to add. The LAMBDA. This `PATTERN . tx . *FUNCTION(tx)` form is the current only way to embed expression code directly in the pattern. I wonder what a nice lambda feature done right in pure SNOBOL4 syntax would look like."* Precedent (the PY code, read s264): **`Λ` (`_backend_pure.py:572`) = IMMEDIATE evaluation as test** — evaluate the expression at match time, truthy → zero-width success, falsy/exception → FAIL (SNOBOL4's `*EQ(X,Y)` embedded-test, generalized); **`λ` (`:600`) = CONDITIONAL execution** — at match time the command is only COMPILED and pushed on the match's commit-stack (`cstack`), zero-width success, POPPED on backtrack; the stack runs in encounter order only when the whole match succeeds. Same immediate/conditional pairing as Θ/θ, δ/Δ, Φ/φ.
**THE SPELLING, LON'S OWN (s264, in-chat, correcting CEO's first sketch): `PATTERN . tx . *(OUTPUT = tx)`.** ⛔ CEO's first draft claimed `P *expr` "already is Λ" — WRONG per the manual (v3.7 pp.85–86, txt lines 3901–3937): a deferred `*expr` in pattern position evaluates INTO THE PATTERN — its value is matched at that point (or feeds LEN/TAB/…), and `*EQ(I,4)` tests only because null matches zero-width. Evaluation-as-pattern is not execution-as-side-effect. The lambda is the OTHER position: **a deferred expression as the TARGET of the assignment operators.** (a) **Conditional λ: `P . tx . *(expr)`** — `.` already fires only on success of the entire match, and `*` defers the target to that moment, so the expression EXECUTES at commit and discard-on-backtrack falls out of `.`'s own timing — no new commit machinery beyond what `.` already needs. (b) **Immediate Λ: `P $ tx $ *(expr)`** — same shape at `$`'s during-match timing: fires as matched, including on branches later abandoned. The PY code's own comments already spell both this way (`Λ`: *"P $ tx $ *func(tx)"* · `λ`: *"P . tx . *func(tx)"*). (c) THE ONE LANGUAGE EXTENSION REQUIRED: today the `.`/`$` target must be a name; the feature defines a deferred-expression target as EVALUATE AT THE OPERATOR'S TIME — if the result is a NAME the capture is assigned to it, otherwise the evaluation itself was the point. (d) Anonymous bodies remain the missing quarter: a `LAMBDA('args','body')` builtin (sugar over DEFINE+CODE, generated name, applied via existing `APPLY`).
**⭐⭐ MEASURED s264 (CEO, `x64/bin/sbl -bf`, witnesses `lam1..lam6` in the ceo scratchpad) — IT WORKS TODAY IN THE DESUGARED FORM, AND LON'S SUGAR IS A PURE DESUGAR ONTO IT:**
| spelling | result |
|---|---|
| `. *(.dummy = OUTPUT = tx)` | ⛔ ERROR 212 — a name VALUE is not an assignment target (+ the known s189 error-path segv) |
| `. *(OUTPUT = tx)` and `$ *(...)` | ⛔ side effect FIRES (prints), then match FAILS — value where name required |
| `. *$("dummy" IDENT(OUTPUT = tx, tx))` | ✅ WORKS — side effect + match ok + capture lands in `dummy` |
| `. *note(tx)`, note ends `note = .dummy :(RETURN)` | ⛔ side effect fires, match fails — a name returned BY VALUE is not a target |
| `. *note(tx)`, note ends `note = .dummy :(NRETURN)` | ✅ WORKS — NRETURN is the missing ingredient |
**THE RULED DESIGN (Lon s264, in-chat): `P . *(expr)` / `P $ *(expr)` — the compiler supplies the implicit `.dummy` NRETURN result "since the user did not", making the construct kosher in the language.** The sugar compiles the deferred expression as a generated name-returning thunk (exactly the proven lam6 mechanism); the expression runs for effect at the operator's own timing (`$` during match, `.` on whole-match success) and the capture assigns into the implicit target. ⭐ CEO recommendation, from this file's own NAMESPACE LAW: the implicit target is an ANONYMOUS HANDLE per site, never a shared visible `dummy` — synthetic names are anonymous handles, and a shared name under nesting/recursion is observable state. ⛔ ORACLE CAVEAT, measured: stock SPITBOL REJECTS the bare sugar (rows 1–2 above), so corpus programs using it are SCRIP-only — the lam4/lam6 desugared spellings are the oracle-gradeable forms. **⭐ SPEED, MEASURED s264 (2M-iteration match loop, wall clock, single runs, INDICATIVE ONLY — SCRIP dev build RT_OPT `-O0`-default unverified-pristine; SPITBOL = clean bench oracle):** SPITBOL: control 0.066s · inline-hack 0.207s (≈70 ns/iter lambda cost) · function-NRETURN 0.222s (≈78 ns/iter) — the hack buys only ~10% because it still pays `$()` name-build + `DIFFER` plumbing per iteration; the TRUE sugar (compiler-inlined BB thunk, anonymous target, zero plumbing) is strictly cheaper than both, so the hack UNDERSTATES the feature. SCRIP: control 0.153s · function-NRETURN 1.009s — **≈428 ns/iter, 5.5× SPITBOL's function cost** (at `-O0`), so the inlined-thunk feature has far more headroom in SCRIP than the oracle numbers suggest; Lon's "no function exists, except the BB linkage" is the design point. ⛔ **DIVERGENCE FOUND while measuring (hq_C-lane, reported s264):** SCRIP m3 FAILS the match on a deferred `$()` indirect-reference assignment target (`lam4.sno`/`bl_inl.sno` in the ceo scratchpad: side effect fires, match fails) where SPITBOL `-bf` matches ok — the inline form is blocked in SCRIP until cured. **TAXONOMY (Lon s264): THREE folding cases, ONE principle — fold whatever is invariant, defer only what is not:** (1) CONSTANT FOLDING — value known at compile time → fold to the value; (2) PATTERN CONSTANT FOLDING — pattern STRUCTURE known → fold to the wired BB graph; (3) **DEFERRED-EXPRESSION CONSTANT FOLDING** — the value is unknowable but the CODE is constant → fold to an inline BB thunk wired into the graph, evaluation alone deferred to match time. Case 3 is case 2's discipline applied to embedded expression code. **⭐⭐ RULED REFINEMENT (Lon s264, in-chat, verbatim in substance) — THE DISPATCH IS ON THE PROVABLE SYNTACTIC CLASS OF THE DEFERRED TARGET, AND THE IMPLICIT DUMMY IS AN ARTIFACT, NOT AN IMPLEMENTATION:**
- **`P . *F()` / `P . *E` (call to unknown F, bare variable E — result class UNKNOWN):** exactly the OLD semantics, no detour — F may legitimately NRETURN a name (lam6) and must keep working. *"We must keep 100% backward compatible... The reason: F is UNKNOWN."*
- **`P . *(expr)` where the expression's TOP OPERATOR provably cannot yield a name** (assignment, arithmetic, concatenation — value-producing forms): LAMBDA semantics — execute for effect at the operator's timing, and **elide the assignment entirely**: the implicit `.dummy` NRETURN is a SPEC FICTION for language-lawyering, never materialized — *"that is an artifact. This needs NOT do that and save the energy."* (As-if rule: the anonymous handle is unobservable, so the store may vanish — and this is why the handle must stay anonymous: a visible `dummy` would forbid the elision.)
- **A deferred expression the compiler can prove CONSTANT** folds outright — case (3) of the folding taxonomy.
- **BODY = ONE EXPRESSION, Python-style** — *"we allow ONE EXPRESSION and if you want more then you call a function."* Multi-statement `*(stmt; stmt; stmt)` REJECTED as too much. (Note: SNOBOL4 concatenation + embedded assignment already give light left-to-right sequencing inside one expression, so the restriction costs less here than in Python.)
⭐ **WHY COMPATIBILITY IS TOTAL, from the s264 measurements:** the new semantics claims ONLY the territory that is today a GUARANTEED failure — a value-yielding deferred target currently fires its side effect and then FAILS the match (lam2/lam3, measured), so no working program occupies the space the enhancement takes. The enhancement gives meaning to what could never work; nothing that works changes.
⛔ **ONE SEMANTIC LEFT TO RULE when rungs open:** a lambda expression that FAILS at evaluation — today failure propagates into match failure (measured); proposal: keep that (gives conditional lambdas test power, Λ heritage), but it needs Lon's word.
⭐⭐ **OPENED BY LON s264, same session** (in-chat: *"I want to fire up 4 more FLEET workers for this"* — a demo is the goal; natural fit is the JSON deserializer, inline λ building objects on commit / discarding on backtrack, which is also the ruled benchmark priority). Batons: the HQs mint (notified s264, before the seats wake — law 5); the deferred-`$()`-target divergence cure is already in hq_C's inbox as `bug-deferred-indirect-target`. Routed by CEO s264, corrected by Lon same session, design settled by measurement, refined by ruling, and opened — all in one day.

**Verbatim in substance:** *"What is your problem. Are you trying to implement SPITBOL's LOAD/UNLOAD. If so look at snobol4dotnet which does it all. Or look at x32. Also regarding saving and EXEC, we might have done something also for snobol4dotnet. Anyway. It's a feature. Can you do it?"*

⛔ **This SUPERSEDES the narrow framing that row `conform-unload-noop` had been working** (routing ERROR 022 correctly on an UNLOADed call). Lon's word is that the target is the **whole feature** — LOAD, UNLOAD, SAVE and EXEC — with **`/home/claude/snobol4dotnet`** (Jeffrey Cooper's C# implementation, verified present on this box) and **x32** named as the reference implementations. Routed by hq_C the same session it was given, per RULES.md's override duty; seat11 flagged it correctly and immediately, and is surveying the reference before touching SCRIP code — which is the right order.

⭐ **WHAT THIS DOES NOT CANCEL, stated because an override tends to swallow the findings made under the old framing.** Two results from that row stand and are independently true regardless of what LOAD/UNLOAD becomes:
1. **The m4 OPSYN function-name alias defect** — `OPSYN(.facto,'fact')` then `facto(4)`: silently fails as a call in mode 4 (loud after seat11's held-back fix). Unclaimed as of this writing; checked against all three existing OPSYN rows, none covers this shape.
2. **`corpus/tests/snobol4/crosscheck/rung10.sno` case 1 has been printing `PASS 1010_func_recursion (4/4)` in mode 4 over that silent failure** — a green board over a wrong answer, owned by row `witness-guard-conflates-call-failure`.

⚠️ **PRIOR ART IN THIS FILE — READ IT BEFORE STARTING, IT IS NOT A CLEAN SHEET.** §LIVE CURSOR s193 (seat7) already measured **nine UNLOAD arms against the live oracle** and cured `UNLOAD_fn`/`_UNLOAD_` to match every one by code *and* message text (user name → null string, and ⭐ **the variable's VALUE survives — UNLOAD removes only the function binding**; never-DEFINEd → silent success; builtin → ERROR 248; non-name string and integer → **succeed**, so the argument is TYPE-validated and never NAME-validated; null/no-arg and ARRAY → ERROR 201). ⛔ And the same section records that **`SETEXIT` is a write-only stub** — `_setexit_label` is written by `_SETEXIT_` and read by nothing — which is an *independent* blocker on `f12_load_unload`, proven independent by a probe containing no UNLOAD at all. **Do not re-derive any of this; do not assume it is still true either — it is measured history, so re-run the one command before relying on a line of it.**

## ⛔ LAWS THAT BIND EVERY RUNG (compact; RULES.md is the parent)
ASM-DIFF-FIRST (s132 supersedes MONITOR-FIRST — mint smallest repro, diff `.s` against a passing sibling, gdb only if the diff doesn't answer it, monitor available but not the first move nor trusted on an unproven-safe program) · TEMPLATE-ONLY (`x86(...)` is the only x86 producer) · BOTH-MEDIUM (no `MEDIUM_TEXT`/`MEDIUM_BINARY` gating in templates — the s91 tiny-site `MEDIUM_TEXT &&` conjunct is a standing violation, R-1 lifts it) · SCRIP FOLLOWS SPITBOL SEMANTICS, CASE-SENSITIVE · ONE AUTHORITY per fact (`emit_match_rbp`, `emit_arbno_rbp`, `emit_match_owns_startd`, `frame_slot_scan`… — never spell a fact twice: s68/s70 "spelled-twice disease") · KILLSWITCH BYTE-IDENTITY + COMPILE-TIME MD5 BLAST RADIUS over the corpus for every codegen change (never a single board run: the board's noise floor ≈5 flips green→red) · **A/B ON EMITTER BEHAVIOUR MUST SWAP `out/libscrip_rt.so`, NOT THE DRIVER** (the emitter lives in the `.so`; s68 vacuous-gate conviction) · GATE-BEFORE-LAND · VERIFY-INHERITED-BLOCKERS (re-test any "X is unavailable" with one command; gdb-404 cost 7 sessions) · CHAT-ESCALATION (any blocker only Lon can clear goes into chat as one question at the TOP of the reply; ≥2 zero-rung sessions ⇒ ask before code) · END-OF-CONTEXT LAW (do not open a hunt or deletion you cannot close; mint the repro, route it, stop) · REGENS ×3 after codegen touches (`util_regen_{benchmark,feature,demo}_s_artifacts.sh`) · ⛔⛔ **NO `-O2` BUILDS. EVER (Lon, 2026-08-23 s262 FACT RULE — this SUPERSEDES the O0-DEV-O2-BENCH text that stood here, which is DEAD and must not be followed).** `RT_OPT` is `-O0` for development AND benchmarks AND demos. Never pass `RT_OPT="-O2 …"`, never build an `-O2` RT_TAG, never quote an `-O2` number as the current state. TWO REASONS, the second the stronger: cost (~9m30 vs ~1m40 per template-touching rebuild, paid on every arm of a measure-and-cure loop), and ⭐ **it measures a compiler we are deleting** — the RT is moving to register-aware ASM (`GOAL-RTCC.md`, `src/runtime/rtx/*.S`) with C kept for at most a few large algorithms, so an `-O2` figure grades gcc's optimizer over code that will not exist. The LABELING duty survives and now reads `-O0`. Authority: `RULES.md` § NO `-O2` BUILDS · CONTAINER LAWS: backgrounded jobs die (build in foreground under `timeout`), `bash scripts/install_system_packages.sh` FIRST, clone `x64` oracle ALWAYS.

Continuation of this session's earlier grammar fix (closed 17/19 R-8 opsyn witnesses, see the R-8 bullet below) and the same-day FINDING that root-caused `opsyn_rebind_twice.sno`, wrote a fix duplicating alias state into `g_rt_gen_procs`, measured a SIGSEGV from a second dispatch path disagreeing with it, and reverted. Re-traced both remaining red witnesses with gdb rather than reasoning from the emitted `.s` alone — this DIRECTLY CONTRADICTED the reverted FINDING's own attribution of *which line* of `f14_opsyn.sno` was actually failing (it was `double(21)`, not `!21`; both compute the same value so the wrong attribution was invisible in stdout) and confirmed exactly why a rebound operator (`&`) but not a once-bound one (`!`/`@`) needs the dynamic `rt_call_arr_bl` path at all (rebinding poisons `lower_snobol4.c`'s `sno_t4_scan`/`sno_t4_target` compile-time fold).

Found and fixed THREE independent, previously-unknown gaps, none of them the mechanism the reverted FINDING guessed at: (1) **`g_user_call_hook` (the extension point `APPLY_fn` falls back to, and the entry point of `_usercall_hook`'s own already-correct alias-retry logic) is never installed in a standalone mode-4 binary at all** — its one assignment lives in `scrip.c`'s driver `main()`, never linked into a `--compile`d binary; measured directly via gdb (`g_user_call_hook == 0x0` for the whole lifetime of a mode-4 process). Fixed in `core_lib_init()` (guarded, so mode-3's own explicit wiring is untouched) — alone sufficient to fix `opsyn_rebind_twice.sno` completely. (2) **`rt_proc_call_open`/`rt_proc_fn`/`rt_proc_jmp_entry`** — the by-name-CALL family `bb_call_proc_staged.cpp` always emits together for any name that folds to a literal string but isn't itself in `g_rt_gen_procs` — **had no fallback for a `_func_buckets`-only alias.** Fixing only `rt_proc_call_open` produced a fresh, different SIGSEGV (a `jmp` through a still-NULL `rt_proc_fn` result), which is exactly the "two things that must agree, don't" shape the earlier revert also hit, but here inside ONE family rather than across two dispatch mechanisms — resolved by factoring one shared `static rt_proc_t *rt_proc_find_alias(const char *name)` helper and calling it from all three, so a fourth consumer can't independently drift. (3) **`rt_proc_epilogue_named`** — SNOBOL4's name-based RETURN convention (a proc's result is read from the variable named after the proc) — did its own unredirected `rt_proc_find` on the ALIAS name, so a call correctly dispatched to `twice`'s real code still read its result back from a variable named `double` that nothing had set (silently wrong, not a crash — the symptom regressed from "statement produces nothing" to "statement produces a blank line" the moment gap 2 alone was fixed, which is what surfaced this one). Same helper. Also fixed, latent, not load-bearing for either witness: `register_fn_alias`'s fallback `entry_label` was self-referential (pointed at the alias's own name instead of the real target) whenever the target had no independent `_func_buckets` entry of its own.

**None of these duplicate `g_rt_gen_procs` state for alias names** — the reverted attempt did, and that second, independently-mutable piece of state disagreeing with the first is exactly what produced its SIGSEGV. This design keeps one table of real procedures (`g_rt_gen_procs`) and lets every by-name-dispatch consumer redirect, at read time, through the one place `register_fn_alias` already records "what does this alias currently point at" (`_func_buckets`'s `entry_label`) — so none of them can disagree with each other, including across a runtime rebind.

**Verification:** DONE-WHEN passes. Full SNOBOL4 corpus regression 365/365 both modes, measured twice — once before and once after an intervening, unrelated 11-commit upstream pull (rebase-baseline rule honored; neither touched file overlapped the incoming commits). The concurrently-landed (different seat, same day, unrelated row) `test_gate_nreturn_by_name_value_broken.sh` passes against this fix. Icon/Prolog/Raku/Snocone/Rebus/polyglot smokes all clean. Valgrind-clean on both compiled witness binaries. `util_verify_s_artifacts_owed.sh`: OWED=0 (behavior-only change, no emission difference; its 24-item TROUBLE count is the pre-existing demo-reorg/icon_bench backlog this file already documents as separate follow-up rows).

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-23 seat10 (`/home/claude10`, Claude Sonnet 5; THE LOOP queue row `goto-tail-wires-audit`, rank 2) — **CLOSED, PROVEN SAFE, NO CODE CHANGE: THE SCRIP_GOTO_TAIL ARM'S MISSING rcx/rdx IS HARMLESS BY CONSTRUCTION, AND A SEPARATE ARM-INDEPENDENT CRASH WAS FOUND ALONG THE WAY.**

seat3's s190 open question (`bb_goto_deferred.cpp`'s tail-jmp arm supplies no rcx/rdx — correct for a RETURN exit, unwitnessed for a transferee that falls off its end). **Structural proof, not just an empirical shrug:** `x86_return_floater()`/`x86_freturn_floater()` (`x86_srf_floater`, x86_asm.h) load their landing address from a FIXED STACK SLOT (`[rsp+16]`/`[rsp+24]`) populated once at CALL time by the procedure's own entry (`f_α` saving its call-descriptor rcx to `[rsp+32]`, per `bb_define.cpp:527`'s frame contract) — never from the live rcx/rdx register at goto time. So RETURN was never "correct" by luck; it doesn't consult them at all. And a transferee that falls off its end (no `:(...)`) lands on a plain **compile-time-wired** `jmp` — the next statement (which builds its own operands from scratch) or, at the true end of the program, `jmp main_γ` = `exit()` — zero register reads either way, because Byrd-box continuation is a static jump, not runtime register dispatch. **Confirmed empirically too:** 3 constructed witnesses (mainline fallthrough; mid-program fallthrough from inside a normally-CALLed activation, no RETURN; fallthrough to true program end) all oracle-match on both `SCRIP_GOTO_TAIL=1` and `=0`, m3 and m4 — re-verified against a full rebuild after an unrelated 304-file upstream sync landed mid-session (SCRIP `a71d3034`; `bb_goto_deferred.cpp` itself untouched since s190). Regression lock `igt_computed_goto_end` + rest of the `igt_*` family + `216_indirect_goto_computed` still green (and the family's two ORIGINAL red witnesses correctly still fail under `SCRIP_GOTO_TAIL=0` — the harness discriminates). Broad crosscheck corpus: 355/357 m3, 353/357 m4 before the sync → **360/361 both modes after** (the other pre-existing fails/skips closed by unrelated concurrent rows in the same pull, not this one) — zero new reds either side. ⭐ **FOUND, NOT CHASED — A DIFFERENT DEFECT, ARM-INDEPENDENT:** a computed goto landing directly on a DEFINE'd procedure's OWN primary label (not an internal label — `f_α` reads its incoming rcx as a live call-descriptor pointer with no validity check) segfaults identically under both SCRIP_GOTO_TAIL arms; the oracle instead reports a clean `ERROR 242 -- function return from level zero` and exits 0. Pre-existing, not introduced by s190's fix or this row. Not fixed here — flagged for HQ to triage (possibly the same class as the open `setexit-write-only-stub` row, unverified). **FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-23-seat10-goto-tail-wires-audit-the-tail-arm-needs-no-rcx-rdx-because-nothing-downstream-reads-them.md`.

**WATERMARK:** SCRIP untouched (no code change — the row closes as proven-safe) · corpus (new probe pair) · `.github` this commit.

## ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-23 seat01 (THE LOOP queue row `160-pat-alt-inner-gen-resume`, hq_C s264) — **LANDED: `SCRIP_ALT_TAIL` DEFAULTS ON. ## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-22 seat05 (`/home/claude05`, Claude Sonnet 5; THE LOOP queue row `table-nested-subscript-cost`, rank 1 — THE SINGLE BIGGEST PERF LEVER ON THE BOARD per s199) — **MECHANISM NAMED AND MEASURED SEPARATELY (nested-value boxing exonerated, chained re-lookup is the dominant near-linear-additive cost, IDENT-guard dispatch is a large depth-independent tax, read+write pays 2x with zero sharing); COMPLETED A HALF-BUILT OPTIMIZATION (`SCRIP_SUB_AGG`/"container-only" was fully wired but its runtime entry point was a no-op alias) — 8-14% Ir reduction at the mechanism level, 5.1% end-to-end on real claws5, zero effect on depth-1 (correctly scoped), existing killswitch reused, corpus fail-set unchanged by name.**

Sibling row `table-flat-1level-segv` was already closed by seat6 ("does not currently reproduce") — unblocked, taken alone. **Oracle correction first:** the brief's own "0.17x" and seat6's addendum ladder both predate the same-day two-oracle ruling (RULES.md s255) and are graded against `x64/bin/sbl`, since measured to carry ~2.2-3.5x monitor-IPC overhead; re-measured against the clean oracle (`/home/resources/spitbol-clean/sbl`) at session start: **claws5 m3:sbl = 0.09x**, not 0.17x. Built 10 mechanism-isolation probes in `corpus/probe/table_nested/` (chain-depth read/incr at depth 1/2/3, value-type boxing, IDENT-guard isolation, plus the realistic 1/2-level claws5 ladder with real `claws5.dat`) — all oracle-identical both modes, graded only via explicit `BENCH_DIR=` override (never joins the scored suite). Fleet load averaged 10-14 throughout this session (7 concurrent seats mid-`make pristine`), making wall-clock readings swing up to 2x run-to-run — pivoted to `callgrind --tool=callgrind` `Ir` (contention-immune) via FIXED-WORK mode (`echo N | scrip --run`) with a marginal-slope method (Ir(50k)-Ir(5k))/45000, canceling one-time compile overhead exactly.

**Mechanism decomposition** (marginal Ir/op, unmodified tree): chain_read d1/d2/d3 = 642.5/1080.1/1521.0 (near-linear-additive per hop, NOT super-linear — root cause: every `[k]` is one independent `rt_subscript_var` call, and every call, hit or miss, mints a fresh 72-byte `VCELL_t` via the GC arena even for a plain read — the asm port's own comment at `rtx_icnsub.S:57-62` already names this "THE REAL DEFECT"). value_type_tbl = 650.1, within 1% of chain_read_d1 — nested-table value boxing is EXONERATED as a per-access cost (a `DESCR_t` costs the same 16 bytes regardless of payload type; the only real boxing cost is the one-time ~2KB `table_new()` alloc per distinct new key). IDENT-guard (wall-clock, 2 independent readings, effect size far outside any documented noise floor): ~2.7x a plain read at the same depth — IDENT() is not inlined, paying a `setjmp`+hash+switch dispatch tax on every call regardless of hit/miss. chain_incr d1/d2/d3 vs. matching chain_read: consistently ~1.7-1.9x, confirming zero sharing between the read-half and write-half of `t[...]=t[...]+1`.

**The cure:** `sx_sub_container_only` (lower_snobol4.c) already marks intermediate subscript hops "container-only" under an ALREADY-EXISTING killswitch `SCRIP_SUB_AGG` (default on), and codegen already dispatches those hops to a dedicated runtime symbol `rt_subscript_var_container_only` — but that function's body was `return rt_subscript_var(base, idx);`, a complete no-op paying full cost anyway. Implemented the fast path: on a table base, HIT, whose stored value is itself a container (`DT_T`/`DT_A`), return the value directly (a 16-byte struct copy) instead of minting a VCELL/NAMETRAP just to have the next hop immediately unwrap and discard it. **Correctness trap found and avoided:** `sx_subscript_lv` (the lvalue/write-target builder) marks EVERY hop container-only including the TRUE FINAL one, whose result feeds `IR_ASSIGN_VAR` directly as the actual store target — a naive fast path there would hand the store logic a disconnected value copy instead of a writable cell whenever the slot being overwritten currently holds a table/array. Fixed by excluding the final write-hop from the marking (`if (k < nidx - 1) sx_sub_container_only(sub);`) — the read-chain loop needed no such exclusion since its result always funnels through `IR_DEREF`, which is transparent to either representation. Net 13 lines, 2 files (`lower_snobol4.c` +1/-1, `pattern_match.c` +10/-1). Killswitch: the pre-existing `SCRIP_SUB_AGG=0` (no new global).

**Measured:** fix's isolated effect (Ir/op, before vs. after) — chain_read_d1 1.003x (correctly a no-op, no intermediate hop), chain_read_d2 0.917x, chain_read_d3 0.881x, chain_incr_d2 0.907x, chain_incr_d3 0.870x, value_type_tbl 0.857x — monotonic, mechanism-precise, zero effect on every depth-1 row. Whole-program real claws5 (callgrind, `iters:` held exactly 1=1 so the Ir totals are a fair comparison): 396,021,624 → 375,702,112 Ir, **−5.13%**. claws5_l2: −3.2%; claws5_l1 (control): +0.005% (noise, correctly ~zero). Cross-engine m3:sbl wall-clock: best low-contamination reading 0.20x; range across this session's noisy conditions 0.09x-0.20x — **not this row's citable number, the Ir evidence is; a precise reading needs a quiet box**, flagged as a loose end.

**Cross-reference:** `ir-ident-differ-inline` (seat07) landed concurrently this session and independently fixes the IDENT-guard mechanism this row separately measured (IDENT/DIFFER now inline to `descr_identical`, killswitch `SCRIP_IDENT_INLINE`) — pulled and rebased mid-session, auto-merged cleanly (disjoint call sites), reconfirmed all probes + real claws5 oracle-identical both modes and full corpus regression clean on the MERGED tree. Combined whole-program effect not independently measured (TIME-mode's `iters:` count was not stable enough across separate callgrind invocations at this session's contention level to trust a raw-Ir comparison) — the two fixes are structurally orthogonal and expected to compose, not asserted to.

**Corpus no worse:** `test_corpus_snobol4.sh` m3 PASS=357 FAIL=2, m4 PASS=355 FAIL=2 SKIP=2, run twice (own fix, and again post-merge) — fail-set identical by name to the pre-existing baseline (`160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume`, `demo_treebank` FAIL; `132_pat_fence_eps_recur_shallow`, `demo_porter` SKIP). Icon smoke 14/14 both modes (cross-language sanity; the mechanism is SNOBOL4-lowering-only, grep-confirmed). Prolog's 2 pre-existing FAILs structurally impossible for this row to have caused (different frontend/lowering file); not chased.

**Follow-ons named, not attempted (see FINDING for full scoping):** `subscript-read-no-vcell` (the biggest remaining lever — eliminate the VCELL allocation for the general READ case, any leaf type, not just container-valued hits; needs the same read/write-context IR distinction carried one level further, full-corpus blast radius); `ident-guard-subscript-cse` (claws5's own `x=IDENT(x)v` idiom re-evaluates the identical subscript expression 2-3 times per fresh key — a codegen CSE recognizing textually-identical LHS/IDENT-argument pairs); `TABLE_BUCKETS` hard-pinned at 256 with no growth path (not a factor here, flagged as latent risk for larger tables).

FINDING: `FINDING-2026-08-22-seat05-table-nested-subscript-cost-container-only-fastpath.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-22 seat11 (`/home/claude11`, Claude Sonnet 5; THE LOOP/HQ-dispatched queue row `rung-arbno-selfloop`) — **CURED, BOTH CAPTURE OPERATORS, BOTH MODES: `breakx.sno` (9.5M+-line runaway) NOW TERMINATES AND MATCHES ITS ORACLE. HQ's OWN "SENTINEL-SKIP" FIX SHAPE WAS INSUFFICIENT — CURES THE HANG BUT NOT THE ANSWER; THE REAL FIX NEEDED `sno_seq_nary`'S `out_rtail`, NOT A SENTINEL PREDICATE OVER `pe`.**

`lower_snobol4.c`'s `TT_CAPT_IMMED_ASGN`/`TT_CAPT_COND_ASGN` (`$`/`.`) took the captured pattern's retry target (`itail`) as `g->all[before_i]` unconditionally — correct only for a single-box capture. When the captured pattern is a multi-element group (e.g. `(BREAK(' ') ARBNO(LEN(1))) $ v`), that position holds `sno_seq_nary`'s own bookkeeping sentinel, wired `γ=nd,ω=save` at creation — `nd.ω` closed a 2-node self-loop with it (`FINDING-2026-08-22-s256-hq-...`). **First attempt (a sentinel-skip falling back to `pe`) cured the infinite loop but produced SILENTLY WRONG output** (`--dump-ir`-confirmed: retry re-entered BREAK, not ARBNO) — `pe` is the group's FORWARD entry (element 0), never the correct RETRY entry (the last element's own resume point, `sno_seq_nary`'s `out_rtail`/`res[ne-1]`, which the general `sno_pat_node` API discards). **Real fix:** new helper `sno_capt_body` (shared by both operators, NO-PER-OP-FILTER) that detects an unfenced multi-element group and calls `sno_seq_nary` directly with a real `out_rtail`, correctly resolving `itail` to the generator's own node; falls back to the original behavior (plus the sentinel-skip as a defensive net) for single-element/fenced captures. Had to additionally replicate `TT_VAR`'s stored-pattern-variable inline-resolution (caught by the `_stored` witness failing first-try) and relocate the helper past `sno_pat_inline_ok`'s definition (caught by the compiler: "static declaration follows non-static declaration"). **Verified:** all 15 `corpus/probe/retry/` witnesses match oracle both modes (new witness `rty_arbno_leftctx_cond` minted to falsify-then-confirm HQ's `.`-operator prediction per HQ LAW 17); `breakx.sno` itself terminates and matches `.ref` both modes; corpus m3 357/2 · m4 355/2/2skip unchanged; crosscheck m3 322/1 · m4 321/1/1skip · DIVERGE=0; two live gates green; `.s` regen touched only `claws5.s` (demo) + the two pre-existing `cond-assign-double-fire` crosscheck witnesses (re-verified still correct, bytes shifted but behavior unchanged) — zero Icon/Prolog/Rebus/benchmark movers, confirming the fix is SNOBOL4-only. Full trail: `FINDING-2026-08-22-seat11-arbno-selfloop-cured-both-capture-operators-with-a-shared-retry-tail-helper.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-22 seat2 (`/home/claude2`, Claude Sonnet 5; THE LOOP queue row `bench-harness-unmeasurable`, rank 0) — **THE BENCHMARK HARNESS COULD NOT BE INSTRUCTION-COUNTED AT ALL — REPRODUCED AND QUANTIFIED ON ALL 15 KERNELS, NOW FIXED WITH A FIXED-WORK MODE THAT ADDS ZERO NEW GLOBALS/KILLSWITCHES/BUILTINS.**

`corpus/benchmarks/snobol4/harness.inc`'s TIME-based mode (`ZBUD`=500ms wall-clock deadline) cannot be
callgrind-counted: under instrumentation every arm self-shrinks its batch to fit the deadline's *slowed*
real time, so total `Ir` measures callgrind's own throughput, not the kernel's. MEASURED across 14 of the
15 kernels (`array_sum` excluded, pre-existing unrelated valgrind SIGSEGV): native per-500ms iteration
counts span **3,840 to 75,497,472 (19,660x)**; default-mode callgrind `Ir` for the SAME 14 kernels spans
only **95,048,167 to 158,657,256 (1.67x)**, and the ordering does not track true cost (`table_access`, the
most expensive kernel per iteration, posts the *highest* Ir; `arith_loop`, the cheapest, posts the
third-highest). Full table and the two-kernel headline demonstration (arith_loop vs table_access, 20,481x
native gap collapsing to 0.95x under callgrind) in `FINDING-2026-08-22-seat2-bench-harness-unmeasurable.md`.

**Fix, per Lon's directive routed through the queue brief** (two modes, TIME- and ITERATION-based, variable
switch, all wrapping in the begin/end-bracket `.inc` files, `.sno` bodies untouched): `harness.inc` now does
`fixed_n = INPUT :S(ZFIXRUN)` right after the CHECK line — `INPUT` fails cleanly at EOF (existing
SNOBOL4/SPITBOL semantics, the same ones the CHECK phase already depends on), so `< /dev/null` (every
existing call site) is completely unaffected, while `echo N | scrip --run k.sno` runs `ZBODY` exactly `N`
times with NO wall-clock check anywhere in the path — batching at the kernel's own pinned `ZK` when it has
one, so `string_concat` (non-steady-state, batch size is part of the workload) still measures many
20,000-char batches, never one string built to the full length. Zero new globals, killswitches, or runtime
builtins — pure SNOBOL4 control flow inside an existing `.inc`. All 15 kernels regression-tested against
`.ref`, both modes, both engines (m3/m4): 15/15 pass; the family's own gate
(`scripts/test_bench_snobol4_timed.sh`) reports `ok=15 bad=0` unmodified. `NOISE-FLOOR.tsv` gained 45
fixed-work rows (new sibling script `scripts/bake_noise_floor_snobol4_fixed.sh`, SCRIP repo) beside the
existing TIME-mode rows.

**WATERMARK:** SCRIP `450368d0` · corpus `4b46a457` · `.github` at this commit. No compiler-source/codegen
files touched (`harness.inc` is corpus, not `src/`), but the 15 benchmark `.s` artifacts were regenerated
anyway via `util_regen_benchmark_s_artifacts.sh` since `harness.inc`'s content — and therefore every
kernel's compiled output — changed. One rebase conflict on `NOISE-FLOOR.tsv` against a concurrent session's
`bench-external-cpu-and-elapsed-clock` re-bake (17-column schema upgrade) resolved by merging both additions
— that session's newer TIME-mode data plus this session's fixed-mode rows, both already in the same
17-column format.

**Not chased, flagged only:** `indirect_dispatch`/`mixed_workload` are documented elsewhere as m4
XFAIL/SIGSEGV but passed cleanly in this session's testing — worth a fresh look before a future session
assumes that status is current, but out of scope for this row.

Defect's own cost 12,235,744,399 → 340,133,099 Ir (**36× cheaper**, now 2.14% of the new total, dominated by hash+occasional-collision-probe, not an O(n) scan). Native wall-clock (sandboxed, directional): m3 2.41s→1.49s (1.6×), m4 (independently built+linked+run) 0.48s→0.27s (1.8×). ## ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-22 seat4 (THE LOOP fleet; queue row `m3-executes-three-times-m4-on-beauty`, rank 0) — **REPRODUCED THE 3.06x m3-vs-m4 RUN-ONLY DIVERGENCE INDEPENDENTLY AND ROOT-CAUSED IT: NOT A CODEGEN ARM, A SHARED RUNTIME TABLE (`g_ab_fn_cells`) POLLUTED ASYMMETRICALLY BY THE JIT-VS-COMPILE-THEN-EXEC PROCESS-MODEL DIFFERENCE. CHECKED IN RED PER THE BRIEF'S OWN DONE-WHEN ALTERNATIVE, NOT CURED THIS SESSION.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `77b79602` unchanged — no codegen touched, no `.s` regen owed · corpus `600ed820` · `.github` at this commit): beauty M1 fixed point re-verified both modes before measuring (byte-identical to input, m3≡m4 output) · callgrind m3 run-only 14,785,003,419 Ir vs m4 run-only 4,833,116,573 Ir = **3.06x**, matching the brief's 3.00x within commit-drift noise.**
**(1) THE MECHANISM, PROFILED ON BEAUTY ITSELF, NOT GUESSED:** `emit.cpp:1366 bb_ab_slot_for` (linear `strncmp` scan, insert-on-miss, no hash) is reached via `rt.c:849 rt_dyn_alpha_fn` for every by-name dynamic-scope call. It is called the **same ~551K times in both media** (`bb_ab_fn_cell_ptr`: 551,979x m3 / 551,216x m4 — the call SITE is medium-symmetric, "one codegen" holds) but the table it scans is **4.7x deeper in m3** (avg depth 488.7 vs 104.2), because m3 (BINARY, one process for compile+run) carries every compile-time alpha-cell registration into the run phase, while m4 (TEXT, compile is a separate process that exits before the linked binary runs) starts the run process's table fresh — and `scrip.c:661`'s upfront seal loop explicitly **excludes any name containing `$`**, so the `EXPR$NN` deferred-expression-thunk names (gdb-confirmed live on beauty: `EXPR$31` alone hit 54,776x in a 105K-hit sample — SNOBOL4's `*expr` deferred-evaluation operator, the brief's own "EVAL/pattern machinery" lead) never get mode-4 upfront-sealed at all. Instruction and store counts are exact, deterministic and layout-independent; §3's censuses, §4's dead-template facts and §5's safety condition are structural and untouched. **(1) ⛔ THE ORIGINAL HEADLINE — CYCLE COLUMN RETRACTED PER (1b), COUNT COLUMNS STAND.** Three arms, same 50M-iteration kernel, hand-edited `.s`, all three printing the identical correct answer, **8 INTERLEAVED rounds A/B/C back-to-back in one loop, ranges DISJOINT**: **A shipped** 112.24 instr/it, 25.347 cyc/it, 24.05 stores · **B slot-coalesced** 112.25 instr, **26.408 cyc**, 24.05 stores = **+4.2% WORSE** · **C box-fused** 93.23 instr, **21.362 cyc**, 17.05 stores = **−15.7%**. B lost to A in 8 of 8 rounds. **Instruction count and store count are IDENTICAL between A and B** — the only variable is whether the binop writes its result back into the cell the var box just wrote or into a fresh one. Writing back into the just-read address serialises store-to-load forwarding on ONE address; two distinct addresses let the out-of-order engine overlap the hand-offs. ⭐ **THE SPINE'S APPARENT REDUNDANCY IS BUYING MEMORY-LEVEL PARALLELISM.** Sharing a cell changes a hand-off's ADDRESS, never the fact of it — so the row's target ("one round trip instead of three") is unreachable BY COALESCING, by construction.
**(2) ⭐ CONVERGENCE WITH seat1's `spine-carve-coalescing`, INDEPENDENTLY, THE SAME DAY — BOTH RANK-0 PERF ROWS DIED OF THE SAME WRONG MODEL.** seat1 measured the `sub rsp` carve **FREE behind two stores** (the 2-stores/cycle port limit retires it in their shadow) and recommends not implementing as specified; I measured slot sharing negative. **The shared false premise is that removing spine instructions/stores buys cycles.** On this box it does not: the hot loop is limited by the SERIAL store→load chain, not by instruction count or store count. Price every future spine idea against the DEPENDENCY CHAIN, never against an instruction census.
**(3) ⛔ BOTH CASES THE BRIEF NAMED FAIL ON ARRIVAL, AND ONE OF THEM MISCOMPILES AS WORDED.** `IR_COERCE_NUMERIC` is a **PAIRWISE** node whose emitted code branches on the **sibling's** tag — census of 337 programs / 266 nodes: 107 meet the brief's literal condition, only **50** are true copies (all `LIT_INTEGER/LIT_INTEGER`), **2 would MISCOMPILE** (`LIT_REAL` vs `LIT_INTEGER` — the literal must be PROMOTED, so it is not a copy), and **0 fire on `arith_loop`** (its 6 nodes are 4×`VAR/VAR`, 1×`CALL/VAR`, 1×`VAR/CALL`). Case (2) is not expressible at all: `cp_source`'s contract is "return the node whose slot this node merely copies", and **`IR_VAR` has no operands** — `copy_prop.c:14` returns 0 for every var that will ever exist. `IR_VAR` is a PRODUCER, not a copy. ⭐ After s249 took the null-concat, **NO `cp_source`-expressible shape remains on `arith_loop`'s chain.**
**(4) ⭐⭐ WHAT IS ACTUALLY THERE, AND IT IS SMALLER THAN I FIRST TOLD HQ: FUSION NEEDS *NO NEW IR KIND*.** `emit.cpp:1080` already dispatches `IR_BINOP` through `switch (g_emit.op_binop_kind)` by **CATEGORY** (`RELOP`/`CONCAT`/`XREP`/`ARITH`), and **one line above**, `emit.cpp:1078` already selects between two templates for ONE IR kind on a shape fact: `case IR_BINOP_TEST: g_emit.op_zres ? bb_binop_relop_val() : bb_binop_relop()`. So the fused arm is selectable inside the existing `BINOP_CAT_ARITH` case on a shape predicate applied uniformly to all five members `{ADD,SUB,MUL,DIV,MOD}` — a CATEGORY road, never an op list, so **NO-PER-OP-FILTER is satisfied by construction**. No new IR enum value, no new lowerer node, no PEERS-RULE field, no new global. ⭐ **AND TWO FUSION TEMPLATES ALREADY EXIST AS DEAD CODE:** `bb_binop_gvar_arith()` and `bb_binop_gvar_arith_slot()` are both declared (`bb_templates.h:82,83`), both defined, and `grep -rn` over all of `src/` finds **ZERO call sites for either**. The `_slot` second arm already handles exactly `{ADD,SUB,MUL,DIV,MOD}` × `{LIT_INTEGER, VAR}`. Someone built these arms and never wired them. What remains genuinely new is only the DATAFLOW half, and it is the smaller half: a fused template that materialises both operands inline makes the `VAR` and `LIT_INTEGER` boxes fall out of the reference set — **which is precisely the pipeline the brief itself describes** (node leaves the reference set → becomes `IR_SUCCEED` → `bc_chase()` walks γ through it → the box never executes). Writing the result straight to the GVA instead of the spine slot is what additionally kills the `ASSIGN` box and yields arm C's ONE round trip.
**(5) THE SAFETY CONDITION, WRITTEN OUT BEFORE ANY CODE AS THE BRIEF DEMANDS.** Fusing `IR_ASSIGN(V) ← IR_BINOP(op, IR_VAR(V), IR_LIT_INTEGER)` is legal iff: (a) `op ∈ {ADD,SUB,MUL,DIV,MOD}`, the closed arith family, every member treated identically; (b) `V` is GVA-resident and the SAME name on both sides; (c) the `BINOP` is the `ASSIGN`'s sole consumer and the `VAR` is the `BINOP`'s sole consumer; (d) **nothing executes between the read and the write that can write `V`** — with both operands materialisable inline the interval contains NO OTHER BOX, so this holds by construction, which is exactly why this narrow shape is the right first rung. ⛔ (d) is also the general rule and why the brief's `f(x)+A` / `A+f(x)` asymmetry must never be approximated: **a var box's spine slot is a SNAPSHOT taken at its own α**, and any scheme that deletes the box and re-reads the GVA at the CONSUMER's α moves the read LATER IN TIME. The general predicate belongs in ONE property-named function, `ir_node_may_write_globals(nd)`, answered uniformly for whole families (ALL `IR_CALL*` may write; ALL `IR_MATCH_*` may write, since `.` and `$` assign during matching; ALL literals may not) — never a per-op list.
**(6) ⛔ WHAT I DID NOT DO, AND WHY — SO NOBODY PAYS FOR IT TWICE.** Did **not** land case (1) in its sound form (both operands static AND same type): it is correct and fires 50 times, but every site is off-chain — the 0.02 cyc/instr class — so landing it would repeat exactly the mistake the brief's own ⛔⛔ banner exists to prevent, and it cannot meet the row's DONE-WHEN. Did **not** build the fusion: it is the design call HQ owns and I do not freelance past a blocker.

## ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-21 s250 (seat1 `/home/claude1`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `substr-zero-length`, rank 1) — **SUBSTR WAS *FOUR* DEFECTS, NOT TWO, AND THE OMITTED-`N` FORM WAS ONLY WORKING *BECAUSE* OF ONE OF THEM. CURED, BOTH MODES. THE s186 `SUBSTR` RED FLIPS GREEN.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `58fd2369` · corpus `f9d15f87` · `.github` `bf540f41`, `make pristine` `PRISTINE_RC=0`, RT_OPT `-O0`, oracle `sbl -b` verified alive first): `corpus/programs/csnobol4-suite/substr.sno` matches its `.ref` in m3 AND m4 (was a 111-line diff) · corpus `test_corpus_snobol4.sh` m3 `338/2` → `339/2`, m4 `337/2/1` → `338/2/1`, SAME two failing names (`160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume`, `demo_treebank`), the +1 total being the new crosscheck test passing both modes · 61 corpus programs calling `SUBSTR(` diffed pre/post binary: 4 changed, ALL accounted for, 57 byte-identical · re-emitted `066_builtin_substr.s` byte-identical, so runtime-only and NO `.s` regen owed · `test_gate_emit_no_lang` rc=0, `test_gate_rtx_inventory_live` rc=0.**
**(1) THE CONTRACT WAS MEASURED BEFORE ANY CODE MOVED, AND THREE ORACLES AGREE BYTE-FOR-BYTE** (`FINDING-2026-08-21-seat1-substr-zero-length-was-four-defects-and-the-omitted-arg-was-riding-the-bug.md`). SPITBOL `x64/bin/sbl`, `csnobol4-clean` and the live csnobol4 all produce identical probe output: **`SUBSTR(S,I,N)` succeeds iff `1 ≤ I ≤ SIZE(S)+1` and `0 ≤ N ≤ SIZE(S)−I+1`; `N = 0` and `N` OMITTED are the SAME contract — both mean TO THE END OF THE STRING; everything else FAILS.** The row flagged zero-vs-omitted as an open question; the probe closed it as one contract, not two.
**(2) THE ROW SAID TWO FACTS; IT IS FOUR:** `N = 0` returned `""` instead of the tail · `N < 0` returned `""` instead of failing · **`N > remaining` silently CLAMPED instead of failing** · `I > SIZE(S)+1` returned `""` instead of failing. The row predicted the first two. The clamp and the `I` bound were found only by probing the grid the row demanded.
**(3) ⛔⛔ THE COUPLING, AND WHY A ZERO-ONLY CURE WOULD HAVE BROKEN THE CORPUS:** `SUBSTR_fn` had NO WAY to express "to the end", so the two-argument form passed a MAGIC LENGTH (`999999999` in `core.c:984`, `1000000000` in `by_name_dispatch.c:5098`) and **relied on defect 3's clamp to cut it back**. `SUBSTR(S,I)` was correct only *because* overlong lengths clamped. Fixing defect 3 alone — as the oracle demands — would have made every two-argument `SUBSTR` in the corpus start FAILING. The two facts are coupled and the coupling is invisible unless you probe the OMITTED form.
**(4) CURE = 4 LINES FOR 4, NO NEW MECHANISM, NO NEW GLOBAL, TWO MAGIC NUMBERS DELETED:** since the oracle says `N=0` ≡ omitted, the code now says so — both synthesised defaults become that zero, and `big`/`1000000000` leave the tree rather than being re-tuned. **One caller needed insulating:** `by_name_dispatch.c:551` is the **Raku method form** `s.substr(from,len)` — a different surface, 0-based, its own default and its own "negative means empty" clamp. It shares `SUBSTR_fn` but NOT its contract, so it now clamps into range and returns empty for a zero span itself. `str_substr.raku` still prints `world`, byte-identical to baseline.
**(5) ⭐ THE s186 RED FLIPS GREEN, AND THE CORPUS ALREADY HELD THE WITNESS.** This file has recorded the defect since s186 (batch-C block below): *"a built-in semantics gap (`SUBSTR('ABC',2,5)` returns `BC` and SUCCEEDS where Spitbol FAILS)"*, with `SUBSTR` in the **checked-in-red** driver list per law 0d. `corpus/programs/gimpel/SUBSTR_driver.sno` — whose last case is commented *"a request past the end"* — printed `[BC]`, took its `:S(DONE)` branch and never printed the failure message. **It is now green against its own `.ref` and against SPITBOL.** Checking drivers in red worked exactly as intended: the witness sat there until the cure arrived.
**(6) ⛔ CROSS-FRONT WARNING FOR ANYONE CITING THE csnobol4 ORACLE ON THIS FRONT** (seat1's other rung this session, `csnobol4-clean-fork`): the live `/home/claude/csnobol4/snobol4` **drops a scanner start position** on `crosscheck/patterns/172_pat_fail_forces_retry.sno`, a program with **no FENCE in it**. SPITBOL, upstream+keepers, our own pre-FENCE snapshot and the `.ref` all disagree with it. Bisected to `723ac19` ("gate-neutral"), whose edit exists **only in generated `isnobol4.c`** — `v311.sil` still carries the original unconditional `PUTLG TXSP,YCL`, so that oracle is **not reproducible from its own source**. Re-check any SNOBOL4 pattern verdict citing csnobol4 since 2026-05-01. SPITBOL was unaffected and stayed trustworthy throughout.
**(7) CHECKED IN:** `corpus/crosscheck/strings/076_builtin_substr_bounds.sno` + `.ref` — the 26-case boundary grid, **`.ref` generated FROM the live SPITBOL oracle** then independently confirmed byte-identical against both CSNOBOL4 binaries, so it is an oracle ref and not a snapshot of SCRIP's own output.
**(8) NEXT, NAMED:** the standing front red `160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume` and `demo_treebank` are untouched by this rung · `test_gate_no_lang_names` and `test_gate_lower_isolation` are rc=1 **and were rc=1 with this change stashed** — pre-existing, `src/emitter/emit.cpp` and `src/lower/lower_pascal.c → parser/pascal/pascal_driver.h`, each wants its own row · the remaining s186 batch-C reds (`SEQ SSORT TEMP TEST TICTACTO TREE TSORT AI AOPA BRKREM SQRT TUPLE ARC ASM`) are unaffected by this cure; `SQRT` is still a missing built-in, not a pattern red.
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-21 s249 (seat2 `/home/claude2`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `setexit-write-only-stub`, rank 1) — **SETEXIT WAS *THREE* MISSING MECHANISMS AND A LITERAL OPERAND HIDES TWO OF THEM. TRAP CURED; RAISE AND RESUME NAMED, WITNESSED, NOT CLAIMED.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `64e2d763`, RE-PROVED IDENTICAL after rebase onto `979feb4a`, RT_OPT `-O0`, oracle `sbl -bf` verified alive first AND all 9 refs re-verified byte-exact after x64 moved to `ec80390`): SETEXIT faces `1/9` → `3/9` m3 AND m4 identical by name · killswitch `SCRIP_SETEXIT=0` → `1/9` both modes (NON-VACUOUS) · A/B `.s` 0 movers / 130 crosscheck programs AND step-4 regen all six trees `changed=0` (623 programs + 487 crosscheck + 22 prolog bench) · crosscheck m3 319/1 m4 318/1 DIVERGE 0, sole FAIL `160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume` = the standing front red · smoke 7/7 both modes · 3 gates green.**
**(1) THE ROW SAID ONE MECHANISM; IT IS THREE** (read `FINDING-2026-08-21-s249-setexit-was-three-missing-mechanisms-and-the-literal-operand-hides-two-of-them.md`): **RAISE** (most SCRIP errors never reach `core_runtime_error` — `1/D` with `D=0` is SILENTLY degraded to statement failure where the oracle raises 014 and aborts) · **TRAP** (`_setexit_label`, 3 writes 0 reads — CURED) · **RESUME** (`:(CONTINUE)`/`:(SCONTINUE)`, needs a statement-indexed trampoline — NOT done).
**(2) ⛔⛔ THE MEASUREMENT TRAP, AND I FELL IN IT FIRST:** probed with LITERAL operands (`1 / 0`, `CHAR(1000)`) the oracle constant-folds and diagnoses at COMPILE time, so the handler never runs — which reads exactly like "SETEXIT is selective by error code". FALSE. Hide the operand in a variable and the same errors trap normally. Every `probe/setexit/` witness uses the variable form on purpose. **Do not re-derive an error-class table; there isn't one.**
**(3) THE CONTRACT IS THE ORACLE'S OWN CODE, `x64/sbl.min:29377-29420`:** ERRLIMIT zero ⇒ ABORT however armed · 3+ fatal ⇒ abort regardless of errlimit AND setexit · else decrement ERRLIMIT · no trap ⇒ the statement takes ITS OWN FAILURE EXIT · trap ⇒ clear it, reset the stored arg to null, jump. **SETEXIT IS ONE-SHOT** and **&ERRLIMIT IS A SEPARATE LOAD-BEARING HALF** — it decides *whether* an error is survivable, SETEXIT only *where* control goes. Bad argument ⇒ ERROR 187.
**(4) CURE = 3 EDITS, NO NEW MECHANISM, NO NEW GLOBAL:** `_SETEXIT_` returns the previous label · `core_runtime_error` grows a read side AFTER the untouched EVAL-boundary longjmp arm, reusing the EXISTING `rt_goto_transfer`/`rt_goto_resolve` indirect-goto road · `lower_snobol4.c` — SETEXIT joins CODE in `sno_scan_code_use`, because a program that transfers to a label at RUNTIME must have its labels registered as `LBL__` procs (without it the trap fired correctly and died at "transfer to undefined label" — right mechanism, empty table).
**(5) ⭐ `f12_load_unload` RE-STATED AND ITS REMAINING FAILURE IS NOT SETEXIT'S.** The SETEXIT half f12 needs WORKS (it arms with the STRING form `SETEXIT('OK')`; both `.L` and `'L'` covered, witnessed standalone). The blocker is RAISE: an UNLOADed call STILL RUNS AND RETURNS ITS VALUE (`MYFN('b')` answers `b` where the oracle raises 022) — seat7's s192 gap (3), whose obvious cure seat7 already excluded by measurement. f12 needs two more rungs, neither this row's.
**(6) ⛔ SECOND DEFECT, PRE-EXISTING AND NOT MINE, PROVEN WITH THIS CHANGE DISABLED:** a label whose statement READS A KEYWORD is never registered, so the EXISTING indirect-goto road silently loses it — `L="H"; :($L)` reaches `H  OUTPUT = "REACHED"` and dies on `H  OUTPUT = "REACHED " &LASTNO`. Look at `bb_label_landing()`. Own row; `se_trap_lastno` is checked in RED and will notice.
**(7) NEXT, NAMED:** RESUME rung (witnesses `se_errlimit_survives`/`se_oneshot`/`se_rearm`, all red) · RAISE rung (witnesses `se_trap_fires`/`se_reset_null`, red) · the `bb_label_landing` keyword-statement row · `g_core_err_stmt` never advances (seat8's, standing).

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s194 (seat8 `/home/claude8`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `subscript-silent-accept`, rank 2 — **ROW CLOSED, HQ'S WIDENED DONE-WHEN MET IN FULL**) — **THE SUBSCRIPT WAS ANSWERING SNOBOL4 WITH ICON'S STRING ARM, AND THE SILENT ACCEPT WAS THE MILD HALF OF IT.**
