Give the variable a value and it is a **SILENT WRONG ANSWER** — `A = 'hello'; X = A[2]` answered `e`, and `DATA('P(X,Y)'); Q = P(1,2); Q[1]` answered `1`, both where the oracle raises 235. A cure scoped to the null string would have passed the row's ORIGINAL DONE-WHEN and left that standing; HQ widened it to the manual's rule — **ARRAY or TABLE or 235** — and that is what shipped.
**CURE (HQ ruling (b), the s172 `bb_ab_slot_for` ONE-ALLOCATOR-TWO-FACES precedent): TWO FACES OVER ONE BODY, SELECTED AT LOWER.** `lower_snobol4.c` `sx_sub_container_only` stamps `IR_LIT(sub).sval = "container-only"` on every 2-operand `IR_SUBSCRIPT` it builds — **both** the TT_IDX rvalue arm and `sx_subscript_lv`, the assignment-target road, which is a different road and would otherwise have stayed permissive. `bb_subscript.cpp` reads the mark and swaps **the callee and nothing else** (same four registers, same `DT_FAIL` omega, same store, both arms, both media). `rt_subscript_var_container_only` is **six lines with no copy of the body**: deref, test `DT_A`-or-`DT_T`, `kwb_error(235,…)`, else hand the **ORIGINAL** base to `rt_subscript_var` — so the RTX veneer's array and table fast paths stay live and there is no second spelling to drift. Test is an **ALLOW-LIST**, so no future datatype slips through; the deref precedes it because a SNOBOL4 base arrives as a VARREF; `A[1,2]` passes because it lowers to TWO chained 2-operand subscripts whose second base derefs to the ROW ARRAY. Out-of-bounds still **FAILS** (p.90 idiom) — the gate is about the operand's TYPE only. Names carry no language; `.sc`/`.reb` inherit the rule because they inherit SPITBOL's, and `test_gate_emit_no_lang` is green.
⛔ **FOURTH-ROAD CENSUS (HQ demanded it before any cure) — ONLY ONE ROAD IS LIVE.** `subscript_get` via `bb_idx_get.cpp` is **DEAD**: `IR_IDX` is not in `IR.h`, `emit.cpp` has no dispatch for it, and its sole producer is `lower_snobol4.gz5-parked-41b53078.c` which the Makefile does not build. `rtx_icnsub.S` is **not a parallel road but DOWNSTREAM** of the new face — reached only after the container test passed, so its `.Lsub_string` and `DT_DATA` arms are unreachable from SNOBOL4 by construction. Corroborated by sweeping every checked-in `.s` outside `programs/lon`: `rt_subscript_var` 38 files, `subscript_get` **0**, `subscript_get2`+`rt_section_var` in 6 files that are **all `benchmarks/icon`**.
⭐ **RECEIPTS.** Two oracle-pinned probes **byte-identical to `sbl -bf` in BOTH modes**: `probe/subscript/sub_container_only.sno` (the **11-face census** — null string, string, integer, real, pattern, name, expression, code, data-object all 235; array and table accepted) and `probe/subscript/sub_shapes.sno` (the four witness shapes incl. the lvalue, plus the same three shapes on real arrays as positive control). ⭐ **BOTH PROVEN LIVE, NOT INERT** (the s193 `leafsib` lesson, same session it was written): under `SCRIP_SUB_AGG=0` the census diverges on EVERY line, and the old code did not even fail consistently — a REAL operand hit `Error 3`. Icon hard gate green: `probe/subscript/icn_string_subscript.icn` (string subscript, string-subscript ASSIGNMENT, list read/write, table read) both modes, smoke 14/14, semicolon + no-stack gates hold; its `.ref` is SCRIP-pinned and says so — this seat has `icont` but not `iconx`. Boards A/B by killswitch: **corpus m4 337/2 SKIP 1 IDENTICAL BY NAME**, crosscheck **319/1 · 318/1 · DIVERGE 0 IDENTICAL BY NAME**, m3 339/1 → 338/2. BOTH-MEDIUM ratchet 0/0. Step-4 regen all six, **attributed file-by-file by recompiling each mover under both arms: 25 movers are this rung** (every one an array/table/subscript program) and **21 are standing drift**; the entire delta in all 25 is `call rt_subscript_var@PLT` → `call rt_subscript_var_container_only@PLT`.
⛔ **THE SOLE RED THIS LEAVES IS `demo_treebank` m3 AND IT IS A DIFFERENT BUG — SEAT3 FOUND THE SAME ONE ONE SESSION EARLIER.** SPITBOL's `( e1, e2, …, en )` **Alternative Evaluation** (p.99) is first-that-succeeds; `treebank.sno` grows its array with `a = ARRAY('0:' (IDENT(a(x)) 0, size*2-1))`, so in SCRIP that statement fails once the list is non-empty, the array never grows, and every element subscript lands on a null base — which the OLD permissive subscript turned into a silent FAIL. `treebank.ref` is only `matched bytes=327`, so it went **GREEN while building an empty tree** (seat3 s193 `treebank-allocating`, verbatim: *"the .ref is true of a program that does no work"*). ⛔ **ALL FOUR ARMS MEASURED ON THIS TREE — IT CANNOT BE GREEN UNTIL BOTH RUNGS LAND:** default `Error 235` (honest) · `SCRIP_VLIST_ALT=1` **SIGSEGV** · `SCRIP_SUB_AGG=0` the false green · both switches **SIGSEGV**. Its m4 arm was ALREADY red in BOTH arms. This rung did not break treebank, it stopped treebank lying; the repair is seat3's asked row `vlist-alt-zeta-depth`. Red probe checked in with a live-oracle ref at `probe/vlist/vlist_expr_alternation.sno`.
⛔ **ADJACENT, NAMED, NOT FIXED:** `g_core_err_stmt` NEVER ADVANCES — every runtime error in this tree reports `in statement 0`, 235 and pre-existing 164 alike, where the oracle names the statement. And `DATATYPE` of a program-defined object answers upper-case (`LIST` vs the oracle's `list`). Both want rows.
**NEXT:** `vlist-alt-zeta-depth` (seat3's, the treebank unblocker) · a row for `g_core_err_stmt`.
See `FINDING-2026-08-20-s194-the-subscript-was-answering-snobol4-with-icons-string-arm.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s194 (seat5 `/home/claude5`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `semantic-driver-m4-segv`, rank 1) — **⭐⭐⭐ DOD ITEM 2 IS CLOSED: `beauty_suite` 17/17 IN BOTH MODES. THE `alpha$<FN>` CELL WAS SEALED ONLY IN THE COMPILER PROCESS, SO *EVERY* BY-NAME CALL TO A DEFINE'd PROC IN m4 ENTERED THROUGH A THUNK ITS OWN COMMENT NAMES AS THE WRONG PROTOCOL.**
**LANDED: SCRIP `f722e70e` · corpus `d16055da`.** `make pristine` EXIT=0 before every verdict (control, patched, and again after the rebase), RT_OPT `-O0`. Gates: `template_medium_invisible` **0** (ceiling 0) · `emit_no_lang` · **`em_beauty_subsystems_mode4` PASS=17 FAIL=0** — it read PASS=16 FAIL=1 on `semantic_driver` for five sessions.
**⛔ THE BRIEF'S "ZERO OUTPUT" WAS A LOST STDIO BUFFER, AND THAT ONE FACT WAS THE WHOLE SEARCH.** Redirected stdout is block-buffered and a SIGSEGV discards it, so the crash *looked* like a startup death. Under `stdbuf -o0` the binary prints **PASS 1/2/3** and dies in test **4** — the first statement that MATCHES the pattern rather than taking its `DATATYPE`. ⭐ **Never localise a SIGSEGV from redirected stdout.**
**⭐ THE DEFECT, IN ONE SENTENCE:** `bb_ab_seal_entry_cells` resolves `alpha$<FN>` through the **live emitter label pool** and is called only from the driver — both compiler-process-only — so a linked m4 binary never inherits the seal (**`grep -c 'alpha$' <any .s>` = 0**, the whole diagnosis in one command), `rt_dyn_alpha_fn` rejects the stub and falls back to `p->fn`, the generic label-chain entry its own comment calls the **"rip=5 crash class"**. gdb: `rt_call_proc_descr("Zero",0)` → **rip=0x1**. m3 is not more correct — **it is the same process**.
**⭐ ONLY ZERO-ARG DEFERRED CALLS REACH THAT ROAD.** `. *F(x)` is lifted to an `EXPR$` thunk (target string `*EXPR$0`); `. *F()` emits the bare name and `rt_dcap_pump` calls it BY NAME. Six sibling witnesses share one preamble and differ only in the pattern line, so the pair `arity0`/`arity1` isolates it to that one ingredient.
**⛔ NOT A CORNER — THE MANUAL'S OWN SHOWCASE.** SPITBOL v3.7 **p.136–137**: `'ABCDE' ? LEN(2) . *PUSH() 'D' LEN(1) . *PUSH()`, the published `NRETURN` + deferred-evaluation idiom, a ZERO-ARG call as the target of `.`. `sbl -bf` and m3 both give `n=2 [1]=BC [2]=E`; m4 SIGSEGV'd. It is also the exact `counter.sno`/`semantic.sno` shape beauty's grammar is built from. **The oracle agrees with m3 on every witness**, so "match m3" and "match sbl" were one target.
**⭐ THE CURE USED TWO AUTHORITIES THAT ALREADY EXISTED, AND INVENTED NEITHER.** `bb_ab_seal_alpha()` is the SAME cell store reached with the address already resolved; the DEFINE bind site calls it with the assembler-resolved `<FN>_α`. Guards: `!bb_ab_cell_addr(fname)` — **ASK THE ALLOCATOR, NOT THE MEDIUM** (`bb_define:406/:474` precedent) which is why **m3 does not move a byte** and no `MEDIUM_*` token was added; and `bb_tiny_shim_ok()`, the declared ONE AUTHORITY on whether `<FN>_α` exists (*"a site may jmp `<fn>_alpha` iff this returns 1"*). **No new global.** ⛔ Guessing that predicate is not a wrong answer but an **undefined symbol at link** — I hit it once via a UTF-8 double-encoding of the α, and it failed loudly at `ld`, the right direction.
**MEASURED — CONTROL BUILT, NOT QUOTED** (the corpus had grown 337→340 rows since s193, so the recorded watermark was not a legal comparison): control **m3 338/2 · m4 331/8 SKIP 1**, beauty_suite m4 16/17 → patched **m3 338/2 · m4 336/3 SKIP 1**, beauty_suite m4 **17/17**. **m4 +5, m3 bit-for-bit unmoved, zero new reds, fail-set a strict subset.** CURED BY NAME: `semantic_driver` · `expr_eval` · `140_pat_eval_double_fn_trick` · `141_pat_eval_double_fn_arbno` · **`demo_claws5`** — ⭐⭐ seat3's s190 row, the class the brief told me to dedupe against rather than re-derive; same defect, same line.
**KILLSWITCH-CLEAN, MEASURED:** control vs `SCRIP_M4_ALPHA_SEAL=0` over 145 programs' `--compile` md5 = **IDENTICAL, 0 movers**. Blast radius at default: **50 of 145** `.s` (the DEFINE-bearing set). Regen ladder all six EXIT 0; ⭐ the **31 crosscheck `.s` were attributed file-by-file** under the killswitch arm — **27 mine · 1 standing drift (seat7's s192 `1021_code_direct_goto`) · 3 bomb stubs.**
**WATERMARK (merged tree, pristine, `-O0`): corpus m3 339/1 · m4 337/2 · SKIP 1 · beauty_suite 17/17 both modes.** ⛔ **THE SURPLUS OVER MY +5 IS NOT MINE** — `145_pat_left_assoc_via_arbno_fence` went green with other seats' commits. Witness set `corpus/probe/m4alpha/` is a LIVE instrument: **m3 7/7 · m4 default 7/7 · m4 under `=0` 5/7** (checked deliberately after s193's inert-`leafsib` finding).
**⭐ GENERALISABLE — THE ALLOWLIST LAW, AND THIS TIME IT WAS A CELL.** `scrip.c` warns twice that *"the startup replay is an ALLOWLIST, not a snapshot, so m3 passing proves nothing about m4."* `alpha$<FN>` had no m4 twin and hid longer for a structural reason: **it is not in the startup ladder's reach at all** — line 668 skips every `dyn_scope` proc, whose registration lives at the DEFINE site. **Auditing m3→m4 fact parity, the ladder is only half the surface; the DEFINE site is the other half.** And: **a cell whose only writer resolves through the emitter's label pool cannot exist in a linked image.**
**NEXT / NOT TAKEN:** (a) the **arity asymmetry stands** — the by-name road is now correct, so this is no longer correctness, but two roads for one construct is the shape Lon's NO-PER-OP-FILTER ruling is about; collapsing them is its own rung. (b) `rt_call_named_proc` (`rt.c:1993`) carries the same sealed-alpha arm behind `SCRIP_BYNAME_ALPHA` — the seal makes it live in m4 too, **named not measured**. (c) `demo_treebank` m4 red, pre-existing, in the control set.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s194-the-alpha-cell-is-sealed-only-in-the-compiler-process-so-every-m4-by-name-call-lands-wild.md`
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s194 (seat2 `/home/claude2`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `csnobol4-pin-provenance`, rank 0 — **ROW CLOSED, DONE-WHEN MET IN FULL, QUESTION ASKED NOT DECIDED**) — **THE CENSUS REPRODUCES. THE "NOT OURS" BUCKET DOES NOT — IT HAS REAL SCRIP DEFECTS IN IT, AND ONE OF THEM IS A MISSING `-INCLUDE` TRIM.**
**MEASURED at SCRIP `c43d8f51` · corpus `fea93a27`, both trees CLEAN, RT_OPT `-O0`. ⛔ ZERO FILES CHANGED in SCRIP or corpus** — the suite was copied to scratchpad first, because several programs do file I/O and an in-place census writes into the corpus tree. **NO compiler change, no `.s` regen (N/A — nothing built), no new global, no killswitch.**
**⭐ THE STRUCTURAL FACT IS REAL AND IT REPRODUCES TWICE.** Dialect-21 arrived at **independently from the source** (lowercase special name as an assignment target) = **exactly 21**, HQ's own number. Non-dialect board reds **56** (HQ 62) · pin reproducible by live SPITBOL **9** (HQ 10) · not reproducible **47** (HQ 52). The deltas are **cures landed since s191** — `collect2`, HQ's own residue-six item (a), is now `PASS/PASS`. **Provenance verdict IDENTICAL 124/124 across two sequential runs**; the only board mover is `nqueens` m4, the one program seat5's row says to distrust on a single draw, and it flapped on an idle box. The 12 `md5_live` movers are **every one on a DEAD or rc≠0 arm** — crash-dump text, the s189 fact restated, no LIVE arm moved.
**⛔ THE BRIEF IS WRONG ON TWO OF ITS FOUR FUNCTIONS, AND THE MANUAL AND THE ORACLE DISAGREE ABOUT A THIRD.** `REWIND` is **NOT** a CSNOBOL4-only builtin: live `sbl -bf` answers **`ERROR 174 -- rewind file does not exist`**, its own documented 172–176 family (v3.7 p.127/137/237, and it sits in the manual's **Built-in functions** table). `SET` is in that same table but the live `sbl v4.0f` raises **`ERROR 022`** — **documented-but-unimplemented, a third category neither the brief nor the manual alone can express.** Only `ORD` and `BREAKPOINT` are genuinely not SPITBOL functions (**0** manual hits each). Controls `CHAR`→`A`, `SUBSTR`→`hel`, so the probe is sound. **⛔ AND THE CALL CENSUS IS 5 PROGRAMS OF 124, NOT THE 52-CLASS** — `collect2.sno:5` and `genc.sno:1322` are **COMMENT lines** (`*` col 1). residue-six (a) reads *"collect2 — it calls ORD"*; **it does not, and collect2 is green.**
**⭐⭐⭐ THE REAL FIND — CUT ON "DID SCRIP PRODUCE OUTPUT" BEFORE "WHOSE PIN IS IT".** **39 of the 56 reds never reached the comparison** (parse error / compile fail / crash / timeout), so **no `.ref` from any oracle can explain them**. Cross-cut with *does live SPITBOL run it*: **17** are SCRIP failing structurally on programs SPITBOL RUNS, of which **8 are airtight** (real non-empty oracle output) — `breakx comment include intval lexcmp line nqueens pow`. **THREE OF THE EIGHT — `include`, `line`, `pow` — SIT INSIDE HQ's "52 NOT OURS".** `include`/`line` are **ROOT-CAUSED**: SCRIP emits `cannot open include 'line2.sno '` — **trailing space inside the quotes** — and `-INCLUDE "line2.sno "` at `include.sno:4`/`line.sno:8` is a **deliberate test that the filename is right-trimmed**. SPITBOL trims it and runs; SCRIP refuses to parse. ⛔ **NOT the same defect as `include2/3/4`**, which name `aa.sno`/`bb.sno` that do not exist in the suite — SPITBOL fails those too, and SCRIP is right to refuse them.
**⭐⭐ TWO CLASSES NO ROW NAMES.** (a) **11 pins contain the LITERAL PLACEHOLDER `xxx`** (`trace1.ref`: `time = xxx`) — CSNOBOL4 **harness-NORMALIZED** output, reproducible by **no engine, CSNOBOL4 2.3.3 included** (it prints `time = 0.`); all 11 board-red, **unpassable by construction**. (b) The inventory is not 124↔125: **3 orphan `.ref`** (`callgraph`, `proc.h`, `static.h`) + **2 programs with NO pin** (`bench`, `line2`), both with a dead oracle, so neither can ever pass.
**⛔ THE FACT THAT DECIDES WHAT DISPOSITION (a) IS WORTH: `run_one`'s `grade()` ALREADY FALLS BACK TO THE LIVE ORACLE** (`pin` OR `live`). A CSNOBOL4 pin therefore **cannot by itself make a program red** — every one of the 47 is red because SCRIP *also* fails against live SPITBOL, or because there is no live SPITBOL at all. **Re-pinning the 52 from live SPITBOL is very nearly a NO-OP for the score.** The harness has emitted a per-program `pin!=live` note all along and nothing reads it.
**QUESTION ASKED (`q-csnobol4-pin-provenance`), NOT DECIDED — and the brief's (a)/(b)/(c) is under-specified: the suite is FIVE populations.** [1] the 8 airtight SCRIP defects are not a disposition question at all (**recommend a row**); [2] the 9 non-dialect placeholder pins are reachable by no disposition short of porting the normalizer; [3] the 30 with no live SPITBOL door are the true out-of-dialect class (seat5's gimpel precedent fits); [4] only **9** programs have both engines running with differing output — the ONLY population where re-pinning moves a verdict; [5] the 19 red dialect programs stay with `kw-uppercase-dialect`. **⛔ AND THE SCORE QUESTION UNDER IT: `csnobol4_suite` carries 5 META weight, yet 9 pins are unmatchable by any engine and 2 programs have no pin — the suite's CEILING IS BELOW 124/124 no matter how good SCRIP gets, and that ceiling has never been stated.**
**⭐ GENERALISABLE: "whose oracle produced the pin" is the SECOND question; the first is "did the engine under test produce output at all". A provenance census that skips that cut files every compile bomb under "wrong oracle" — and it fails in the FLATTERING direction, because the bucket labelled *not our fault* is the one nobody re-audits.** Second: **a pin containing a literal placeholder is not a pin** — grep every `.ref` for normalization artefacts before trusting a suite's score. Third: **when a brief hands you a list of functions "the oracle does not have", CALL THEM** — two of these four were in the manual's own table and one answered from its own documented error family.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s194-the-csnobol4-pin-census-reproduces-and-the-not-ours-bucket-is-hiding-real-scrip-defects.md`

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s194 (seat1 `/home/claude1`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `indirect-nonname-silent-accept`, rank 1) — **THE BRIEF NAMED THE NULL STRING; THE NULL STRING WAS THE CHEAP HALF. `$` ASKED THE *OUTPUT STRINGIFIER* WHETHER ITS OPERAND WAS A NAME, SO EVERY AGGREGATE WAS SILENTLY ACCEPTED — `$ARRAY(3) = 'x'` CREATED A VARIABLE CALLED `ARRAY('3')` AND RAN ON.**
**LANDED: SCRIP `7153ccd7` · corpus `c26738c1`.** Manual v3.7 **p.83** (*"provided the null string is never encountered as a variable name"*, worked example → 239) and **p.196** (*"$(.A) is the same as using the variable A"* — a NAME converts not at all). ORACLE-MEASURED boundary (`sbl -bf`, `&ERRLIMIT` converting so the oracle prints diffable lines, one credit per refusal): **ACCEPT** = non-null STRING incl. punctuation `$'='`, INTEGER, REAL, NAME · **REFUSE 239** = null string, ARRAY, TABLE, PATTERN, CODE, EXPRESSION, DATA object.
**⭐ ONE AUTHORITY, TWO FACES, BECAUSE `$` IS SPELLED TWICE:** `core.c rt_sno_indirect_name` decides; `bn_sno_name` serves the value AND assignment positions (both lower to `CALL "SNO$NAME"`, `--dump-ir` verified) and `rt_goto_resolve`'s `$` arm serves the computed goto, which had its own hand-written message. `VARVAL_d_fn` classifies, so the refusal set is not spelled twice. Errors ride `kwb_error` (the OPSYN precedent), so the default `&ERRLIMIT` arm reports and terminates exactly as SPITBOL does.
**⛔ THE NULL TEST READS THE DESCRIPTOR, NEVER THE C STRING, AND BOTH WRONG CUTS WERE MEASURED FIRST.** (1) `&ALPHABET` is 256 bytes whose FIRST byte is NUL and the oracle **accepts** `$&ALPHABET` — a `!*sv` test raises a FALSE 239 there. (2) `STRVAL()` sets `slen = 0` and this runtime reads `slen==0` on a `DT_S` as *"ask strlen"*, not *"null"* — so a naive `slen == 0` test refused every INTEGER and REAL. Two conventions one line apart, each one produced a wrong cut before the right one.
**⛔⭐ THE FIRST CUT ALSO DEREFERENCED A NAMETRAP, AND ONE PROGRAM IN 340 CAUGHT IT:** `expr_eval` regressed m3 — its `Push = .stk[stk[0]]` / `$Push = x` is indirection through a NAME, and derefing `DT_N slen==2` turned a pre-existing SILENT WRONG ANSWER into a false 239. **`$` THROUGH A NAMETRAP LOSES THE TRAP AND IS STILL LIVE** (oracle `stk1=hello stk2=world`, SCRIP `stk1= stk2=`): `bn_sno_name` always rebuilds a string-keyed NAME, so a trapped name is dereferenced and the VALUE is named instead — double indirection where p.196 says the operand IS the variable. Cure looks like two lines (`if (v.v == DT_N) { *out = v; return 1; }`); **NOT taken here, it is a separate row** — the SNOBOL4 stack-push idiom is the witness.
**⛔ TWO MORE NAMED, NOT DONE:** ERROR **38** (*Goto undefined label*) was conflated with 239 in one printf — oracle answers 38 for a bad label and 239 for a null one; the 239 half is split out and 38 keeps its legacy path. And the goto face **cannot take `:F`** (GOTO_DEFERRED has no ω edge), so under a non-zero `&ERRLIMIT` it reports and exits where the oracle branches — loud, not silent.
**⭐ WITNESSES `corpus/probe/indirect/`, all three `.ref` minted LIVE through `util_ref_mint.sh` → the board's own oracle door.** `indirect_name_boundary` + `indirect_name_datatypes` **green in both modes and RED under `SCRIP_IND_NAME=0`** — they discriminate the arms, which is the s193 test for whether a witness is evidence at all. `indirect_nul_prefix_name` is **RED ON PURPOSE**: SPITBOL accepts a NUL-first name, SCRIP's namespace is C-string keyed and cannot spell one, and **no `&ERRLIMIT` credit is spent** (`before=50 … after=50`) — which is how the file proves NAMESPACE LIMIT rather than false 239. ⛔ No CODE-object line: SCRIP's `CODE()` answers `DATATYPE` **STRING** (measured), so that line would have passed for the wrong reason and was removed rather than banked.
**MEASURED pristine (RT_OPT `-O0`), RE-PROVED AFTER REBASING onto seats 3/6/7: board m3 339/1 · m4 337/2 SKIP 1, fail-set IDENTICAL BY NAME to the `SCRIP_IND_NAME=0` control** (the same equality held pre-rebase at 338/2 · 331/8; the jump is their VLIST-ALT/DCAP-FRETURN, not this rung). **beauty AB-IDENTICAL 9/9** one-line inputs — the `$'$'` idiom at beauty.sno:104 provably untouched. Gates `emit_no_lang` OK · BOTH-MEDIUM ratchet **0**. Six RULES step-4 regens: five `changed=0`; crosscheck regenerated 4 `.s` **all byte-identical across the killswitch = STANDING DRIFT from seat7's s192 direct-goto**, and the rebase dropped that commit as "already upstream", confirming it independently. No new global, no template, no codegen.
**⭐ GENERALISABLE: a stringifier is not a classifier.** Asking the OUTPUT-side renderer *"is this a name?"* silently converts *"I can display this"* into *"this is legal"* — and it answers NON-EMPTY for exactly the datatypes that must be refused.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s194-the-dollar-operator-asked-a-display-function-whether-its-operand-was-a-name.md`

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s193 (seat3 `/home/claude3`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `treebank-allocating`, rank 26) — **THE ALLOCATING TREEBANK MATCHED AND BUILT NOTHING, ITS `.ref` PINNED THAT, AND SPITBOL'S ALTERNATIVE EVALUATION `( e1, e2, …, en )` WAS NEVER IMPLEMENTED.**
**WATERMARK: NOT CLAIMED AND SAYING SO IS THE POINT — THE COMPILER CHANGE SHIPS DEFAULT OFF, so the emitted tree is byte-identical to `c512089a` BY CONSTRUCTION (the default arm is the same one-line `return` it replaced) and RULES step-4 regen is N/A. Board evidence is per-row through `scorecard_snobol4.sh one`, not a re-measured watermark I did not earn.** SCRIP `c512089a` + this rung, corpus `f7ce61c3` + this rung, RT_OPT `-O0`, oracle live `sbl -bf`.
**⛔⛔ THE ROOT CAUSE IS NOT THE PATTERN ENGINE AND THE WITNESS HAS NO PATTERN MATCHING IN IT.** `lower_snobol4.c:705` lowered `TT_VLIST` as `return sx_lower(cx, t->c[0], …)` — the parser builds every element and **LOWER drops `e2..en` on the floor**. Correct exactly when `e1` succeeds; **silently fails the whole statement** the moment `e1` fails. `x = ARRAY('0:3'); OUTPUT = 'a[' (IDENT(x) 0, 7) ']'` → oracle `a[7]`, SCRIP **prints nothing**. Manual v3.7 **p.99 "Alternative Evaluation"** + Appendix note 3, and characterised against the live oracle rather than read off the page: `(S1(),F1())`→`S` with F1 **never called**; `(F1(),S1())`→`S`, F1 once; `(F1(),F1(),S1())`→`S`, F1 twice.
**⛔ ONE LINE OF `treebank.sno` IS WHY IT REACHES THIS ROW.** `ListInsert4  a = ARRAY('0:' (IDENT(a(x)) 0, size * 2 - 1))` — `IDENT(a(x))` succeeds only on the FIRST insert, so **every growth after the first** fails, `a` stays null and `ListInsert8` assigns that **null string** to `a(x)`. Measured with no pattern matching: after append B the oracle has `ARRAY 0:1`, SCRIP has **`STRING`**.
**⛔⭐⭐ THE `.ref` WAS TRUE OF A PROGRAM THAT DOES NO WORK.** `treebank.ref` is `matched bytes=327` — a property of the **input**. I added a re-serialiser: oracle `(BANK (ROOT (S (NP (DT The) (NN cat)) (VP (VBZ sits)))) …)`, **SCRIP m3 `tree=` EMPTY**. The pin cannot tell those apart. *Non-empty is not alive*, checked in as a reference file.
**⛔ THE BRIEF WAS WRONG ON ARRIVAL IN BOTH HALVES.** (a) The oracle was **never dead**: `ERROR 217 duplicate label ×5` is the **s189 case-folding phantom** — `treebank.sno` holds exactly five case-differing label pairs (`Init_list`/`init_list` …), five pairs, five errors, and the five lines named are the lowercase halves. Under the ruled arm `sbl -bf` it runs green. (b) m3's `Error 5` is downstream of the VLIST defect (`bank` never assigned ⇒ `a(bank)` dies).
**⭐ THE ROW'S DELIVERABLE, GREEN IN ALL THREE ENGINES: `corpus/programs/snobol4/demo/treebank-alloc.{sno,ref}`** — same grammar/tape/input as `treebank-match`, action routines that allocate an ARRAY per node into a TABLE, on the NON-CAPTURE `*fn()` + immediate `$` road (p.87). **Identity gate = an ALLOCATION-SEQUENCE CHECKSUM, not `matched bytes`** — a run that matches while allocating nothing cannot pass it. `.ref` minted through `scorecard_snobol4.sh oracle` (the door the board grades through), **LIVE**, read before commit; **PASS/PASS on the board**. ⛔ It is deliberately NOT the treebank parse and the reason is MEASURED: rewriting the actions to immediate ones makes the **oracle itself** build `(ROOT (S) (NP) (DT) The …)` — ARBNO tries its EMPTY match first, so the trailing `*pop_list()` fires on every abandoned length. **The Ch.9 p.136 action-routine ploy genuinely requires conditional assignment.**
**⭐⭐ AND THE TIMED ROW INVERTS THE NON-ALLOCATING RESULT** (`bench_pt0_3way.sh --progs treebank-alloc --reps 40 --samples 7`, **RT_OPT `-O0`**, ratios only per LAW 2): `treebank-alloc` **m3/sbl 0.53× · m4/sbl 0.47×**, against s168's `treebank-match` 1.14×/1.19×. **SCRIP is ~2× FASTER once the match allocates, having been ~1.2× slower when it did not.**
**⭐ THE GC SPLIT — H1 FALSIFIED FOR ALLOCATING MATCHING TOO, AT THE SHIPPED DEFAULT.** Storage regenerations, ONE pass over the 100 KB input, `SCRIP_ZETA_TELEM=1`: at the shipped **512 MB** `ZC_HEAP_MB` default the allocating row does **0** — and so does the 40-rep bench tape — so its **GC share is 0 %**, exactly as s168 measured for the non-allocating half. **But it is the first member of this family that CAN drive the collector at all:** at an 8 MB arena `treebank-alloc` does **6 372** regenerations where `treebank-match` still does **0**. So the workload difference is real and now measurable, and H1 (*"the GC tax follows the match"*) is falsified for allocating matching too — not because it does not allocate, but because the arena never forces a collection.
**⛔⭐⭐ THE COMPILER FIX SHIPS **DEFAULT OFF** (`SCRIP_VLIST_ALT=1` opts in), the `SCRIP_B1C_LAND` precedent, AND THE ARMED ARM IS NOT A WORKING FEATURE — IT IS HALF ONE.** The lowering is provably right (element *i*'s **ω** IS element *i+1*'s **α**; the last element's ω is the caller's, so *"all fail ⇒ the list fails"* falls out of the wiring, not a test) and a **bare two-element** list works armed. Everything else breaks, and the asm says why: the ζ-spine addresses operands at a **static RSP depth** and the retreat contract reads every ω as *"leave the statement"* — `n24_var_β: add rsp,16 / add rsp,16 / jmp n29_lit_integer_α` unwinds the **enclosing expression's live cells** first, and the out-of-line element is planned at a depth that fits no frame (writes `[rsp+320]` in an 80-byte frame). Nested ⇒ `7]` for `A[7]`; three-element or all-fail bare ⇒ **SIGSEGV**. **This is the s192 `zd_plan` family: an arc landing INSIDE the statement read as a statement exit.**
**⛔ RE-PROVED AFTER THE REBASE AND IT WAS NOT A FORMALITY** (seat6's `af1b9d64` and seat4's `99cd7ede` both landed on the same road mid-measurement): `make pristine` at merged SCRIP `40a5b01a`, driver + `.so` from ONE build, zero `-O1`/`-O2` — `treebank-alloc` **PASS/PASS**, `vl_alt_second` red default / **green armed**, `vl_alt_nested_cat` red both arms, all four gates green.
**⛔ AND THE INERTNESS IS PROVEN, NOT ASSERTED: A/B ON ONE TREE, 400 PROGRAMS** (demo + crosscheck + probe; `programs/lon/` excluded BY CONSTRUCTION), same tree with and without the patch, `--compile` md5 both arms: **0 movers / 400**. Gates green: `emit_no_lang` · `template_medium_invisible` · `icn_no_stack` · `icn_one_reg_frame`.
**⭐ ALSO LANDED: `scripts/bench_pt0_3way.sh` `-b` → `-bf`** (one of the 19 unconverted sites RULES.md names) + `treebank-alloc` in `input_of`. ⛔ **MEASURED INERT for the two rows that predate it:** `treebank-match` and `treebank-match-fence` are **byte-identical** under `-b` and `-bf` on `VBGinTASA.dat`, so s168's baseline is not invalidated.
**⭐ WITNESSES, `.ref`s ALL MINTED LIVE AND READ: `corpus/probe/vlist/`** — `vl_alt_first_ok` (**green**: the `e1`-succeeds half that always worked, so the reds cannot be misread as "the construct is unparsed") · `vl_alt_second` (**red, GREEN ARMED** — the lowering half) · `vl_alt_nested_cat` (**red, and RED ARMED** — the ζ half). **Two intended reds, declared.**
**⭐ THE GENERALISABLE MOVE: a reference file is one half of a disagreement, and a program that prints a property of its INPUT has pinned nothing about its OUTPUT.** `matched bytes=327` is true of the correct parse, of an empty parse, and of a program that only measures the string it was handed. One added line that printed what the program *built* found a defect that had sat under a green pin since check-in. Same family as s191's *"a lookup that prints is not a lookup that checks"*.
**NEXT / ROWS ASKED (HQ notified via the question box, twice, with receipts):** (1) **`vlist-alt-zeta-depth`** — arm the switch by teaching the release planner this arc; `vl_alt_nested_cat` is the ready-made witness, `vl_alt_second` the green control. (2) ⭐ **`dcap-alpha-cell-m4` IS ALREADY DONE — seat4 landed it THIS SESSION as `apply-snodef-m4` (SCRIP `99cd7ede`), and my rebase is how I found out. RE-MEASURED on the merged pristine tree: the faithful capture-target twin's m4 SIGSEGV is GONE and m3 ≡ m4 (both build the empty tree, i.e. the VLIST defect), so the ONLY thing left between a faithful allocating treebank and green-both-modes is `vlist-alt-zeta-depth`.** ⛔ Residual recorded not chased: `treebank.sno` (EVAL form) m4 moved `Error 22` → `Error 5` where m3 prints `matched bytes=327`. The original s190 mechanism, for the record, is why the twin died in m4 (`rt_dcap_pump` → `rt_call_proc_descr` → an `alpha$<FN>` cell only the compiler process fills; `rip=0xb`); it also owns `claws5.sno` m4 (D-2). Checked not guessed: `push_list_α`/`pop_list_α` ARE symbols in the emitted `.s` and `.Lstartup_prec<N>` + `rt_proc_register_rec` already exists to hang the seal on. (3) **`treebank-ref-is-blind`** — that pin cannot fail; it should assert the tree, not the input size. ⛔ Also recorded: the `.`-capture treebank overflows the ORACLE's stack (`ERROR 246`) on the 100 KB bench input with OR without EVAL, so it could never have been the timed allocating row.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s193-the-allocating-treebank-matched-and-built-nothing-and-spitbols-alternative-evaluation-was-never-implemented.md`.
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s193 (seat6 `/home/claude6`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `dcap-freturn-false-accept`, rank 0) — **BEAUTY'S TOKEN-CLASSIFICATION WALL IS DOWN. THE ARM THE ROW POINTED AT WAS DEAD CODE, THE LIVE ROAD HAD NO FAILURE CHANNEL AT ALL, AND THE CRASH IS NOT DOWNSTREAM OF THE MIS-PARSE.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `af1b9d64`, after `make pristine`, RT_OPT `-O0`; live `x64/bin/sbl -bf` verified alive first): corpus m3 **335/2** · m4 **328/8** · SKIP 1 (337) AT BOTH ARMS, fail-set identical BY NAME — this cure is corpus-neutral.** 4 gates green. Step-4 regens moved exactly ONE artifact tree-wide: `test/snobol4/capture/059_capture_dollar_deferred.s` (+3/−1).
**⭐ THE CURE.** `SCRIP_CAP_FAIL_RETREAT`, **DEFAULT ON**, `=0` reverts VERBATIM. Runtime half (`pattern_match.c`): `c_rt_cap_open`'s `*` arm returns a **-1 sentinel** when the target FAILED instead of falling into the shared `return 0`. Emitter half (`bb_match_capture.cpp`, **both** IMM arms — no per-op filter): `cmp rax,-1 ; je L(4)` **before** the transfer test, and an `L(4)` landing that leaves the alignment anchor exactly once then `x86_omega()`. Both halves read one env name (s121 law).
**⛔⛔ THE ROW'S FINDING NAMED THE WRONG SITE AND MEASURING IT FIRST IS WHAT SAVED THE SESSION.** It located the defect at `rt_dcap_pump`/`MATCH_END` ("drained after the match already succeeded"). At this HEAD **the witness never records a dcap entry at all** — `SCRIP_DCAP_TRACE` prints **`end_ok n=0`**, no `[DCAP] WARN` is emitted, `IS_FAIL_fn` is never evaluated. `$ *chk()` is the **IMMEDIATE** road: the asm `rt_cap_open` delegates every `'*'` target to `c_rt_cap_open`, which calls the proc ITSELF, skips the assignment on failure, and returns **`0` == "fully handled"** — the same 0 it returns when the target ASSIGNED. That shared return is the bug, and it is why **no existing killswitch moved the witness**: `CAP_NAME_STRICT` is DEFAULT ON since s178 and its failure edge is wired into `IR_MATCH_END` for the **COND** road; this element is IMM.
**⭐⭐ A SECOND DEFECT ON THE SAME ROAD, READ OFF THE EMITTED `.s` (ASM-DIFF-FIRST, no gdb), FIXED TOO:** the transfer's **ω** landing (target FAILED) ran its epilogue, called `rt_cap_finish`, and then **FELL THROUGH into the box's common continuation** — the very label the **γ** arm jumps to. **Four ports, three wires**, with a real fail port (`n36_match_assign_imm_β`) sitting one line below that ω never reached. That arm is **DEAD on this witness** (the box takes `je L(1)` first), which is why fixing only it changed nothing — the two defects had to be found separately.
**⛔ THE HAZARD THE BRIEF NAMED AT MATCH_END EXISTS AT `rt_cap_open` TOO:** `test rax,rax` treats every non-zero value as a **TRANSFER ADDRESS** to `jmp rax`, and every positive value is a legitimate `fbytes` frame size — so "just propagate rc" jumps to address 1 from this side as well. Hence the **-1 sentinel**, tested BEFORE the transfer test.
**⭐⭐⭐ THE ORACLE RULED THE CURE'S SHAPE AND THE OBVIOUS CURE WAS WRONG.** Reusing s170's `IR_MATCH_END` ω would **abort** the statement. Live `sbl -bf`: `(SPAN('ABC') $ *chk('NO') | 'ABC')` prints one `CHK` and then **MATCHES** — a **FAILING** target **RETREATS** and the next alternative runs; `&FULLSCAN=1` gives `nomatch` with exactly one call. So a failing target **concedes** on the box's own ω wire (where `x86_beta_trampoline` already routes β) while a **not-a-name** target still **aborts** through MATCH_END. **Two verdicts on one road — do not merge them.** SCRIP is oracle-identical on all three probes.
**⭐⭐ BEAUTY, THE DONE-WHEN:** on `L       X = 1` with the FINDING's own `xTrace = 5` ablation, `X` is now **`Push(Id)`** and `1` is **`Push(Integer)`** — **ORACLE-IDENTICAL**, where `=0` calls both `Push(BuiltinVar)`. Beauty reaches `Shift(Integer, 1)`.
**⛔⛔ AND THE ROW'S PREMISE IS HALF FALSIFIED — THE NEXT BEAUTY SEAT NEEDS THIS.** The row states *"Every downstream symptom incl. the wild jump is a consequence of the mis-parse."* Measured: beauty **SIGSEGVs at rc=139 after exactly 18 trace lines in BOTH arms** (same depth, same count), and **`board_beauty_m1.sh --modes m3` is IDENTICAL across arms — 5/10, first red at rung 40, per-rung diff empty.** The mis-parse and the crash are **TWO INDEPENDENT WALLS**; curing the first does not move the ladder. **Do not hunt the SEGV downstream of token classification.**
**RECEIPTS:** blast radius **128 / 1648 `.sno`** with a **1-row noise floor**, every mover a `$`-capture carrier. **Killswitch verbatim-revert PROVEN, not asserted:** the patch was set aside, HEAD rebuilt as a real control, and swept — `=0` vs unpatched control = **0 movers**, sole row `unary_not.sno` (emits uninitialised memory into `.rodata`, differs every compile in every arm; reported s191).
**NEXT RUNG:** the SEGV wall at prefix-rung 40 — now the sole beauty blocker on this ladder, arm-independent of every capture switch, and wants its own row minted from the 18-line trace ceiling rather than from the classification symptom.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s193-the-failing-capture-target-arm-was-dead-code-and-the-live-road-had-no-failure-channel.md`
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s193 (seat7 `/home/claude7`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `unload-missing`, rank 1) — **UNLOAD WAS ONE OF *THREE* GAPS BETWEEN f12 AND A PASS, AND THE BRIEF NAMED ONLY THE FIRST. SETEXIT IS A WRITE-ONLY STUB.**
**LANDED: SCRIP `e7c783d1`, corpus probes, FINDING pushed.** RT_OPT `-O0`. No new global, no new killswitch, no template touched. Fail-sets **identical BY NAME**: crosscheck m3 319/1 · m4 315/4 · DIVERGE 3; broad corpus m3 339/1 · m4 332/7 SKIP 1.
**⭐ NINE ARMS, ALL MEASURED ON THE LIVE ORACLE, NONE CHOSEN** (the brief forbade guessing, and two arms would certainly have been guessed wrong): user-defined name → succeeds returning the **NULL STRING**; ⭐ **the same name's VARIABLE VALUE SURVIVES** — UNLOAD removes only the function binding; never-`DEFINE`d → succeeds **in silence**; twice → succeeds; a **builtin** → **ERROR 248** *attempted redefinition of system function* (the manual's user-functions-only rule as SPITBOL speaks it); a non-name string `'1BAD'` → **SUCCEEDS** and an integer → **SUCCEEDS**, so the argument is **TYPE-validated and never NAME-validated**; null/no arg → **ERROR 201**; an ARRAY → **ERROR 201**. SCRIP now matches every one by code AND message text.
**THE CURE:** `register_fn` is a thin wrapper over `DEFINE_fn`, so builtins and user functions share ONE table and the system/user discriminator is `->fn` (`register_fn` is its only writer; `_DEFINE_` registers with `fn` NULL down both arms). `UNLOAD_fn` unlinks and answers removed / system / unusable-name; `_UNLOAD_` turns those into null / 248 / 201.
**⛔ THE ARM IT DOES NOT REACH, AND THE ROAD THAT WAS EXCLUDED BY MEASUREMENT:** a self-unloading function's NEXT call must raise ERROR 022 — **SCRIP CALLS IT ANYWAY**, because the call site jumps through a **cell `DEFINE` baked** and never asks the registry. I tried the obvious symmetric cure (`rt_proc_set_fn(name,NULL)`) and it made the witness **WORSE** — a wild call and a core dump — which is the proof the call road is the cell. Reverted. Curing it is a **template rung** (a NULL test at the cell routed to the error), so PLAN.md step 7's BB-codegen design set binds whoever takes it.
**⛔ AND f12 STILL FAILS ON A GAP THAT IS NOT UNLOAD'S: `SETEXIT` IS A WRITE-ONLY STUB** — `_setexit_label` (core.c:1277) is written by `_SETEXIT_` and **read by NOTHING** (grep across `src/`: three writes, zero reads). Proof it is independent: a probe containing **no UNLOAD at all** (`&ERRLIMIT=1` + `SETEXIT('OK')` + a call to a never-defined function) is **oracle PASS / SCRIP abort**. `keywords.c:187` already says so in its own comment. f12 has **MOVED** — from aborting at statement 0 to running to its own verdict line — but **no `.ref` was minted for it: pinning one now would pin a lie.**
**⭐ LOAD STUB AUDITED AS THE ROW ASKED — IT IS ITS OWN DEFECT:** `_b_LOAD_stub` (core.c:433) answers only names beginning `MON_` and **silently FAILS** everything else, where the oracle raises **ERROR 142** *load function does not exist*. A **monitor hook wearing LOAD's name**. Left untouched so the monitor keeps working. **THREE ROWS IMPLIED** (SETEXIT transfer · the unloaded-call cell test · the LOAD stub); asked of HQ. Probes: `corpus/probe/unload/` — three green both modes, plus `un_self_exec.sno` checked in **RED and deliberately un-reffed** (the oracle answers it with an error termination dump; class (ii) of `ref-the-ungraded-suites` forbids minting a ref from one). Full write-up: `FINDING-2026-08-20-s193-unload-was-one-of-three-gaps-and-the-brief-named-only-the-first.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s193 (seat4 `/home/claude4`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `apply-snodef-m4`, rank 25) — **THE m4 IMAGE WAS NEVER TOLD WHERE `<FN>_α` LIVES, AND THE SEAL THAT WOULD HAVE TOLD IT WAS DELETED BY A RULING ABOUT SOMETHING ELSE.**
**LANDED: SCRIP `99cd7ede` · corpus `07bd862e` · .github this commit. `make pristine` at the MERGED head, RT_OPT `-O0`, both A/B arms at that one commit** (four seats' commits landed beneath mid-session — `c7560c6b` `3f0dc46a` `eef7fec9` `c52f3529` = 16 files/~1000 lines, then `c43d8f51` FENCE1-DEPTH on a SECOND rebase — so every number below is post-rebase, re-proved pristine after BOTH, not quoted forward).
**ROOT CAUSE, THREE INSTRUMENTS.** `rt_call_proc_descr` enters a dyn_scope callee via `rt_dyn_alpha_fn`, which correctly DECLINES an unsealed `alpha$<FN>` (D-18c) and falls back to `p->fn` — the body-entry STATEMENT label `rt_define_site` registered, i.e. the GENERIC ENTRY THUNK, whose protocol `rt_proc_enter`'s wire jmp cannot speak. m3 seals the cell (`m3_seal_entry_cells`); **m4 never does.** Proven by (1) killswitch equivalence — `SCRIP_DYN_ALPHA=0` in **m3 reproduces the m4 SEGV exactly** on every APPLY witness while leaving the direct-call sibling green; (2) gdb `rip=_rtld_global`, the **verbatim** signature `rt.c:1993`'s s117 comment names; (3) asm diff — the DEFINE half and the whole `ADD1` blob are byte-identical between green sibling and crash. **This is s117's hazard through the sibling it was never applied to, and s170 R1 layer 2 reached with NO EVAL and NO fragment.**
**⭐ THE SEAL WAS COLLATERAL OF A CORRECT RULING.** The m4 startup hoist already emits `lea rsi,[rip+<FN>_α]`; Lon's s114 STARTUP-HOIST-DELETE skips every dyn_scope proc because REGISTRATION belongs at the DEFINE. **A seal is not a registration** — both by-name entries consult `rt_proc_find` FIRST and still fail until the DEFINE runs, so this rung publishes the address only and the ruling stands verbatim. Two facts were riding one emission; deleting the first took the second with it.
**⛔ THE α FACE IS NOT ALWAYS EMITTED AND THE LINKER IS THE ONLY HONEST ORACLE FOR IT.** It exists only where the TINY shim is admitted (`1010_func_recursion` has NO `fact_α` and passes without one); the driver cannot ask the live label pool because a proc blob's pool is retired long before `module_init` emits at the bottom. `.weak <FN>_α` + `@GOTPCREL` yields rsi=0 for a missing face and the leaf's `!fn` guard no-ops it. **A plain `lea` is R_X86_64_PC32 against an undefined symbol, which a PIE link REFUSES — MEASURED: that first spelling turned `1010_func_recursion` from PASS into SKIP(compile/link), caught only because SKIP moved 1→2 while PASS moved +4 against 5 cures. A cure count that does not reconcile with the pass count is hiding a regression, and the SKIP lane is where a link failure goes to be silent.**
**BOARD (A/B = `SCRIP_M4_ALPHA_SEAL` on ONE build):** corpus **m4 332/7 → 337/2 (+5)**, m3 **339/1 unchanged both arms**; crosscheck **m4 315/4 → 318/1 (+3)**, m3 319/1 unchanged, **DIVERGE 3 → 0**; bench **ok=15 bad=0 xfail=0 xpass=0** (`indirect_dispatch` XFAIL:CRASH → green, marker retired). **Cured BY NAME: `expr_eval` · `140_pat_eval_double_fn_trick` · `141_pat_eval_double_fn_arbno` · `semantic_driver` · `demo_claws5` — ZERO new reds.** ⭐ `semantic_driver`/`demo_claws5` are **beauty-family**: `semantic.inc` IS the OPSYN grammar engine FINDING-s156 named as the B1 blocker.
**KILLSWITCH BYTE-IDENTITY AT SCALE:** `=0` regenerates the checked-in `.s` byte-identically on **213/213** comparable programs, **0 movers**. Gates `emit_no_lang` · `template_medium_invisible` · `icn_no_stack` · `icn_semicolon_required` GREEN. ⛔ prolog + snocone smokes RED in **both arms**, identical verdict lines — **pre-existing, not this rung.**
**⛔ NOT MINE, SAID OUT LOUD:** `mixed_workload` XPASSes **with the killswitch OFF too**, and all four `probe/mwseg/` witnesses are green in both arms — the s170 §2a class was closed by an EARLIER rung. Marker retired as stale **by measurement**, not claimed as a cure.
**RULES step-4 regen: benchmark 15 · feature 21 · demo 6 · crosscheck 41 · programs 0 · prolog-bench 0.** The crosscheck 41 were attributed **file-by-file under the killswitch**: **37 are this rung's seal block, 4 are seat7's `goto-code-object-parse`** (3 newly-emitting — they had no `.s` at all before — plus `1021_code_direct_goto`, which owns all 39 deletions).
**NEXT / ROUTED:** (a) `rt_call_proc_direct` is the **third** by-name path and still unsealed-blind — `rt.c:2006` says so in its own "s117 SUSPECTED TWIN, NOT LANDED" comment; no witness reaches it yet, left verbatim rather than changed blind. (b) `$FN(X)` is a **different** class, still divergent: oracle `ERROR 022`, SCRIP `Error 5` in **all three arms** — both errors, so no board shows it.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s193-the-m4-image-was-never-told-where-the-alpha-face-lives.md`
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s191 (HQ, beauty lane, hands-on per Lon) — **THE M1 LADDER MOVED 3/10 → 5/10, FIRST RED 10 → 40**
**LANDED (SCRIP `008c2264`, killswitch `SCRIP_PRE_ORDER`, default ON):** the pattern pre-chain drained in **reverse source order**, so a failing guard could not short-circuit the operand it guards. Witness: `A = 0` / `'abcdef' GT(A,0) LEN(A-10) . ind` → oracle `nomatch`, SCRIP fatal `Error 121`. **beauty's `Gen.inc:42` is that shape verbatim** and the guard exists precisely to keep the LEN argument non-negative; feeding beauty the single line `START` went `Error 121` → `START`. The loop's own comment was the tell (*"SEQ lowers right-first…"* — false at this HEAD; the IR shows `COERCE_INTEGER` at node 18 and the guard's `CMP_TEST` at node 23). **Mirror-image witness** (`probe/preord/pre_unsafe_then_guard`) proves the mechanism: unsafe-first, the oracle errors and SCRIP said `nomatch`. Receipts: corpus **m3 333/4 · m4 326/10**, fail-set identical armed vs `=0`; **0 `.s` movers** across benchmark/feature/demo/programs; smoke 7/7; killswitch non-vacuous. 6 witnesses at `corpus/probe/preord/`.
**NEXT WALL, ROOT-CAUSED, NOT YET CURED (rank-0 row `dcap-freturn-false-accept`, seat6):** a deferred call in **capture-target** position that **fails** (`:F(FRETURN)`) is treated as SUCCESS. Every beauty token rule is `SPAN(…) $ tx $ *match(LIST, TxInList)` with `match()` returning `:F(FRETURN)` on non-membership, so **the first guarded alternative always wins** — measured on `L X = 1`: oracle classifies `X` as `Id` and `1` as `Integer` and calls `match()` **7×**; SCRIP calls both `BuiltinVar` and calls it **4×**. Drained by `rt_dcap_end_ok_open` from `bb_match_end` — *after* the match succeeded, so failure has nothing to retract. ⛔ Hazard: the pump's `rc` shares a register with the FN-RET **transfer protocol**. 8-line witness + green `NRETURN` control at `corpus/probe/dcap/`.
**BEAUTY_SUITE, MEASURED BOTH MODES (M1 DOD item 2):** **m3 17/17 · m4 16/17** — the single red is `semantic_driver` (m3 byte-identical to its ref, m4 SIGSEGV), row `semantic-driver-m4-segv`.
**FINDINGs:** `…s191-a-failing-guard-could-not-short-circuit-the-operand-it-guards.md` · `…s191-a-failing-deferred-capture-target-is-treated-as-success.md`.

Board **m3 335/2 · m4 328/8 SKIP 1** = seat3's s192 watermark exactly, **measured twice — before and after the rebase**. Gates: `emit_no_lang` OK · BOTH-MEDIUM ratchet **0** (ceiling 0). **No compiler change, no new global, no killswitch, no template.**
**⛔ THE BRIEF IS HALF TRUE AND THE FALSE HALF NAMES A DELETED FUNCTION.** *"an ALT arm holding ARB or BAL takes the same wild write"* — **TRUE**, and now witnessed. *"`leaf_frame_candidate` REFUSES both, so `SCRIP_SPAN_FRAME` cannot cure it on either arm"* — **`leaf_frame_candidate` has not existed since s174 (`44b8b82c`, Lon's NO-PER-OP-FILTER ruling)**, `leaf_frame_member` is location-only, `zdp_scratch_cell` names ARB and BAL as ordinary members, and **`SPAN_FRAME=1` cures BOTH ops in BOTH media** — it has been the **shipped default since s188 (`d3251f23`)**.
**⛔⭐⭐⭐ THE REAL FIND: `probe/leafsib/` IS A DEAD INSTRUMENT.** All eight siblings emit **byte-identical `--compile` output under `SPAN_FRAME=0` and `=1`** and read 8/8 on both — so the set would report **GREEN straight through a full regression of the class**. Cause is the ROAD, not the switch: all eight take the **STORED** road, where the cell is already rbp-resident by blob-frame authority (`leafsib_arb` spells it `[rbp-96]` with the switch OFF). They are green for a reason unrelated to the switch they test.
**⛔ AND THE INSTRUMENT MISLABELLED ITS OWN ARM FOR FIVE SESSIONS.** `sn4_span_frame()` is `(e && *e=='0') ? 0 : 1` — **unset is ON** — while `probe_leafsib_measure.sh` read `ARM="${SCRIP_SPAN_FRAME:-0}"`, correct at s131 and false from s188: every default run printed the **OFF label over ON-arm numbers**. This is line 590 of this file recurring one rung later (*"a killswitch outlives its arm, and the gates that grade it keep scoring for years"*). Label is now **COMPUTED from the compiler's own rule, never typed**.
**⭐ WITNESSES MINTED (the row's deliverable), `corpus/probe/leafsib/leafsib_{arb,bal}_flat_{red,grn}`** — the same leaf on an **INLINE** ALT arm, where the cell IS a raw flat rsp coordinate. Only the padding count differs within each pair, so the pair proves the defect is the **COORDINATE'S MAGNITUDE**, never the op: 20 pads → ARB `[rsp+480]` / BAL `[rsp+464]/+468/+472`, **m4 rc=139**; 5 pads → `[rsp+240]`/`[rsp+224]`, green. Under `=1` all home at `[rbp-80]`, carve `sub rsp,24`→`56` (+32 = the two consecutive 16B slots s174 granted), `retry_whack` `[rbp-56]`→`[rbp-88]`. **A green on the OFF arm is LUCK** — one more padding assignment is a SIGSEGV. Instrument now **12/12 default · 10/12 under `=0`**: it discriminates again. All four `.ref` minted `LIVE` from `sbl -bf` through `util_ref_mint.sh` → the board's own oracle door.
**⭐ BOTH s131 DECLINES ARE DEAD, EACH A DIFFERENT DEATH.** `leafsib_bal` (*"spends +0/+4/+8, d=8 is the neighbour's granule floor"*): the **spend is still real and visible in the asm**, but the **8-byte window law that made it a refusal died at s174** — every leaf claims 32B, so BAL's third word is in its own claim. `leafsib_arb` (*"its cell is never re-entered"*): true of **that witness**, false of the op — on the inline road the cell is re-entered on every implicit alternative (**SPITBOL v3.7 p.207: ARB behaves as `(LEN(0)|LEN(1)|LEN(2)|…)`**, and the manual's own worked example `'ABCDEF' ? 'A' (ARB | 'D') 'E'` **is this row's shape**).
**⛔ NO COMPILER FIX, AND THE ROW'S OWN `fix ONLY if killswitch-clean` IS WHY** — the class is cured at the shipped default; the OFF arm is the documented legacy revert, and widening anything to green it would be arming a killswitch's dead side. The one source edit is `emit.cpp:1710`'s stale *"BAL SELF-REFUSES"* comment, **proven inert: 0 `.s` movers / 40** (20 programs × both arms) across the pristine rebuild. **NOT CLAIMED:** `cn_alt_leaf_{flat,lit}_red` were already green at the default and are red only under `=0` — measured as controls, not cured here. m3/m4 asymmetry on the OFF arm is **named, not root-caused** (reproduces s170's SPAN witness exactly).
**⭐ GENERALISABLE: a witness set is evidence only while it still DISCRIMINATES THE ARMS — and the test is cheap: diff the emitted code across the killswitch.** Eight witnesses byte-identical across the switch they exist to test is a dead instrument, and it fails **GREEN**, the direction nobody audits. Run it whenever a killswitch flips default, and flip the instrument's label in the **same commit**.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s193-the-leafsib-set-went-inert-and-both-declines-outlived-their-refuser.md`
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s192 (seat8 `/home/claude8`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `prototype-spelled-twice`, rank 2) — **`PROTOTYPE` IS ONE AUTHORITY NOW, AND THE ERROR IT REFUSED TO RAISE WAS HIDING TWO DEAD `CONVERT` ARMS.**
**LANDED: SCRIP `eef7fec9` · corpus `3ab5391b`.** Gates green on the pushed, pristine tree: `emit_no_lang` · `template_medium_invisible` (0, ceiling 0) · `icn_no_stack` · `icn_one_reg_frame`. ⛔ **`.s` REGEN NOT APPLICABLE — ZERO emitter / template / lowerer / machine / optimizer files touched;** the rung is four runtime files and two corpus files.
**⭐ THE CURE — ONE BODY, TWO FACES, ARITY TEST DELETED RATHER THAN WIDENED.** `agg_prototype()` (`aggregates.c`) is the one authority; `BID_PROTOTYPE` and `core.c`'s `_PROTOTYPE_` are both faces over it. A count-conditioned admission is what produced the split, so the faces normalise a *missing* argument to the null string and pass the VALUE down — the body never learns how many arguments were written. Three answers, all measured on `sbl -bf`: **ARRAY** → its creating prototype, all-digits ⇒ INTEGER else STRING (**byte-identical to the arm it replaces**, so the brief's requirement-3 holds by construction); **TABLE** → its hash-header count as an INTEGER (`TABLE()`⇒11, `TABLE(5)`⇒5, `TABLE(5,7)`⇒5; `COPY`/`CONVERT` carry it) — the live `BID_TABLE` had been **discarding its arguments entirely**; **anything else** → `ERROR 164`. ⛔ **NO NEW FIELD:** `TBBLK_t.init` was already the one record of the creating first argument (nothing sizes on it — `TABLE_BUCKETS` is a fixed 256); its default moves 10→11 (SPITBOL) and the **three** places that re-typed that default collapse to one. Minting a second field would have been the row's own disease wearing a struct.
**⛔⛔ THE 164 TURNED A GREEN TEST RED AND THE TEST DESERVED IT.** `crosscheck/rung11/1113_table` had been passing **vacuously**: `ta = CONVERT(t,'ARRAY')` was FAILING, `ta` stayed null, `PROTOTYPE(null)` failed the statement — and **a failed statement takes the same `:f` branch the correct answer takes.** Two one-line defects underneath, both the same spelled-twice disease one builtin over: (1) `BID_CONVERT` answered `*out = FAILDESCR; return 1` — *"I handled it, the answer is failure"* — for every target type it does not implement, **shadowing** `core.c`'s `_CONVERT_` which DOES implement ARRAY/TABLE/PATTERN, and it admits `nargs==2` i.e. **every legal `CONVERT` call**, so `CONVERT(t,'ARRAY')` and `CONVERT(a,'TABLE')` were **dead on the live path corpus-wide**; (2) `_CONVERT_`'s array→table arm keyed the table with `VARVAL_fn(kd)` — the plain string — while every table read/write keys through `tbl_key_str`, which **type-tags** the key, so INTEGER `7` was stored under `"7"` and looked up under `"\001i7"`. SCRIP now matches the oracle byte-for-byte on the whole round-trip.
**⭐ THE BLAST RADIUS WAS MEASURED, NOT ARGUED — AND THE INTERMEDIATE BUILD IS WHY.** Pristine (HQ-27) at every point, RT_OPT `-O0`, `test_corpus_snobol4.sh` (⛔ **not** `scorecard_snobol4.sh`, which runs the `programs/lon/` suite RULES.md forbids running): baseline **m3 334/3 · m4 327/9 SKIP 1** → prototype cure + 164 with `CONVERT` untouched **m3 333/4 · m4 326/10**, i.e. **exactly ONE mover per mode and it is the vacuous pass** → both `CONVERT` repairs **m3 334/3 · m4 327/9, fail-set IDENTICAL BY NAME to baseline.** Landing it all at once would have shown 334/3→334/3 and proved nothing about where the risk lived. (A stale binary also cost a bogus `216_indirect_goto_computed` red on the first, non-pristine baseline — HQ-27 earning its keep in one command.)
**WATERMARK on the PUSHED, MERGED tree (SCRIP `eef7fec9`, corpus `3ab5391b`, pristine, `-O0`): m3 335/2 · m4 328/8 · SKIP 1.** ⛔ **THE +1/+1 OVER MY OWN A/B IS NOT MINE AND SAYING SO IS THE POINT** — it arrived with the 9-commit rebase (`175_pat_bal_generator_retry`), not with this rung. My rung's own delta is **zero net, one false green converted to a true one.** Probes `proto_excess_arg` · `proto_table` · `proto_faces` GREEN both modes; rung11 **1110/1112/1113** PASS both modes, 1113 now for real. A runtime `OPSYN` alias of `PROTOTYPE` used to answer `1:3` where the direct call answered `3`; alias, `APPLY` and direct call now all answer INTEGER 3 — which is why `_PROTOTYPE_` was kept as a face rather than deleted: the registry is a **reachable entry point**.
**⛔ NOT DONE HERE, DELIBERATELY: the 164 TERMINATES and does not honour `&ERRLIMIT`.** That is a property of `core_runtime_error`, which is how every other builtin code in this runtime is raised (22, 35, 42, 251, 341, 342); the Ch.16 conversion lives in `keywords.c`'s `kwb_error`, `static` there, serving only 208/209/210/287. Measured: `&ERRLIMIT = 5` then `PROTOTYPE('abc')` still terminates. Routing the builtin codes through the ERRLIMIT-aware raiser is **one general question for one rung** — half-doing it inside PROTOTYPE would make this builtin the only one in the runtime that converts.
**NEXT — three things this row paid for, for whoever takes the follow-ons.** (a) ⭐ **`return 1` from the by-name chain is a CLAIM, and a claim carrying `FAILDESCR` is a lie that shadows a working implementation** — `BID_CONVERT` is unlikely to be the last; the audit is mechanical (grep the chain for arms ending `*out = FAILDESCR; return 1` on an *unrecognised sub-case*, then check whether `core.c` registers a richer spelling of the same name). (b) ⭐ **A `:f(label)` guard on an assertion cannot distinguish the right answer from an upstream collapse** — both 1113/005 and 1113/006 were vacuous for this reason and the crosscheck corpus is written in this style throughout. (c) **`BID_TABLE` still ignores its third argument** (the missing-key default; `TBBLK_t.dflt` exists and is unwired) and raises no 195/196 for a non-integer first argument — filed, not taken.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s192-prototype-was-spelled-twice-and-the-error-it-should-raise-was-hiding-two-dead-convert-arms.md`

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s192 (seat5 `/home/claude5`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `opsyn-3arg-ruling`, rank 2) — **THE RULING IS (a). THE THIRD ARGUMENT IS A KIND SELECTOR, NOT AN ARITY, AND SCRIP WAS NOT BEING *PERMISSIVE* ABOUT IT — IT WAS DISCARDING IT. THIS CLOSES HALF OF R-8.**
**WATERMARK (pristine at the PUSHED heads SCRIP `3f0dc46a` · corpus `6f174239`, RT_OPT `-O0`, both trees clean; re-proven from pristine a THIRD time because the push rebased onto seat8's `prototype-spelled-twice` `eef7fec9`, which edits the same `core.c`/`core.h` this rung touches):** broad corpus **m3 335/2 · m4 328/8 · SKIP 1 (337)**. ⛔ **THE +1/+1 IS NOT MINE.** This rung's own A/B is **0/0**: pre- and post-patch boards at SCRIP `d1994fe3` were BOTH `m3 334/3 · m4 327/9 · SKIP 1`, fail-sets identical by name. The board moved only after a `git pull --rebase` brought in seat3's s192 `rty-fail-inline-retry` (`ed61196c`) and seat6's `const-nest-flip` (`c512089a`), and the row that went green is **`175_pat_bal_generator_retry`** — *a generator never retries*, seat3's brief verbatim. **A board run after a rebase measures the MERGED tree**; this seat wrote that warning at s191 and nearly walked into it from the other side here, because this time the delta was FLATTERING. ⭐ Corroborated without asking: seat3's own s192 cursor already records `m3 335/2 · m4 328/8 SKIP 1` at `ed61196c`, measured before this patch existed, and my post-rebase board reproduces it to the digit. This rung's board contribution is **zero by construction** — the only program it moves is the new probe, and the broad board does not run `probe/`. Gates: `emit_no_lang` · `template_medium_invisible` · `icn_no_stack` · `icn_one_reg_frame` · `icn_semicolon_required`, all **rc=0**, re-proven from pristine AFTER the rebase. **`.s` regen N/A AND STRUCTURALLY SO** — the three touched files are all runtime (`pattern_match.c`, `core.c`/`.h`, `keywords.c`); nothing in `emitter/`, `templates/`, `x86_asm.h`, `lower_*` or the optimizer moved and `opsyn`'s symbol/signature are unchanged, so no emitted byte can differ.
**⭐⭐ THE BRIEF WAS WRONG TWICE AND THE MANUAL SETTLES BOTH.** (1) It calls arg 3 an **arity**; v3.7 p.116 + p.234-235 make it a **KIND SELECTOR** — 0/omitted = FUNCTION synonym, 1 = s1 must be an UNUSED UNARY operator, 2 = s1 must be an UNUSED BINARY operator, else **ERROR 156**. (2) It calls SCRIP "more permissive"; `opsyn()` opened `(void)type;` — the argument was **read and discarded**, which is worse: `OPSYN('SUM','+',2); OUTPUT = SUM(3,4)` printed **NOTHING** and `OPSYN('+','ADD',2); OUTPUT = 3 + 4` printed **7** with the rebind silently dropped — matching **neither** oracle.
**⛔⭐ THREE FACTS MEASURED ON THE ORACLE, EACH AGAINST THE OBVIOUS READING.** (a) A 19-char sweep returns unary `! # % / = |` and binary `# % & @ ~`, and `1 + 5 == 6` IS the SIL's `add =opbun,wb / beq wb,=opuun,sops3` arithmetic — sweep and engine confirm each other. (b) **i is NOT restricted to {0,1,2}**: that arithmetic sends 1 to the unary table and EVERYTHING else non-zero to the binary one, so `i=3` and `i=99` are accepted binary opsyns — a `kind == 2` test would have been **stricter than SPITBOL**. (c) The 153 cutoff is **2^24, bisected** (16777216 accepted, 16777217 refused) — not INT_MAX; **the identical bound KW-7 bisected for ERROR 210**: one small-integer range, two error codes, corroboration rather than a second authority.
**⭐⭐⭐ WHY IT IS A CORRECTNESS FIX, NOT CONFORMANCE PEDANTRY: SCRIP'S OWN OPTIMIZER ALREADY DEPENDED ON THE RULE THE RUNTIME NEVER ENFORCED.** `optimizer/proc_collect.c` `scc_taint_graph` treats a literal arg 3 of 1 or 2 as PROOF the OPSYN is an operator rebind and leaves SCC on — so `OPSYN('SUM','+',2)` rebound a FUNCTION straight through that exemption. That is the manual's own stated reason arrived at from the other end (p.116: *"by not allowing basic system functions and operators to be redefined, SPITBOL is able to optimize the code it generates"*). `proc_collect.c` is UNTOUCHED: its test now taints CONSERVATIVELY over an invariant that is, for the first time, enforced.
**⛔ THE ERRORS RIDE `kwb_error`, AND THE PROBE FORCED IT.** The first cut used `core_runtime_error` and **printed and exited** where the oracle merely fails the statement — its conversion arm needs a pushed `jmp_buf` and only the EVAL boundary pushes one. `kwb_error` is KW-5's ONE `&ERRLIMIT` adjudicator (decrement, publish `&ERRTYPE`/`&ERRTEXT`, return 0 for the caller to propagate FAIL) exactly as 208/209/210 do, and **at the default `&ERRLIMIT` of 0 it calls `core_runtime_error` itself — so the default arm is behaviourally unchanged.** `is_numeric_like` exported from `core.c` rather than respelled. NO NEW GLOBAL: killswitch `SCRIP_OPSYN_KIND=0` is a bare `getenv`, no memoising `static` — `opsyn()` runs once per OPSYN statement, so the cache would have bought nothing and minted file-scope state.
**BLAST RADIUS, MEASURED THREE WAYS, ZERO COST.** Census: **7 refused sites in 4 files** of 70 parsed — and not one is a currently-green graded row (`alis.sno` parse-errors on `\` in BOTH engines; `SYSTEM.sno`'s sites are unreachable **by that module's own dialect gate, and SCRIP agrees with the oracle about why** — `IDENT(DATATYPE(.X),'STRING')`, where `sbl` and SCRIP both answer `NAME` and take `SYSTEM_2` while CSNOBOL4 answers `STRING`; `SYSTEM_driver` prints `SPITBOL` on the oracle and in both killswitch arms; `f14_opsyn` and `coverage_sno_nodes` have no `.ref`). Killswitch A/B (`SCRIP_OPSYN_KIND=0`) over **all 54** OPSYN-mentioning corpus programs: **1 mover / 54**, and it is the new probe. **`programs/include/` cleared WITHOUT READING A LINE OF IT:** all **144** gimpel drivers run with it on `SNO_LIB`, scanned for the *presence* of 152/153/156 — **0 of 144**. A question about content answered by error-code presence; a grep would have published the files.
**⛔⭐ THE INSTRUMENT ALMOST PUBLISHED TWO FALSE MOVERS.** An md5 A/B first named `ORVISUAL_driver` and `PERM_driver`; **neither arm is self-stable** (ORVISUAL: 3 md5s in 3 default runs; PERM: 3 distinct md5s in EACH arm). A single-draw diff over a flapping program is two draws from one distribution. ⭐ **Self-diff each arm before an A/B convicts** — seat1's s191 nqueens and seat7's s190 mover count, one family. The replacement instrument (does a NEW error code appear?) does not care about flapping at all.
**LANDED — corpus:** `feat/f14_opsyn.sno` REWRITTEN to the legal forms (Lon's rank-0 `kw-uppercase-dialect` doctrine: normalize the program, do not relabel it) + `.ref` byte-identical to `sbl -bf`, **m3 ≡ m4 ≡ oracle**; **NEW `probe/opsyn/opsyn_kind_selector.sno`** pins the whole contract in **13 `&ERRLIMIT`-converted diffable lines**, `.ref` from the live oracle, **13/13 both modes**, green in `probes_misc`.
**⭐⭐ TWO SEATS REACHED THIS FILE FROM OPPOSITE ENDS IN ONE SESSION AND AGREED.** seat1's s191 `ref-the-ungraded-suites` minter pinned **16** new `feat/` refs and **skipped `f14_opsyn`**; through its own `scorecard_snobol4.sh oracle` door the PRE-rung program classifies **`DEAD_REPORT`** (`sbl` exited 0 while printing a fatal dump) — unmintable by construction. Both of this rung's `.ref` files were validated through that same door and come back **`LIVE`, byte-identical**. Their tool refused the file as unpinnable; this row says *why the program was wrong*, and after normalizing it the same door mints it.
**⭐⭐⭐ THE GENERALISABLE MOVE — WHEN THE ORACLE'S ANSWER IS AN ERROR, DO NOT PIN THE ERROR REPORT; MAKE THE WITNESS CATCH THE ERROR AND PRINT ITS CODE.** `&ERRLIMIT` + `&ERRTYPE` turns any SPITBOL error class into stable, honest, diffable output. It dissolves `probe/opsyn/unstable-oracle/` — parked at s105 because the ERROR-156 banner is unpinnable, with **R-8(c)** posed as "normalise the grader OR leave it manual". This is option **(c)** and needs no grader change. ⛔ And the park's stated reason did NOT reproduce: five live draws of the parked witness gave **one md5, five times** — the banner is unpinnable because **`sbl` exits 0 while dying**, not because it drifts. README rewritten in place, `.manual` pair kept, **nothing deleted**. `test_stack` (R-3(f)) is the obvious next candidate and is NAMED, not claimed.
**NEXT — R-8's SURVIVING HALF, AND ITS s105 TABLE IS TWO-THIRDS STALE (re-measured this session, pristine, both modes):** `&`/`@`/`~` were SIG11 and are now **PASS/PASS**; `opsyn_rebind_twice` was SIG11 and is now **m3 PASS / m4 FAIL**. Surviving class = the one R-8 already named — the grammar hard-wires the UNUSED operators to arithmetic (`T_2POUND→TT_MUL`, `T_2PERCENT→TT_DIV`, `T_1BANG→TT_POW`, …) so `#`, `%` and the whole unary family carry a static identity SPITBOL forbids them: `opsyn_bin_pound`, `opsyn_bin_pct`, `opsyn_builtin_target`, `opsyn_unary_target`, `d_unary` (the last three do not even COMPILE in m4). ⛔ **TWO m3 ≠ m4 SPLITS, AND ONE IS IN NO LEDGER AT ALL: `opsyn_rebind_twice` and `f_apply` (m3 PASS, m4 SIGSEGV).** Both PRE-EXISTING — the killswitch A/B moves neither. **This rung teaches the REFUSAL half only and says so:** the *legal* unary form still does not PARSE. ERROR 155 deliberately not implemented (the oracle ACCEPTS `OPSYN('&','DIFFER',0)`, so "natural variable name" is wider than "identifier"; no witness, and a guess could only reject working programs).
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s192-opsyns-third-argument-is-a-kind-selector-and-scrip-was-discarding-it.md`

A board run after a rebase measures the MERGED tree; attributing its whole delta to your own rung is how a watermark becomes a lie (seat5 s191, seat4 s192 — the third sighting).
**⛔ THE DEFECT IS ONE LOAD.** `IR_MATCH_FENCE1` banks its watermark at a **static RSP offset** (`mov [rsp+176], rsp`). A FENCE1 inside an ARBNO body is **re-entered at a NEW depth every iteration** (the retreat arrives `PAT$N_β → arbno_β → fence1_α` with the previous instance's growth still on the stack), so the same `+176` walks. gdb ignore-counter on `rty_fence_capdefer_plainleft`: iteration 1 slot = `rbp+104` (above the frame, harmless); **iteration 2 slot = `rbp-24`** — the blob head's **DTP cell** (`blob_head_bytes`: `-8 r10 · -16 r11 · -24 rdx · -32 r12`) — and `n7_match_defer_α: mov rdi,[rbp-24]` then loaded **`0x7fffffffdf80`, the fence's own saved rsp**, as its pattern operand. SPAN matches garbage, ARBNO never extends, answer `fail`.
**⭐⭐ THE CURE IS ONE LINE AND THE MECHANISM ALREADY EXISTED.** `R-4(f) SLICE 3` (s96) built the depth-safe arm — watermark on an activation-frame slot `[rbp+off]`, *"no RSP-relative offset at any depth"* — and `frame_slot_scan` already supports the stored-blob class (`R-4(b)` s97, rc=2). `fence_frame_candidate` never asked for it because **it inspects only the fence's OWN BODY SPAN**, and here P is the bare literal `'+'`. The hazard was never in the body; it was in the **enclosing context**. The lowerer has stamped this class since s42+1 (`IR_LIT(F).ival = 2`, `lower_snobol4.c:1695`) and used it only to suppress the U-2 frame. CURE = admit `ival==2`. Watermark → `[rbp-80]`, below the blob head; blob frame 72→88. **1 insertion, 0 deletions, one file.** ⛔ Keyed on **ENTRY DEPTH, never an op** (NO-PER-OP-FILTER). **NO new global, NO new killswitch** (`getenv` count unchanged), NO new opcode, NO template touched.

Stored-vs-inline was never the arm selection. It was the depth. ⭐ Also corrected: the 398-line `.s` diff that opened the hunt said nothing (a capture renumbers every node); what broke it open was a **2×2 ablation** (FENCE × variable-width element × capture — the red needs all three) and the manual's own trace idiom (v3.7 p.140), `$ OUTPUT`: red prints `2` and stops, green prints `2` then `3`.

**⭐ THE GENERALISABLE MOVE: a static offset is a claim about depth, and a box that can be re-entered has no single depth to make it about.** The predicate was written to detect *"my body will move the frontier"* and was right about every witness that had one; it never considered the frontier moving **underneath it** between one entry and the next. Same family as s192's `k > r` and s189's `default: return 0`. ⭐⭐ **And the tree named this bug twice and never wired it to a predicate:** the s192 header on `rty_fence_capdefer_both_segv` (*"a STATIC-DEPTH slot … rsp moves per iteration, Tier D"*) and the FENCE1 template's own `fence_whack_commit` H14 note, which carries the witness `mov rsp,[rsp+176]` verbatim. **A comment that knows the bug is not a guard.**
**NEXT:** (1) `fz_hang_16` still rc=124 both arms, untouched. (2) The FENCE1 watermark is now depth-safe **only for the ARBNO-body class**; any other construct that re-enters a FENCE1 at a shifted depth would need the same admission — the honest scope is "re-entered at a moving depth", not "in an ARBNO". (3) `160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume` is the last m3 red on the broad board.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s193-a-fence-in-an-arbno-body-is-entered-one-iteration-deeper-every-time.md`.

Did NOT reclassify them either — that MOVES META, and HQ ruled at s189 that what counts as scoreable is the CEO's knob. Asked as `q-ref-parser-vacuous-passes`. ⛔ **TWO REASONS DEAD_REPORT MUST NEVER BE PINNED AND THE SECOND IS NOWHERE WRITTEN DOWN:** the dump defines SPITBOL's ERROR as correct, **and it embeds the ABSOLUTE PATH of the source file** — so the pin is not portable between seats at all (seat2 fails it by construction, its root is `/home/claude2`).

Oracle invocation is now ONE function `sc_oracle_run`; the ragged WEIGHTS tail is parsed by ONE function `sc_suite_fields`. ⛔ `cmd_run`'s loop is a THIRD spelling, deliberately NOT converted (it iterates every row; rewriting it would risk the headline instrument inside another seat's row) — flagged, not hidden. **VALIDATED THE DOOR BEFORE MINTING:** `f13_eval_code` already had a pin and re-mints **byte-identical**. Refactor inert: csnobol4_suite moves exactly ONE row of 124 across it — `nqueens`, the ASLR row, **third sighting and nothing else**. `util_ref_mint.sh` censuses by default, mints only on LIVE, names every other class, and **refuses `programs/lon/` + `programs/include/` by construction** (both tested).
**ROUTED:** `parser-acceptance-harness` (parser/ wants an ACCEPT/REJECT comparison, not stdout diff — and that instrument immediately converts the 23 DEAD_REPORT rows into a real finding: **the oracle REJECTS that syntax and SCRIP ACCEPTS it** — `binary_opsyn.sno` gets `ERROR 029 undefined operator` from SPITBOL while SCRIP compiles it clean, and **all 88 parser programs emit asm under `--compile`**; same class as `opsyn-3arg-ruling` at 23× scale) · `parser-fn-define-m4-asm-fail` · `q-ref-parser-vacuous-passes`.
**⭐ THE GENERALISABLE MOVE: a test that CANNOT FAIL is worse than a missing one, because a missing test is visible in the denominator and a vacuous one is not.** The 61 were never an unfished pond — they were 61 green lights wired to nothing, and the board had been reporting them as passes the whole time. ⭐ And when a brief's DONE-WHEN would make the corpus worse, the deliverable is the MEASUREMENT that shows it, not the compliance.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s192 (seat3 `/home/claude3`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `rty-fail-inline-retry`, rank 1) — **THE `FAIL` RETREAT WAS WIRED ALL ALONG. A STATEMENT-TERMINAL POP WAS STAMPED IN FRONT OF IT, AND IT FREED THE RETRY CURSOR THE RETREAT WAS ON ITS WAY TO READ.**

RULES step-4 regens run (6 scripts incl. `util_regen_crosscheck_s_artifacts.sh` — 175's `.s` is in that tree and DID change).

(a) **There is no `IR_MATCH_FAIL`** — `FAIL` lowers to a **bare `IR_GOTO`** with BOTH ports φ-tagged (`lower_snobol4.c:1365`), repointed at the left neighbour as **β** by `sno_seq_nary`'s fixup. (b) **The retreat edge was CORRECT** — the asm jumps to `n6_match_assign_imm_β`, exactly right. The whole defect is the instruction in FRONT of it: `add rsp,48`, the statement-terminal ζ release, freeing the `[rsp+0]`/`[rsp+4]` cells `n5_match_arb_β` reads to extend. Oracle A/AB/ABC, SCRIP A.
**⭐⭐ THE LINE, AND THE ω SIDE HAD ALREADY FIXED IT ONCE.** `zd_plan`'s γ-release membership test was FORWARD-ONLY (`k > r`); a β-tagged γ arc is a RETREAT and lands at or BEHIND its own run position (the FAIL relay chases straight back to the node itself, `k == r`), so it read as leaving the statement. The ω twin carries the same relaxation already and **its comment predicted this bug verbatim**: *"ZD-5b-SAVE-OIN (s14): drop k>r on oin — … forward-only guard was correct for gin but wrong for oin."* CURE = one derived predicate + one conjunct, `(k > r || gib)`. ⭐ The chase is mandatory: **the tag lives on the intervening RELAY, never on the producer** — ASSIGN_IMM's own `γ.sz` is CLEAR. ⭐⭐ **AND THIS IS WHY THE STORED ROAD WAS GREEN — the `nblob` arm ONE LINE ABOVE has NO positional test at all.** The brief's *"the blob road IS the specification"* landed on the exact line that made the two roads disagree.

**⭐ THE DEFECT WAS LIVE IN EIGHT PROGRAMS AND VISIBLE IN ONE.** In the other seven the retreat cascades straight into an ABSOLUTE unwind (`lea rsp,[rbp-56]` retry_whack / frame whack) that re-establishes RSP before anything reads it. Only a retreat landing on an EXTENDING generator — the manual's implicit-alternatives class, ARB/BAL (v3.7 p.208), which keeps its retry cursor on the ζ-spine at a static offset — gets poisoned. SPAN is not in that class (p.126, longest match, one instance), which is why `rty_fail_span_ctl` is the right guard: **its emission moves and its output does not.**
**⭐⭐ BLAST RADIUS, ALL 4982 CORPUS PROGRAMS, ALL SEVEN LANGUAGES** (`programs/lon/` excluded BY CONSTRUCTION, `find … -prune`): **9 rows differ, 8 are the cure, 1 is the instrument.** ⛔ **THE NOISE FLOOR WAS MEASURED FIRST AND IT IS NOT ZERO** — the control arm self-diffed against ITSELF gives **1 row**, `programs/snobol4/parser/unary_not.sno`, whose `.S0` rodata is nondeterministic (one binary, three runs, three md5s — s147's named class). It is IN the mover list; quoting 9 without the self-diff would have published a phantom. All 8 real movers are ONE mechanism (a single `add rsp,N` deleted) and all 8 are ORACLE-IDENTICAL at `sbl -bf`.
**⛔ ZERO Icon/Prolog/Raku/Pascal/Rebus MOVERS, AND THAT IS THE LOAD-BEARING HALF.** The planner's own `ZK-2 GEN-BACK-GIN SCOPE BOUNDARY` comment records that setting `gin=1` for the Icon `every` back-edge (`IR_ASSIGN.γ → IR_TO.β`, ALSO a β-tagged γ arc) was MEASURED wrong and refused. **⭐ RE-PROVED AT MERGED `ed61196c`, ITS OWN `make pristine`, AND THAT WAS AN INTERACTION CHECK NOT A FORMALITY:** seat2's FENCE-RTAIL (`56842aab`, Class B) touches the same seq/resume-carrier machinery and landed between my A/B and my push; also in: seat4 REAL-FN-FAMILY, HQ beauty PRE-CHAIN. **⭐ THE GENERALISABLE MOVE:** **a guard that is correct only because no input has ever exercised it is not correct — it is unexercised.** `k > r` was written when every γ arc went forward and stayed load-bearing while that remained accidentally true; the s14 fix's own comment called the γ side "correct", and it was correct about the inputs of that day.
