## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s189 (seat6, Claude Opus 5; queue row `alt-resume-carrier-selection`, rank 1) — **IT IS NOT A CARRIER-SELECTION DEFECT. THE ALT'S CHOICE RECORD IS ADDRESSED OFF THE *LIVE RSP*, SO ANY ARM-INTERIOR CARVE MOVES IT — AND THE CURE IS BUILT, DOCUMENTED "DEPTH-IMMUNE", AND ADMITTED ONLY FOR SHAPES THAT DO NOT NEED IT.**
**WATERMARK (measured at SCRIP `3dc52576`; ⭐ RE-PROVED IN FULL AT `28e2122a` / corpus `1741f3ad` after seat7's `alt-seam-tier` landed MID-RUNG on this FINDING's own files — `make pristine` EXIT=0 BOTH times, RT_OPT `-O0`, NO FILE TOUCHED, both trees clean):** corpus board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1 (337)** at BOTH revisions, fail-set identical **by name** (diffed). barer twin GREEN · **ptc3 grid 8/8 GREEN**.
**⛔ THE RE-PROVE MOVED TWO WITNESSES, SAID FIRST:** seat7's tier-3 admission **CURED `ptw_min_fence_alttop` and `ptw_min_fence_left_altresume`** (GREEN both arms at `28e2122a`). Still red: the fence-free **PRIMARY** `ptw_min_alttop_nofence_ctl` and `ptw_min_rseal_unsealed_ctl`. **Everything else re-measured IDENTICAL** — mechanism, full prediction table, asm signature, twin, ptc3, board. The class did not shrink; it is now exactly the residue seat7 had to fence off.
**⭐⭐⭐ SEAT7 HIT THE SAME BOUNDARY FROM THE CRASH SIDE AND THIS ROW IS THE MISSING *WHY*.** `28e2122a` records that tier 3 alone *"cured two witnesses and SIGSEGV'd two programs"*, so `_nc == 1` was an **earned conjunct**: *"ALT-FLAT makes arm INTERIORS footprint-0, **which is not the claim that an arm holds no other carving choice node**."* That clause IS this rung's mechanism, reached empirically — seat7 established THAT a second carving choice node in an arm breaks the box; the asm trace below says WHY, instruction by instruction. Two independent results agreeing. **`_nc == 1` is therefore a correctness boundary with a named cause, not a conservative hold to be relaxed later.** All four witness `.ref`s re-derived from the LIVE oracle, identical under **both** `-b` and `-bf`. ⛔ scorecard NOT run (executes `programs/lon/`).
**DISPOSITION: INVESTIGATION-ONLY, MECHANISM NAMED** — Lon ruled it in-chat, and the row's own "if killswitch-clean" test is **NEGATIVE by measurement** (see below).
**⛔ THE BRIEF'S SUSPECT ROAD IS FALSIFIED.** It names the publisher (`sno_pat_carrier_build`/`sno_pat_publish_body_root`) and says it must publish "the arm's RESUMABLE node (the inner alternation), not the arm head". The GREEN barer twin and the RED primary publish the **SAME** carrier — `rn=1 brt=0 body_root_op=54 tier=0`, op 54 = the **OUTER** `IR_MATCH_ALTERNATE` — so carrier selection cannot be the discriminator; and publishing the inner alternation is backwards (it is the **exhausted** node). Controls `( 'a' 'b' | 'aa' )` and `( 'aa' | 'a' ('b'|epsilon) )` are GREEN with the same carrier.
**⭐⭐⭐ THE MECHANISM (ASM-DIFF-FIRST step 2, no gdb).** `bb_match_alternate` carves a 32B choice record at α (`sub rsp,32`) and addresses it as a fixed offset from the **live rsp**: `[rsp+0]` cursor · `[rsp+8]` β-resume · `[rsp+16]` next-arm. When arm 1's tail is another choice box it carves its OWN 32B record and — correctly — does **not** pop on success (`n4_match_alternate_as: jmp n0_match_alternate_s0`, no `add rsp,32`), so the OUTER box's `s0` executes `mov [rsp+8],rax` **into the INNER record**, and the external β then reads `[rsp+8]`/`[rsp+0]`/`[rsp+16]` from that foreign cell. **A box's state address is computed from the live rsp, but its cell is wherever rsp was when IT carved.**
**THE PREDICTION, MEASURED (subj `'aa'`):** arm 1 succeeding WITHOUT a carve is GREEN — `( 'a' POS(1) | 'aa' )`, `( 'a' LEN(0) | 'aa' )`, `( 'a' RTAB(1) | 'aa' )`; WITH a carve is RED — `( 'a' ('b'|epsilon) | 'aa' )` nomatch and **`( 'a' ARBNO('z') | 'aa' )` SIGSEGV rc=139**. ⭐ The SIGSEGV is the SAME defect: `n0_match_alternate_β` is `jmp [rsp+8]`, so a record read at the wrong depth is an indirect jump through foreign bytes — wrong answer and wild jump are the benign and malignant faces of one mechanism.
**⛔ THE ROW IS TWO CLASSES, AND THE CANDIDATE 4th JOINS THE OTHER ONE.** `SCRIP_FENCE_IGNORE=1` splits the four **2/2**, disagreeing with the brief's bracketing and agreeing with the witnesses' own headers: **IN** (rsp-depth) = `ptw_min_alttop_nofence_ctl` (fence-free, publishes a carrier) + `ptw_min_fence_alttop` (fence refusal stacked on top; ignore the fence and the depth defect is what remains). **OUT** (pure s182/s121 fence refusal, already dispositioned) = `ptw_min_fence_left_altresume` + `ptw_min_rseal_unsealed_ctl` — **both cured by `FENCE_IGNORE=1`; the 4th is classified OUT with evidence, as the row asked.**
**⭐⭐⭐ THE CURE EXISTS AND ITS ADMISSION EXCLUDES EXACTLY THESE SHAPES.** `bb_match_alternate.cpp:38` `sn4_choice_rbp_off()` moves the record to a negative rbp base so *"every spelling below is depth-immune under arm-interior carves"* — this defect, named in the tree. `blob_choice_rbp_scan` (`emit.cpp:2488`) admits `if (_nc != 1 || _fn) return 0;`. The fence-free primary is `_nc=2` (*"the refused two-record-composition class (s126)"*); the other three are `_fn=1` (*"the FENCE semantic refusal"*). **MEASURED: `SCRIP_CHOICE_RBP=1` AND `=0` leave all four at `nomatch`** — the scan refuses before the killswitch is consulted, while the single-choice twin (the admitted class) is green.
**⛔ NEXT — NOT A PREDICATE EDIT.** `_nc>1` is refused because **two choice records must compose** in one blob frame: the rbp-resident record is a single 32B slot from `blob_frame_bytes`, so admitting two means one record per choice node, in the frame and in the template. And **NO-PER-OP-FILTER binds** — the cure must be a family property (*a choice record is addressed relative to the record, never to the live rsp*), never a widened admission list; the present predicate is already near the shape that rule forbids. Honest alternative: make the LEGACY rsp-carved path depth-correct (re-seat rsp at the arm's exit, or address through a saved base as `n3_match_fence1_β`'s `mov rsp,[rbp-48]` already does). FINDING: `FINDING-2026-08-20-s189-the-alt-choice-record-is-addressed-off-the-live-rsp-so-an-arm-interior-carve-moves-it.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s189 (seat6, Claude Opus 5; queue row `fuzz-diff-batch`, rank 2) — **THE BATCH IS TWO CLASSES, THE CAPTURES THE BRIEF NAMES ARE INERT IN BOTH, AND THE CLASS ALREADY HAS A NAMED CORPUS WITNESS THAT SIGSEGVs.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `2c8d2b34` then RE-PROVED IN FULL AT `3dc52576` after a mid-rung rebase moved SCRIP six commits — corpus `7aa87e81`, `make pristine` EXIT=0 before verdicts BOTH TIMES, RT_OPT `-O0`, NO COMPILER FILE TOUCHED; all 18 witnesses, the board, and both inline arms identical at both revisions):** corpus board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1 (337)** — seat4's s188 numbers exactly, fail-set identical **by name**. **DIRECTION (the row's promote-immediately clause): ALL SIX ARE FALSE REJECTS. ZERO FALSE ACCEPTS. NOTHING ESCALATED.** Every `.ref` re-derived from the LIVE oracle, and identical under **both** `sbl -b` and `sbl -bf` — so the rank-0 `scorecard-oracle-case` folding hazard does not touch this batch.
**⭐ `fz_diff_01` HAS HEALED** — green both modes at HEAD, stable 6/6. The row's "6 wrong-answer witnesses" is **5**. m3 ≡ m4 on all six.
**⭐⭐⭐ THE SPLIT (inline-vs-stored is the instrument).** All five are stored patterns falling to the `PAT$` blob (`sno_pat_inline_ok` has no case for BAL/FENCE/ARB/captures → `default: return 0`). Writing the same pattern INLINE splits them: **D-1** = `fz_diff_13` + `fz_diff_22`, inline is **correct and deterministic** (8/8) — faces of the existing `blob-resume-refusals` row and its s183 untiered-disjunction root, **not new defects**. **D-2** = `fz_diff_07` + `fz_diff_08` + `fz_diff_20`, inline **hangs (124) / SIGSEGVs (139) / is nondeterministic** — broken on BOTH paths, no existing row, and **strictly worse inline than stored**, so admitting these shapes to `sno_pat_inline_ok` would trade 3 wrong answers for 2 crashes and a coin flip.
**⛔ THE BRIEF'S TWO NAMED SHAPES ARE FALSIFIED.** `fz_diff_07` is not about `(*G0) $ v1`: drop the capture — still nomatch; inline `G0` so no defer remains — still nomatch. Minimum is `(BREAKX('abc') | FENCE('a'))` **unanchored**, needing BOTH arms (`('zz' | FENCE('a'))` and `(BREAKX('abc') | 'a')` both pass). `fz_diff_20` is not about the capture-of-a-capture: `(ARBNO(REM) BAL | '')` fails without any capture while `ARBNO(REM) BAL` passes. **The alternation is the ingredient, every time.** BAL is exonerated (extends correctly under `POS(0)…RPOS(0)`, even through a capture); its one real under-enumeration is driver-is-`FAIL` (`175_pat_bal_generator_retry`), a **different** mechanism — do not merge them.
**⭐⭐ THE CLASS ALREADY SCORES ON THE BOARD.** `crosscheck/patterns/160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume.sno` (`'aXb' ? ('a' ARB . V | 'q') 'b'`) is class D-2 written INLINE, is already in the standing fail-set, and **SIGSEGVs rc=139 in both modes** (stable 6/6). Curing D-2 should turn it green — the cheapest available proof a candidate cure is real.
**LANDED (corpus):** 12 witnesses in `corpus/probe/fuzz/` with live-oracle `.ref`s — **5 reduced RED** (`fzr_07_breakx_alt_fence`, `fzr_08_alt_nested_bal`, `fzr_13_alt_of_arbno`, `fzr_20_alt_arm_bal_extend`, `fzr_22_nested_arbno_not_first`) **+ 7 GREEN controls**, one per removed ingredient. The one-ingredient ladder, subject `'ab'`, stored: `(ANY('abc') | BAL)` match · `(ANY('abc') | (BAL))` match · **`(ANY('abc') | (BAL | ''))` nomatch** · `(ANY('abc') | ('ab' | ''))` match.
**⛔ HARNESS HAZARD FOUND EN ROUTE:** `'aa a' ((BREAKX('abc') | FENCE('a'))) $ v1 RPOS(0)` inline returns `match` / `[ZHP] heap exhausted` / SIGSEGV **from the same unmodified binary across consecutive runs**, both modes — a one-run board row on that shape is an arbitrary draw (the s186 OR-family class). Every verdict in this rung was re-run for stability; the 5 checked-in witnesses are stable 6/6.
**NEXT:** D-2 wants its own queue row — *a generator's implicit alternatives and an enclosing `ALTERNATE`'s arm dispatch do not compose, on both the blob and the statement path*; fix the statement path first (it crashes, and it is where any `sno_pat_inline_ok` widening would route these shapes). D-1 folds into `blob-resume-refusals` as two more witnesses, no more. FINDING: `FINDING-2026-08-20-s189-the-fuzz-diff-batch-is-two-classes-and-the-briefs-captures-are-not-the-ingredient.md`.

At `lon`'s post-s185 honest **12.5** the same arithmetic projects **37.7 → 38.9, +1.2**. ⛔ **A POST-s189 META IS NOT COMPARABLE TO A PRE-s189 ONE AND THE DIFFERENCE IS IN THE FLATTERING DIRECTION** — the board improved by no longer running its worst-scoring suite. **Declared weight total 118 → 113 and it STAYS short: `lon`'s 5 points are NOT redistributed (Lon's knob).** ⛔ Do not confuse the **declared** total with the **effective** `tw`, computed per board from suites with scoreable rows — META renormalises and is never "out of 118".

A seat budgeting ±5 PASSES of tolerance is discarding real single-program cures. The honest floor is ±5 rows of failure-class churn, ±0 passes.**

Both directions move rows in and out of the denominator without any tree change. That is seat2's own unlanded s186 guard plus a **second, opposite** direction they had not named.
**⛔⭐ THREE MECHANISMS IN ONE SESSION MOVED THIS BOARD'S HEADLINE WITH NO COMPILER CHANGE:** (1) **dropping a suite** — `lon` gone, **+0.7**; (2) **an oracle that times out** — an unpinned program becomes `ORACLE_FAIL` and **leaves the denominator**, and both such rows were *failing*, so `misc` rose 65.9→66.9, **+0.1** (an oracle that gets slower can only make the board look better); (3) **an oracle flag change** — **−0.3**, which seat2's 3-arm sweep resolves into 1 genuine SCRIP defect, 10 ground-truth artifacts and 5 flakes, **ZERO fail→pass board-wide**. HQ-73's *"UNSCR hides SCRIP reds"* is the general law and all three are instances. ⛔ **Never compare two METAs without first comparing their denominators, their oracle flags, and their builds.**
**⛔⭐ THE ONLY MECHANISM THAT MOVED META IS DIRECTIONAL AND IT FLATTERS — AND IT IS THIS ROW'S OWN SHAPE BY A SECOND ROAD.** `run_one` sets `have_live=1` only on oracle `rc -eq 0`, so a `timeout 60 sbl` failure makes an unpinned program `ORACLE_FAIL` and **it leaves the denominator**. Both such rows (`parser/cf_label_assign`, `parser/cf_loop`) were *failing*, so their departure raised `misc` 65.9 → 66.9 (N 123→121, UNSCR 24→26). **An oracle that gets slower makes the board look better; it can never lower a suite whose departing row was red.** Dropping `lon` raises META by removing a low-scoring **suite**; an oracle timeout raises a suite by removing a failing **row** — **two mechanisms in one session by which the headline improves because it measured LESS.** HQ-73's *"UNSCR hides SCRIP reds"* is the general law; never compare two METAs without comparing denominators first.
**⛔ A TRAP THAT VOIDED MY FIRST NEGATIVE TEST AND WILL VOID YOURS:** both the gate and the scorecard derive `S4E`/`SC` from **their own `dirname`**, so a control copy run from a scratchpad measures **nothing** while looking like it measured everything — the scorecard control printed `SKIP scrip not built` and **the gate printed `GATE GREEN` on zero programs**. Keep control copies in `SCRIP/scripts/`. ⭐ Independently: **that gate has no floor — it reports GREEN on an empty list.** Named, not fixed; wants row `argnote-gate-floor`.
**⛔ RESCUED EN ROUTE, AND IT WAS IN GIT NOWHERE:** an **uncommitted** `lower_snobol4.c` patch on the campaign's only FALSE ACCEPT was sitting dirty in this seat's tree (mtime 22:57 UTC), residue of the seat5 session HQ orphaned by **renaming** `arbno-nullalt-false-accept` → `arbno-tail-false-accept` five minutes after seat5 locked the old name. ⛔ **A renamed row bricks its holder — `next(1)` resumes an unfinished claim BEFORE scanning `QUEUE.tsv`, so the seat is pinned to a row that no longer exists, forever.** Pushed as `WIP-2026-08-20-s189-…-rescued-group-tail-resume-surface.patch`, routed to seat8, who replied with corrections now recorded **in that file's header**: its SEQ half is redundant with seat8's landed `a2979dc6`, its embedded gdb theory is the **wrong mechanism** (the IR arrives mis-wired, visible in `--dump-ir`, reproduced at `SCRIP_OPT=0` — **LOWERER, not emitter**), and only its **ALT half** (`case TT_ALT` :1796) is live, as row `alt-arm-resume-surface`. My own inference about `ptw_min_nullalt_retreat_falsereject` is **falsified and marked as such in place**.
**OFF-LIMITS DISCIPLINE HELD:** no file under `corpus/programs/lon/` was read, cat'd, grepped for content, or executed; **only path counts** appear anywhere. `lon-include-root` **confirmed still SUSPENDED** (claim = `seat1`, no `DONE`; row still at rank 3).
**NAMED NOT FIXED —** three more wildcard walkers, none a violation today (all default to safe roots, each becomes one if a caller widens it): `util_sweep_dyn89_parse_errors.sh` (root is **`$1`**, and it prints the parser's first error line — the *credential* half, not the execution half), `test_gate_zdp_on_null.sh` (`$CORPUS_DIRS`), `util_fc_spine_census.sh` (`$CORPUS`). ⭐ **THE GENERALISABLE MOVE: a directory is protected by name only against tooling that says its name — grep the corpus ROOTS that tooling walks, not the directory being protected.** Census: `grep -rn 'find .*\$CORPUS[" ]' scripts/*.sh`, then read each default root.
**NEXT / ROWS ASKED (not worked):** `rules-lon-propagation-fix` · `argnote-gate-floor` · `lon-weight-ruling` (Lon's call on the orphaned 5 points) · `q-scorecard-contention` (recommend: record loadavg + `--jobs` into the results dir and print them in the report header — a contended board is currently indistinguishable from a clean one by inspection of its output, the HQ-27 class exactly).

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s188 (seat4 `/home/claude4`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `fuzz-segv-batch`, rank 1) — **THE ELEVEN FUZZ SEGVs ARE FOUR MECHANISMS, AND IN TWO OF THEM THE ROAD IS AN INGREDIENT: THE IDENTICAL PATTERN TEXT IS GREEN INLINE AND CRASHES THROUGH A STORED PATTERN.**
**WATERMARK (`make pristine` at SCRIP `87ffd0b5`, RT_OPT `-O0`, tree clean, oracle `-bf` verified alive):** corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 SKIP 1 (337)** — the row's baseline **to the digit**, fail-set unchanged. **NO SCRIP SOURCE CHANGE; `.s` regen NOT APPLICABLE.** corpus `e18c3dec`, 22 new files in `corpus/probe/fuzz/`.
**⛔ RE-PROVED AFTER THE REBASE AT MERGED SCRIP `2cf31532` (SECOND `make pristine`), NOT ASSUMED — SCRIP had moved EIGHT commits, including seat3's `2c8d2b34` *"a DEFERRED THUNK MAY RETURN AN EXPRESSION"* which lands on the very defer road M1/M4 stand on:** all 11 originals **still rc=139** (none cured), all 11 reductions unchanged in their documented mode profiles, both breach witnesses still breaching in both directions, board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 SKIP 1** with the fail-set **IDENTICAL BY NAME**. ⭐ The classification is stated at `2cf31532`, not at the tree it was found on.
**FIRST DELIVERABLE ANSWERED — ALL 11 STILL RED AT HEAD, BOTH MODES (11/11 rc=139), all 11 `.ref`s re-verified live with ZERO drift.** One movement: **`fz_segv_02` is now rc=124 HANG in m3** (m4 + gdb still SEGV) — timing-bearing, not cured; a one-run verdict on it is an arbitrary draw.
**⭐ THE CLASSIFICATION — 11 WITNESSES → 4 MECHANISMS + 1 COMPOUND, every ingredient established by ABLATION (remove it and the program turns GREEN):** **M1 ARBNO RE-ENTERS A DEFERRED ELEMENT** (`fz_segv_14`, `24`, `03`arm2) · **M2 SEALED CAPTURED GENERATOR + FORCING RIGHT NEIGHBOUR** (`10`, `23`, `18`, `03`arm1 — **dropping the CAPTURE cures all four**; `.` and `$` identical) · **M3 `ARBNO(<null-first-arm ALT> <generator>)`** (`17`, no defer needed) · **M4 BLOB-ROAD FENCE/DEFER ADJACENCY** (`09`, `15`, `19`, `02` — **the identical text written inline is GREEN in all four**). ⛔ **`fz_segv_03` IS A COMPOUND AND ITS TWO ARMS CRASH INDEPENDENTLY** — one row per file loses one.
**⭐ THE SMALLEST CRASHER IN THE BATCH, TWO LINES:** `G0 = FENCE('')` / `'ab' ARBNO(*G0) RPOS(0)` → oracle **match**, SCRIP **SEGV both modes**. Every ingredient load-bearing: inline `ARBNO(FENCE(''))` GREEN · `G0 = ''` GREEN · no tail GREEN · empty subject GREEN · **defer depth 2 GREEN**, which is the OPPOSITE of `defer-depth-floor`'s signature and is why it is not that row.
**⛔ THE ROAD IS AN INGREDIENT — THE RESULT MOST LIKELY TO BE RE-DERIVED.** `ARBNO(*G0)` on `'a b c'`, inline vs through `P = ARBNO(*G0)`: `FENCE('')` SEGV/SEGV · `(BREAK(' ')|'')` **AGREE/SEGV** · `ARBNO(TAB(1))` **AGREE/SEGV** · `(LEN(1)|'')` AGREE/AGREE · `''` AGREE/AGREE. ⭐ **A probe grid without the stored-pattern road scores these clean — the mechanical reason s183's 168-combo hand grid scored 167 AGREE while the random walk found 27 red shapes: a generator varies the ROAD, a hand grid varies the PATTERN.**
**⛔ NOT DUPLICATES — ALL FIVE NAMED ROWS RUN AT HEAD, NOT ASSUMED:** `defer-depth-floor` rc=0 `match` · `arbno-tail-false-accept` rc=0 `nomatch` · `arb-immed-assign-retry` rc=0 `match` (all three cured) · `arbno-alt-fence-L1` still red but **rc=0, a wrong ANSWER with no crash** (M3 is its adjacent neighbour, not its duplicate) · `m1-composed-wild-jump` shares the wild-transfer signature but none of beauty's ingredients.
**⭐⭐ THE ONE-CURE QUESTION, ANSWERED BY MEASUREMENT: NO — SO THIS ROW CLOSES INVESTIGATION-ONLY.** Best candidate was seat8's routed `alt-arm-resume-surface`: their s187 `sno_seq_nary` correction applied at the twin site `lower_snobol4.c:1800` `case TT_ALT` (the `g->all[before]` they told the next seat to grep), behind an **opt-in** `SCRIP_ALT_TAIL` probe (default OFF, no cached state, **no new global**). ⭐ **IT CURES `160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume`** — the standing crosscheck red, rc=139 → rc=0 `V=[X]`, the live oracle's answer, **one line**. ⛔ **AND IT MOVES ZERO OF THE 11.** That zero is the load-bearing result: **the fuzz SEGV batch is NOT the ALT-arm resume-surface class** — the seal that matters in M1/M2/M4 is FENCE and the ARBNO instance boundary, separate resume-surface sites. **Probe REVERTED; handed to that row via `s4e_msg.sh ask`, not taken here.**
**TEN KILLSWITCHES INERT ON M1'S MINIMAL WITNESS** (`FENCE_IGNORE`, `FENCE0_WHACK`, `SPAN_FRAME`, `OPT`, `SEQ_TAIL`, `ARBNO_ALTSIB`, `PAT_INLINE`, `CAP_SEAMTIER`, `DEFER_XPAT`, `RTSEQ_RESUME`). ⭐ **`SCRIP_FENCE_IGNORE=1` not curing it is the sharpest fact in the batch: the defect is not in the fence VERDICT but in the fence WIRING.** `SCRIP_OPT=0` reproduces ⇒ at or below LOWER.
**⛔ THE 1:1 INVARIANT BREAKS IN BOTH DIRECTIONS INSIDE ONE THREE-TOKEN SHAPE, and one ingredient flips it** — `fz_red_m2_breach_m3only` (m3 SEGV / **m4 correct**) and `fz_red_m2_breach_m4only` (**m3 correct** / m4 SEGV), checked in for this alone. ⭐ **Mode agreement is therefore NOT evidence of correctness on this class**; my own first reductions of `10`/`23` silently changed class this way and were re-minted to both-mode forms.
**ALSO SEEN, ROUTED NOT WORKED:** `FENCE('a' ARB . V) 'b'` on `'aXb'` → **SIGABRT rc=134** — the M2 shape with the seal spelled FENCE instead of `|` reaches the **`fuzz-abort-batch`** class; that row should read M2 first rather than re-reduce.
**NEXT, PRECISELY:** ASM-DIFF the one-token pair `ARBNO(FENCE(''))` (GREEN) vs `ARBNO(*G0)` (SEGV) — RULES' ASM-DIFF-FIRST has rarely had a cleaner witness pair. ⛔ Not the monitor, not `SCRIP_ZSM_ALL`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s188 (seat4 `/home/claude4`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `gimpel-drivers-D`, rank 4) — **INCLUDING ONE FILE TWICE HANGS SCRIP IN BOTH MODES WITH NO OUTPUT AND NO DIAGNOSTIC, AND THE `*` IN A CAPTURE TARGET IS WHAT BYPASSES `lower_snobol4`'S OWN REFUSAL.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `6d4cab2d`, `make pristine` before every verdict — TWICE, once at `cecb7d11` and again after a mid-session rebase moved SCRIP five commits; tree clean, RT_OPT `-O0`, NO COMPILER FILE TOUCHED):** corpus board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1 (337)** at BOTH revisions — the brief's numbers exactly, fail-set identical **by name**. A no-op by construction: the board enumerates `crosscheck` + `beauty_suite` + demos and never reaches `programs/gimpel` or `probe/`.
**LANDED (corpus `1a8d6a9e`):** batch D's 25 manifest rows → **25 drivers** (`<NAME>_driver.sno` + live-oracle `.ref`, cases from each module's HEADER COMMENT). **6 GREEN both modes** (`CH COUNT DECOMB FORTPUT FORTREAD HEX`) · **1 green in exactly ONE mode** (`LPERM`) · **18 RED, checked in red per law 0d** and named in the FINDING.
**⭐ THE HEADLINE — `-INCLUDE "X.sno"` TWICE NEVER TERMINATES.** rc=124, zero bytes on stdout AND stderr, m3 and m4 alike; the oracle is **include-once** and answers normally; the one-include control is GREEN. Not exotic — it is the ordinary "name every module you use" habit: `MINP` includes `SPACING` **and** `COUNT` while `SPACING` includes `COUNT`, so MINP hangs; `LINE` reaches `REVERSE` three ways and `COUNT` three ways; `FRSORT` doubles `COUNT` and `SEQ`; `HYPHENAT` doubles `BALREV`. Witness/control: `probe/gimpel/gim_double_include_hang{,_A,_once_control}`.
**⭐⭐ THE SECOND CLASS — A NON-SIMPLE CAPTURE TARGET IS REFUSED LOUDLY WITHOUT THE `*` AND GOES SILENTLY INERT WITH IT.** `CRACK` returns an array of the **right size with every element null**; its whole mechanism is `PAT = BREAK(B) . *CRACK<I> LEN(1)`. Ablated four ways: `. V` **correct** · `. *V` **correct** · `. A<I>` **LOUD** (*capture target in a runtime-built pattern is not a simple variable*) · `. *A<I>` and `. *$N` **SILENT, rc=0, no diagnostic, both modes**. ⛔ A fix aimed at "deferred capture targets" aims at the wrong half — `. *V` is already right; the refusal must follow the NON-SIMPLE target through the defer. Batch A's `EVAL`/`CONVERT` lesson on a new mechanism: **a feature declared unlanded must fail on EVERY path that reaches it.**
**⭐ THIRD CLASS — THE DIRECT GOTO `:<expr>` (TRANSFER TO A CODE OBJECT) MISREADS RATHER THAN REFUSES:** `:<G()>` is a **parse error**; `:<L>` is read as the **literal label name `L`** where both oracles say `ERROR 024 -- goto operand in direct goto is not code`. Kills `FPROFILE` (via `LPROG`) and `CATA` (via `SEQ`); neighbourhood of the already-red `216_indirect_goto_computed`.
**⭐⭐ AND THE RE-MEASUREMENT PAID FOR ITSELF — `FORTPUT` WAS AN m4-ONLY SIGSEGV AND THE SPAN-FRAME FLIP CURED IT.** At `cecb7d11`: m3 rc=0 correct 8/8 · **m4 SIGSEGV 16/16** (ASLR on and off) from a `.s` **BYTE-IDENTICAL across compiles**, so codegen divergence was ruled out by construction. ## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s188 (seat1 `/home/claude1`, Claude Opus 5; the `-b`/`-bf` ORACLE-FLAG row, successor to `lon-include-root`) — **THE ORACLE FLAG IS A LANGUAGE SWITCH; `-b` MANUFACTURES THE SIGSEGVS IT IS BLAMED FOR; AND beauty's ORACLE IS NONDETERMINISTIC UNDER THE FLAG 13 OF 14 SUITES USE.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `213771e2`, corpus `66259281`, both trees clean, RT_OPT `-O0`; NO compiler source and NO corpus program touched):** one new script + one FINDING. RULES step-4 `.s` regen NOT APPLICABLE. ⛔ `corpus/programs/lon/` never enumerated, run, or compiled — the new tool carries TWO independent locks (suite unlisted + per-path guard), which is why this was measurable at all while `scorecard_snobol4.sh` stays quarantined.
**⭐ THE MANUAL SETTLES IT, NOT TASTE.** `-b` (l.6943) suppresses the sign-on banner and does NOTHING else; `-f` (l.6945) is the CASE-FOLDING switch; folding is the DEFAULT (l.1205, l.7049) and reaches names, labels (l.7531), `$` indirect reference (l.7743) and `&CASE` (l.7891). **l.11074-11077 is decisive: `-f` is what you use "if compatibility with standard SNOBOL4 is desired."** RULES.md declares SCRIP IS CASE-SENSITIVE, so `-bf` is the conformance arm. ⭐ **The tree already knew and never propagated it:** `util_run_beauty_oracle.sh:61` and `test_monitor_3way_sync_step_auto.sh:205` both say `-f` = case-sensitive; only the SCORING half runs `-b`.
**⭐⭐⭐ THE HEADLINE — beauty.sno has NO STABLE ORACLE under `-b`.** Three runs each: `-b` → rc 139 / 139 / **231**, three different md5s, 1081 bytes of error listing; `-bf` → rc 0 / 0 / 0, **md5 `6f1671c07577` identical, 40971 bytes.** Under `-b` beauty dies in an `ERROR 217 duplicate label` storm at 559/561/563… on `visit_1`/`visitEnd` — camelCase labels that collide ONLY WHEN FOLDED — then cores. **SPITBOL cores in ERROR RECOVERY and folding manufactures the errors** (2-line witness `endlbl.sno`: `ERROR 215` + rc=139 under `-b`, clean rc=1 under `-bf`). ⛔ **THREE documents in this tree blame the environment for it** — CLAUDE.md/RULES.md ("plain `-b` SIGSEGVs after 34 lines"), `board_beauty_m1.sh:18`, and `cmp3_snobol4.sh:31` ("benign sandbox segfault-on-exit"). It is the flag, not the sandbox and not beauty.
**MEASURED, 1786 programs × 12 suites (`scripts/util_oracle_flag_sweep.sh`, NEW):** `same` 1683 · **MOVER 77** · FLAKY 26. Oracle health per arm — **SIGSEGV 19 (`-b`) vs 12 (`-bf`)**, ERROR-exit 7 vs 2, refused 168 vs 216 (the +48 is §8's dialect). ⛔ **THE CONTROL ARM IS NOT CEREMONY:** the naive 2-arm form reported **103** movers; 3 arms report **77 + 26 FLAKY** — ~26 timing-bearing/runaway programs disagreeing with THEMSELVES. A flag sweep without a same-flag control arm reports its own noise as signal.
**⛔ THE FLIP'S HONEST COST, AND MOST OF IT IS A SCORING DEFECT.** Replaying `grade()` over all 77 movers: **m3 49 FAIL→FAIL · 15 PASS→PASS · 13 PASS→FAIL · ZERO FAIL→PASS** (m4 31/20 NOBUILD/15/**11**, also zero gains). The 13 split: **8 are VACUOUS passes** — SCRIP emits 0 bytes, `-b` emits 0 bytes rc=0, so `grade()` says PASS **while each has a pinned `.ref` with real content** (`preload1.sno`'s whole source is the line `end`; its pin says `aa`). ⭐ **The scorecard awards PASS for MUTUAL SILENCE against a pin that says otherwise** — a grading defect independent of the flag. The other **5 are curable** by §6 below.
**⭐⭐ A REAL SPITBOL BUG, 3 LINES: `-f` SILENTLY DISABLES LOWERCASE `-include`.** `-include`→**ERROR 022** under `-bf`, `-INCLUDE`→works, `-Include`→**ERROR 022**. Contradicts manual **l.7311** ("Controls may be specified in upper- or lower-case, **regardless of the current state of case-folding**") and fails SILENTLY (unrecognized controls are ignored). Census (lon excluded): **973 `-INCLUDE` vs 35 `-include`; exactly 18 files use ONLY lowercase** (4 `crosscheck/library`, 4 `SCRIP/test/…/library`, 9 `csnobol4-suite`, 1 `gimpel/INFINIP.sno`). ⭐ **Independently confirms seat5's s186 census that exactly ONE gimpel module uses that spelling.** Uppercasing costs nothing under `-b` and cures all 5 real regressions.
**TWO CHECKED-IN TESTS THE LIVE `-b` ORACLE GRADES WRONG** (pin + SCRIP agree, `-b` dissents): `crosscheck/rung2/210_indirect_ref.sno` (`bal` folds onto the PROTECTED built-in `BAL` → `ERROR 042`) and `crosscheck/patterns/127_pat_json_keyvalue.sno` (lowercase `s` and uppercase `S` become ONE variable, leaking the subject). Both survive today only because `grade()` accepts pin OR live. ⭐ **seat5's "BAL is oracle-incompatible" call SURVIVES the flip** — `gimpel/BAL.sno` names its function in UPPERCASE, so its clash is real under both arms. Confirmed, not contradicted.
**TWO SCRIP DEFECTS FOUND, both needing NO flag ruling:** (1) ⛔ **SCRIP DOES NOT REQUIRE AN `END` STATEMENT** — `noend.sno` runs and exits 0 where **BOTH** arms say `rc=1 "No END statement found"`. The lexer (`snobol4.lex.c:1466`) is correctly `strcmp(…,"END")`; SCRIP simply runs off the end. **Own row.** (2) ⛔ **`&CASE` REPORTS 1 WHILE SCRIP BEHAVES AS 0** — `-b`→`CASE=1`, `-bf`→`CASE=0`, SCRIP→`CASE=1`, while SCRIP demonstrably does not fold. These `kw` probes are the ONLY genuine head-to-head evidence for `-b` in the whole sweep, and they dissolve into a SCRIP bug.
**⛔ WHAT NEEDS LON, NOT A SEAT — THE LOWERCASE DIALECT.** **53 of 77 movers are `csnobol4_suite`**: a wholesale lowercase dialect (`output`, `size`, `&alphabet`, terminator `end`) that DEPENDS on folding. **SCRIP cannot pass them under either arm** (`alph.sno` → `** Error 5 Undefined function or operation`, because lowercase `size` is not `SIZE` — exactly what a case-sensitive engine must do). Under `-b` they are graded against an answer SCRIP is FORBIDDEN to produce; under `-bf` they have no oracle. The flag only changes the LABEL (FAIL vs UNSCR) — **and UNSCR moves META, which is Lon's knob (HQ-73).** Principled disposition = seat5's treatment of the 3 oracle-incompatible gimpel modules: mark out-of-dialect, stop scoring. **Not attempted; escalated.**
**⭐ PREREQUISITE DISCOVERED: queue row `datatype-case` (rank 34) CANNOT BE ADJUDICATED BEFORE THIS RULING.** 4-line witness: `DATA('jobj(a,b)')` → `DATATYPE` answers **`JOBJ`** under `-b`, **`jobj`** under `-bf`, and **SCRIP answers `JOBJ`**. SCRIP's uppercase answer is a bug IFF the reference is `-bf`. Same mechanism is the single `demos` mover (`json.sno` `root=JOBJ` vs `root=jobj`).
**NEXT:** (1) Lon's ruling on §8 dialect + the flip itself; (2) then flip `scorecard_snobol4.sh:80` (deleting the `beauty_self` special case — it is not special, it is the only suite graded correctly) plus `board_sno15_{perf,perf2,ident}`, `board_sno_apps`, `test_bench_snobol4_timed`, `cmp3_snobol4`, `test_demo_descent_sweep`; (3) uppercase the 16 `-include` control words in the 14 non-`include*.sno` files; (4) fix the vacuous-PASS rule; (5) correct the three "SIGSEGV workaround" texts. ⛔ None of the five requires running a `programs/lon` program.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s188-the-oracle-flag-is-a-language-switch-and-b-manufactures-the-crashes-it-is-blamed-for.md`
**⭐⭐⭐ LON'S RULING, EXECUTED IN-SESSION (in-chat s188): *"We fix all the programs by uppercasing the keywords."*** §8's dialect is REPAIRED, not dropped. **34 programs / 35 files uppercased and EACH INDIVIDUALLY VERIFIED** (15 == pinned `.ref`; 18 == pre-edit `-b` output with a pin that matches NEITHER arm, pre-existing breakage reported not papered over; 1 unpinned). ⛔ **THE TRAP A CORPUS-WIDE sed WALKS INTO IS NOT HYPOTHETICAL — IT BREAKS beauty.sno.** A naive pass changes **197** files and corrupts the programs that already work: `beauty.sno` uses `Integer` and `tab` as USER GRAMMAR NONTERMINALS (uppercasing collides them with built-in `INTEGER()`/`TAB()`), and `210_indirect_ref.sno` deliberately names a variable `bal` which must not become the protected `BAL`. **THE DIALECT GUARD — the pinned `.ref` is the ARBITER of which dialect a program is written in:** pin == `-bf` answer ⇒ CASE-SENSITIVE dialect, NEVER touched; clean under `-bf` ⇒ not broken; else folding dialect ⇒ uppercase the program AND its transitive `-INCLUDE` closure, ACCEPT only if `-bf` afterwards reproduces the pin (or the pre-edit `-b` output where the pin is independently broken), else AUTO-REVERT. The guard reproduced mechanically the 4 case-sensitive programs I had identified by hand. **NEVER touched:** string-literal contents, `*` comment lines, `;*` tails, every user-defined name. **39 NOT FIXED, named with reasons** (13 still differ, 11 still rc!=0, 8 already all-caps so their difference is `&CASE` or a name minted from a STRING LITERAL by `DATA`/`DEFINE` — the `datatype-case` mechanism, 7 ambiguous); all reverted, none half-edited.
**⭐⭐ THE PAYOFF, STASH A/B ON THE 33 PINNED EDITED PROGRAMS: SCRIP 0/33 → 12/33, IN BOTH MODES, m3 ≡ m4 (12/12 both).** Before, every one failed (`alph.sno`: `** Error 5 Undefined function or operation`, lowercase `size` is not `SIZE`); after, 12 match their pins byte-for-byte. The rows this cursor called unwinnable-by-construction are now ordinary passes, and the residue is honest red against real references. **Sweep delta on the repaired tree: movers 77 → 42 BY NAME over the 1786-program intersection, 36 cured, ZERO genuine new ones.** ⛔ The raw diff shows one apparent new mover (`smoke/empty_string.sno`) — **chased down, FALSE ALARM**: untouched by this session and nondeterministic under BOTH arms (4 runs → 4 md5s under `-b`). ⭐ **LIMITATION FOR ANY SEAT REUSING THE TOOL: the control arm is a PROBABILISTIC filter, not a proof** — a nondeterministic program slips through as MOVER when its two control runs coincide, so a mover set is an UPPER bound (the mirror of seat7's s184 note that a single-run two-arm sweep is a LOWER bound).
**GATE RECEIPT after the 35 corpus edits: corpus board m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1 (337) — watermark to the digit, fail-set unchanged.** A no-op by construction (the board never enumerates `csnobol4-suite`/`gimpel`), which is what makes it the right proof. SCRIP tree clean ⇒ NO `.s` regen debt.
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s188 (seat2 `/home/claude2`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `arb-immed-assign-retry`, rank 1) — **THE `$` BLOB-RESUME REFUSAL WAS WRITTEN DOWN AS "UNMEASURED" AND THE MEASUREMENT INVERTS ITS REASONING. THE BRIEF'S CLASS NAME IS FALSIFIED.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `9d811427`, `make pristine`, tree clean, RT_OPT `-O0`):** corpus board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1**, fail-set identical **by name**. Smoke **7/7 both modes** (m4 hard gate) · medium-invisible **0** · emit_no_lang **LANG-BLIND OK**. RULES step-4 regen: benchmark/feature/demo `changed=0`; programs **emitted=623 changed=0**.
**LANDED (SCRIP `9d811427`, ONE case label in `src/contracts/zeta_depth.c` + the stale s180 comment corrected in place; corpus `0e33b7cb`, 5 probe pairs).**
**⭐ THE BRIEF'S CLASS NAME IS WRONG AND ITS OWN WITNESS'S TWIN FALSIFIES IT.** Not *"an immediate assignment ON a retrying generator"*: the identical pattern written **INLINE** (`'a+aa' POS(0) ARB $ v RPOS(0)`) has always been GREEN. **`*E` is not the ingredient either** — reference `E` **BARE** and it is equally red. The ingredient is that the pattern is **BUILT AS A VALUE** and matched through the compiled `PAT$n` blob. ⛔ And the brief's second control proves less than it looks: `ptw_min_span_immed_ctl`'s `.ref` is **`nomatch`**, so oracle and SCRIP agree only in *refusing* and `v` is never printed — a positive control was minted beside it.
**⭐⭐ ROOT CAUSE — s187'S SHAPE, ONE FILE OVER.** `zdp_seam_tier()` (`zeta_depth.c:38`) left `IR_MATCH_ASSIGN_IMM` untiered, so `resume_carrier_ok` refused it and the blob published `PAT$N_β: jmp PAT$N_ω` — the retreat died at the `$` node and never reached the generator. s180 cured this for the `.` twin and **wrote down why it left `$` behind**: *"ASSIGN_IMM stays 0: unmeasured, and its side effect fires DURING the match (undo is not a pure record pop)."* **That sentence IS the defect** — as s187's `dtx_used` cap was (*"mirrors the old body EXACTLY"*). A candid TODO decays into a ruling by being read.
**⭐⭐⭐ THE MEASUREMENT INVERTS IT.** Emitted asm: `imm_β = jmp n5_match_arb_β` **BARE** vs `cond_β = sub r12,24; jmp n5_match_arb_β`. **IMM is a STRICT SUBSET of the shape s180 blessed.** Manual **p.87**: `$` commits *"whenever a subpattern matches, **even if the entire pattern match ultimately fails**"* — the side effect being committed is why there is **no undo to get wrong**. The manual's OWN example is this pattern (`'A' ARB $ OUTPUT 'E'` → null/B/BC/BCD/Success — ARB extends and re-fires every retry); stored, SCRIP printed the null string then `Failure`. **p.123–124** closes the escape hatch: SPITBOL **deleted** the quickscan heuristics, matching is exhaustive, `&FULLSCAN` may only be non-zero.
**THE CURE IS A FACT-RULE REPAIR:** `bb_match_capture` owns **COND, IMM and SAVE** — one mechanism, two phases — so admitting COND while refusing IMM was the **NO-PER-OP-FILTER** shape. Family now uniform, rides the existing `SCRIP_CAP_SEAMTIER` killswitch, **adds no state** (no global, no new env knob).
**BOUNDARY, 18 PROBES × 2 MODES, ALL GREEN, `m3 ≡ m4` THROUGHOUT** (so no m4 row is owed): stored+ARBNO/BAL/ALT-arms, `$` **off** the generator (`ARB ('a' $ v)`), `*(inline)`, literal forcing tail — every red fell to the one line. ⭐ **The two greens are load-bearing:** `E RTAB(0)` is tier 0 *and green* (nothing forces a retry — which is what proves tier 0 only manifests as a retreat refusal), and `E = ARB` + `E $ v` at the **use** site is green (the `$` compiles into the statement graph, not the blob).
**⛔ ONE HONEST NON-CLOSURE, MEASURED AND ROUTED, NOT FOLDED IN — WANTS A ROW.** **`IR_MATCH_ATP` (the `@` cursor operator) is untiered in the same switch and red by the same symptom** (`E = ARB @v` → oracle `match4`, SCRIP `nomatch`; inline twin GREEN). Checked in red per law 0d (`ptw_min_atp_stored_red` + `ptw_min_atp_inline_ctl`). **Deliberately not folded in: ATP owns `bb_match_atp.cpp`, a separate template ⇒ a separate box family**, and the FACT RULE closes the family that shares a mechanism (`bb_match_capture` is now closed). **Measured so the next seat inherits it: ATP's inline β is `add rsp,16; jmp n4_match_arb_β`, a self-undo fail-through jumping LEFTWARD — the tier-2 definition verbatim and the same shape as `ASSIGN_SAVE`'s β. The cure is one case label.**
**⛔ ALSO SEEN, NOT MINE, DO NOT MINT A THIRD WITNESS:** `IR_MATCH_ALTERNATE` is untiered too (`E = ARB ('x'|'a')` stored → red, tier 0) — that is s183's **`blob-resume-refusals` untiered disjunction**, already routed; standing board reds `160_pat_alt_inner_gen_resume` / `175_pat_bal_generator_retry` sit in that lane.
**⭐ THE GENERALISABLE MOVE:** **a refusal documenting itself as "unmeasured" is a TODO, not a ruling.** Two sessions running the blocker was a candid comment explaining why half a mechanism was left out. **When a comment justifies a limitation by anything but a measurement — "mirrors the old body", "unmeasured" — it is a LEAD, not a wall: go measure it.** Both times it took minutes and inverted the stated reasoning. **Corollary:** a control whose expected output is a *failure* proves only agreement in refusing; where a mechanism's job is to produce a value, at least one control must show the value produced.
**⭐ RE-PROVED AFTER THE REBASE, NOT ASSUMED:** the push brought in two pattern-machinery commits beneath this one — seat4's `cecb7d11` ARBNO-ALTSIB and seat3's `213771e2` ONE AUTHORITY (the duplicate `DT_X` chain drain, s187's own file) — so every number was re-measured after a second `make pristine` at merged SCRIP **`9d811427`**: board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1** fail-set identical **by name**, smoke **7/7 both modes**, medium-invisible **0**, emit_no_lang **LANG-BLIND OK**, and all 8 checked-in probe pairs verdict-identical (the `atp` pair red as checked in, its inline control green), `m3 ≡ m4` on every one.
**NEXT:** route `atp-seam-tier` (one case label, β already measured). FINDING: `FINDING-2026-08-20-s188-the-immediate-assignment-refusal-was-written-down-as-unmeasured-and-the-measurement-inverts-it.md`.
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s184 (seat4, Opus 5; queue row `arbno-alt-fence-L1`) — **THE FENCE WAS NOT LOAD-BEARING. s126 MADE THE ARBNO RETREAT EDGE A CHOICE-NODE *COUNT* ON A CONVICTION MEASURED AGAINST **NESTED** ALTERNATIONS, AND **SIBLING** ONES WERE SWEPT UP WITH THEM.**
**WATERMARK:** corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11**, fail-set **byte-identical across the killswitch A/B** · passthru board **m3 140/157 · m4 127/157** (was 136 · 123 — **+4 both modes, removals only, zero new reds**) · blast radius **0 movers / 1282** mode-4 `.s` (only the known self-differing `unary_not.sno`) · all five step-4 regens `No changes` · pristine before every verdict.
**⭐ THE SYMPTOM IN ONE PROGRAM (`ptw_min_arbno_altsib_ladder`):** `ARBNO((LEN(1)|LEN(2)) (LEN(1)|LEN(2)))` matched **every EVEN prefix length and NO odd one**, where the oracle matches every length ≥ 2. Per-iteration widths are {2,3,3,4} — every even total is reachable with all-first-arms iterations and **every odd total needs exactly one iteration to take a non-first arm**. The generator was exploring **only the all-first-arms path**. The same body **without** the ARBNO backtracks correctly, so wrapping it in ARBNO is what loses the retry.
**⭐ THE ASM DIFF NAMED IT, gdb never needed:** at `n0_match_arbno_af`, GREEN emits `cmp r14d,eax; jne <body β>` and RED emits `cmp r14d,eax; jmp PAT$0_ω` — **the conditional retreat edge is absent and the `cmp` sets flags nothing reads**. `SCRIP_RESUME_WHY=1` reports the SAME `body_root_op=57 tier=1 in_nodes=1` for green and red, so the carrier publication was never the problem; **edge emission** was.
**⭐ THE CAUSE:** `emit.cpp:1192` admits the edge only for **EXACTLY ONE** choice node (`_alt==1`). s126's own comment records the conviction — nf3 `ARBNO(FENCE('a'|'ab')|'c')`, where the fence blocks the flatten and the admitted edge **livelocks at rc=124**. That is a **NESTED** pair. The IR distinguishes the two outright, since `MATCH_ALTERNATE`'s operands are its arm delimiters: siblings carry `[7,7,8,8]` and `[5,5,6,6]` (disjoint) while nf3's outer carries `[33,33,36,36]` where **node 33 IS the inner ALTERNATE**.
**⭐ THE CURE — ARBNO-ALTSIB (SCRIP `cecb7d11`, killswitch `SCRIP_ARBNO_ALTSIB=0`):** admit `_alt>=1` **when no span choice node sits inside another choice node's arm operands**. Structural containment, never an op-name blacklist and never a per-op filter. **Zero new globals.** ⛔ The unguarded `_alt>=1` was tried FIRST and MEASURED WRONG — it cures the ladder **and** reproduces nf3's rc=124 livelock verbatim; the nesting guard is the difference between the two shapes, not a precaution. **s126's conviction is preserved: nf1–nf4 all PASS, nf3 rc=0 `nomatch`.**
**⛔ THE ROW'S OWN WITNESS IS NOT CURED, AND EXACTLY WHY:** `ptw_min_arbno_alt_fence_L1` keeps its FENCE; a FENCE'd alternation is **sealed**, which sets `_ref=1` and **vetoes the edge outright** even though its UNSEALED sibling `(LEN(1)|LEN(2))` is a valid carrier the manual requires be retried (p.125/p.204 makes alternatives *inside P* invisible, and says nothing about a sibling to its left). Compounding it, `sn4_arbno_tailbeta()` aims the edge at the span **TAIL** — which here *is* the fenced ALT. **NEXT RUNG, PRECISELY:** a sealed choice node must not be re-entered itself but must not veto the edge when an unsealed sibling exists, and the edge should then aim at the rightmost **UNSEALED** choice node. That is a retarget, not a relaxation, and was deliberately not attempted.
**ALSO FOUND, NOT FILED:** `ARBNO(LEN(1) FENCE(LEN(1)))` **hangs at rc=124** where the oracle answers `nomatch`.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s184-s126-counted-choice-nodes-and-swept-sibling-alternations-in-with-nested-ones.md`. **Witnesses:** `corpus/probe/passthru/ptw_min_arbno_altsib_*` (corpus `5d72325f`).

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s187 (seat2 `/home/claude2`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `defer-depth-floor`, rank 0) — **THE DEFER-DEPTH FLOOR IS FIXED AND IT WAS NOT BEAUTY'S FIRST BLOCKER. A DOCUMENTED ONE-SHOT `DT_X` RESOLUTION CAPPED EVERY DEFER CHAIN AT ONE LINK.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `fccc81cc`, PRISTINE rebuild, tree clean, RT_OPT `-O0`):** corpus board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1**, fail-set identical **by name** to the control arm — the board never enumerates `probe/`, so a no-op there is the correct result and it is what proves no regression. Smoke 7/7 both modes (m4 hard gate) · medium-invisible 0 · emit_no_lang LANG-BLIND OK.
**LANDED (SCRIP `144330e8`/`fccc81cc`, 3 lines + one corrected comment in `src/runtime/pattern_match.c`; NO codegen file touched, emitted `.s` BYTE-IDENTICAL control vs patched, so the `.s` regens are a provable no-op).**
**⭐ THE ROOT CAUSE, AND IT WAS WRITTEN DOWN RATHER THAN HIDDEN.** A deferred EXPRESSION (`DT_X`, what `P = *Q` builds via `SNO$MKEXPR`) was resolved **exactly once** per defer activation; when that evaluation yielded ANOTHER `DT_X`, the second was stored unresolved and `c_rt_defer_close` `-1`'d it. `pattern_match.c:911` states the cap verbatim — *"at most one DT_X-triggered call (`dtx_used`); a second DT_X result is stored, not re-called"* — and justifies it as *"mirrors the old body EXACTLY"*. ⛔ **That phrase IS the defect: the discipline was validated against SCRIP's previous implementation, not the oracle** — while the SAME comment block quotes manual p.85-86 saying a `DT_X` evaluation **may itself run a match**, the very recursion `dtx_used` forbade. A limitation was frozen into a contract by being copied forward.
**⭐⭐ THE BOUNDARY, MEASURED THREE WAYS.** Ladder on plain-string · `ARBNO(LEN(1))` · Lon's `ARBNO(LEN(1)|LEN(2)|LEN(3))` terminals: **depth 0/1 GREEN, depth 2+ RED, identically in all three.** The terminal type is irrelevant — which is what proves the mechanism is the defer road and not any pattern family. Fixed on **BOTH roads** (either alone leaves half the class red): `rt_defer_take` (whose callers at `:1046`/`:1057` set `dtx_used=1` *before* entry, so the guard was already false and the second `DT_X` fell straight to `s->val`) and `rt_defer_xpat_dtp`. **BOUNDED, not `while`: the oracle HANGS forever on `V = *V`** (killed at 8s), so a faithful `while` inherits the hang; `RT_XPAT_CHAIN_MAX 256` is ~14× beauty's deepest chain (`Expr0..Expr17`) and on a cycle terminates back into the exact pre-s187 answer. No new global — a `#define` has no storage, `_g` is a local.
**A/B, swapping `out/libscrip_rt.so` only:** DONE-WHEN witnesses (HQ's s183 family) `floor pp_L2 pp_L3 defer2_arbno depth9` all **nomatch/nomatch → match/match in BOTH modes**; `pp_L0`/`pp_L1`/`defer1_arbno_ctl` controls **unchanged green**. `board_passthru_combo` **m3 134→139/148 · m4 121→125/148, movers named, ZERO regressions in either mode.** `SCRIP_RTSEQ_RESUME=0` green throughout (never resume-sequencing); `SCRIP_DEFER_XPAT=0` returns `pp_L2`/`depth9` to nomatch — that switch's documented job — **while `floor` stays green under it**, the cleanest proof the cure landed on both roads.
**⛔⛔ THE ORIENTING CORRECTION — THE ROW DOES NOT SUBSUME THE M1 ROWS.** `board_beauty_m1.sh --modes m3` is **UNCHANGED: 3/10 green, first red still at 10 lines.** Beauty's first red is the **s183 CLASS A completion SEGV** — all four 1–9 byte witnesses (`m1_lad_empty` = a lone `"\n"`, `barelabel`, `end`, `comment`) still SIGSEGV rc=139 on the patched build. The brief's premise (beauty's grammar is a deep defer chain, so nothing in it could ever have matched) is **true about the grammar and does not move the board**: the depth floor sat BEHIND a wall beauty never reaches. The s183 override sweep (`*Stmt` two levels SEGVs, `*Comment` one level clean) was real evidence of a real defect — **this** one — but of a second, independent blocker, not the first. **NEXT: route class A (the completion/output-path SEGV) on its own; it is now the sole standing M1 first-red.**
**⛔ ONE HONEST NON-CLOSURE.** `ptw_min_defer2_evalbuilt` m4 stays red with a **NEW and FURTHER first-red**: it advanced from `nomatch` (the defer wall) to `** Error 22 … Undefined function called` — an EVAL-built `DEFINE` present in the m3 image but absent from the mode-4 binary. A 1:1 breach in the m4 direction on a **different** mechanism, uncovered *because* the defer wall moved; not a regression (red in both modes before), not this row's class. Worth its own row.
FINDING: `FINDING-2026-08-20-s187-the-defer-depth-floor-was-a-one-shot-dtx-resolution-and-beauty-dies-earlier.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s186 (seat5 `/home/claude5`, Claude Opus 5; queue row 4 `gimpel-drivers-A`) — **GIMPEL BATCH A IS IN: 25 DRIVERS, NINE DEFECT CLASSES, AND THE s183 BLANKS SILENT WRONG ANSWER BOTTOMS OUT IN A FIVE-LINE MODULE.**

**⭐⭐ THE HEADLINE — BLANKS WAS NEVER THE HARD SHAPE.** HQ read s183's `BLANKS('X = A')` → `X = A` wrong answer as recursive `ARBNO` through a defer + `FENCE`-in-`ARBNO` + a deferred call with args. **It is none of those.** BLANKS strips blanks by calling `DIFF(S,' ')`, and `DIFF.sno` is FIVE LINES; `DIFF_driver` is red on **all five** contract cases (SCRIP removes NOTHING). Ablated to five lines with **no function, no ARBNO, no defer**: `S='ABCDEF' ; X='BDF' ; X = SPAN(X) ; S X = ; OUTPUT = S` → oracle `ACDEF`, SCRIP `ABCDEF`. **The ingredient is the SELF-REFERENCE and a committed GREEN CONTROL proves it** — bind the same SPAN to a *different* variable and SCRIP is correct. `X = SPAN(X)` is the ordinary SNOBOL4 "make this argument a matcher in place" idiom and it goes inert **silently**. Pair: `probe/gimpel/gim_span_self_rebind_{wrong,control}`.
**NINE CLASSES, seven minimized to ≤6 lines in `probe/gimpel/` with oracle refs.** `X = SPAN(X)` inert · `SPAN` over a PARAMETER matched against the return variable (`ACDEF`→`CDEF`) · **omitted LEADING argument shifts args left**, `F(, 'y')` puts `y` in `A` (trailing omission is correct) · `~` negation = IR op=3 no template (**FATAL**) · deferred `TAB(*R)` = IR op=82 no template (**FATAL**) · trailing-dot real literal `25.` (**parse error, no code generated**) · **`EVAL` of a `CONVERT`ed expression ALWAYS SUCCEEDS** · ASM360 field mis-split · runtime re-`DEFINE` to a second entry label (heap runaway). ⛔ **Cite the CONSTRUCT, never the op number** — `54b6c478` made the enum alphabetical.
**⛔ `EVAL` IS LOUD ON ONE PATH AND SILENTLY WRONG ON ANOTHER.** FLD and DEXP get an honest *"EVAL and CODE are outside the landed subset"* refusal; FIND routes through `EVAL(CONVERT(PRED '(MAX,TEST)','EXPRESSION'))` and gets an **answer** — always success, so `FIND` returns `1` for every array and every predicate against oracle `2/3/2/3`. **A feature declared unlanded must fail on EVERY path that reaches it.** **FIVE of the nine classes are silent wrong answers.**
**⛔⭐ THE MISS THAT ALMOST SHIPPED — HQ-27 IS NOT CEREMONY.** The first full pass ran on the `scrip` in the tree: built 2026-08-19 21:18, **52 commits stale**, including the s183 RT-CARRIER cure (`0b7b8d29`/`943e404a`) — the exact machinery these witnesses land in. **Nothing failed.** It produced a complete, internally consistent, entirely plausible board. Caught only because a number disagreed with a document. After `make pristine`: **the board did not move** (same 10/12/3) but **two "core dumps" became SILENT WRONG ANSWERS** (SPAN-over-parameter, ASM360) and one op number moved 90→82 — i.e. two classes were mis-prioritised, since a crash is self-reporting and a wrong answer is not. ⭐ **The rule, stronger than "run pristine before a gate": a stale build is INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A CURRENT ONE BY INSPECTION OF ITS OUTPUT, so when a measurement disagrees with a checked-in figure, SUSPECT THE BUILD BEFORE THE DOCUMENT.** Here the doc was right twice and this seat first read it exactly backwards, publishing "the brief's baseline is stale" — **RETRACTED, kept visible in the FINDING.**
**⭐ METHOD NOTE THAT WILL SAVE BATCHES B–F A FALSE RED:** a driver can be contaminated by a defect in a construct it uses only INCIDENTALLY. The first `LAST_driver`/`COPYL_driver` built their lists with `LINK(, 'B')` — the omitted-leading-argument defect — and both came back red on a defect belonging to NEITHER module; rewritten with an explicit null variable, **LAST went green** and COPYL's own runaway became visible underneath. Ablate the DRIVER's scaffolding before naming the MODULE red, and build scaffolding from constructs already proven green in the batch. Also: `DUMP` is a SPITBOL **system function** (`ERROR 248`) — never name a helper that.
**⛔ FOR HQ — `DRIVER-MANIFEST.tsv` MISCLASSIFIES EXACTLY ONE MODULE, AND IT IS IN BATCH A:** `INFINIP` is classed `n_include=0` but carries `-include "INFINIP_lib.sno"` in **lowercase**, which the census regex missed. Censused: exactly **1** module uses that spelling; the other 77 use `-INCLUDE` and are correct. **Batches B–F need no reclassification.**
**THE 3 ORACLE-INCOMPATIBLE MODULES SHIP WITHOUT A `.ref`, DELIBERATELY** (SPITBOL refuses them under `-b` AND `-bf`; hand-authoring is forbidden): **BAL** `ERROR 042` — its function name collides with SPITBOL's built-in protected `BAL` pattern · **FTRACE** `ERROR 248` — `OPSYN('DEFINE','FTRACE')` over the protected `DEFINE`, which is the module's whole mechanism · **INFINIP** `ERROR 156` via `REDEFINE.sno`. ⭐ The INFINIP driver still earned its keep: bare, the module dies at *"No END statement found"* (lowercase `end` LABEL, no `END` STATEMENT); the driver supplies the `END` and advanced the failure to the real incompatibility.
**NEXT:** rows `gimpel-drivers-B..F` (B is the next self-contained batch) and `gimpel-suite-harness`. The two unminimized classes (ASM360 field mis-split, COPYL runtime re-`DEFINE` runaway) are owed witnesses.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s186 (seat2 `/home/claude2`, Claude Opus 5; queue row `gimpel-drivers-B`) — **A STORE THROUGH THE NAME OF A DATA FIELD SILENTLY DOES NOTHING, AND THE 1:1 LAW IS BREACHED IN BOTH DIRECTIONS INSIDE ONE 25-MODULE BATCH.**
**WATERMARK (SCRIP `8d7c5917`, tree clean, RT_OPT `-O0`, NO COMPILER FILE TOUCHED):** corpus board **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1** — the brief's numbers exactly, fail-set identical **by name**. **LANDED:** batch B's 25 manifest rows → **24 drivers** (`<NAME>_driver.sno` + live-oracle `.ref`, cases from each module's HEADER COMMENT, `sbl -b` throughout, four carrying `<NAME>_driver.in` per the scorecard's own `stdin_for()` convention). **9 GREEN both modes** (`LIKE LPAD PERMUTAT PLI_STMT PUSH READ READRL RESOLUTI REVERSE`) · **15 RED, checked in red per law 0d and named in the FINDING.**
**⭐ THE HEADLINE — `$P = v` OVER `P = .R(X)` IS A SILENT NO-OP.** 9-line witness `probe/gimpel/gim_name_of_field_store.sno`: the field keeps its old value, the same idiom over a **plain variable** is correct, so the ingredient is **the field name**. Two silent wrong answers fall out of it — `LINEARIZE` truncates its chain after 2 of 6 nodes, `READL` returns an empty list — and **neither errors at the point of loss**. ⛔ **`PUSH_driver` is GREEN and that is the discriminator:** `PUSH` returns `.VALUE(…)` by `NRETURN` and assignment *through the returned name* works. **Name-return-to-a-field is right; `$NAME =` indirect-store-to-a-field is wrong.** A fix aimed at "names of fields" generally aims at the wrong half.
**⭐⭐ M1 EVIDENCE FROM A 1971 DATE ROUTINE.** `gim_defer_pred_in_pattern_segv.sno` (8 lines): a pattern carrying `$` immediate assignment **plus a deferred predicate call WITH ARGUMENTS** (`*GT(DY,X)`) over the just-assigned variable SIGSEGVs. Distilled it dies in both modes; inside `MDY.sno` it dies in **m3 while m4 runs the whole driver correctly**. Same family as beauty's `nInc() *Expr5 FENCE(…)` and BLANKS's `LEN(*DIFF(N,' '))` — on code that cannot have encoded a theory of the bug.
**⛔ THE 1:1 LAW IS BREACHED BOTH WAYS IN ONE BATCH:** `MDY` m3 SIG11 / m4 **PASS** · `REORDER` m3 **PASS** / m4 SIG11 · `ORDER` m3 wrong answer / m4 SIG11.
**⛔ TWO HONEST NON-CLOSURES, RECORDED SO THEY ARE NOT RE-DERIVED.** (1) `ORBREAK` is **nondeterministic** — same binary, same input: m3 `DIFF/SIG6/DIFF`, m4 `SIG11/SIG11/SIG6`, the SIG6 arm being `[ZHP] heap exhausted (512 MB)`; when it completes, only the **last** alternative survives the `ORBREAK = ORBREAK C` accumulation (`L` for `LLAL`). The obvious 6-line accumulator repro is **GREEN both modes**, so the trigger is more specific than self-accumulation. (2) `gim_break_value_drops_prefix` is real (`ORDER('bab')` → SCRIP `bb`, oracle `abb`, a 3-char input) with a green sibling, but **size is a correlate, not a proven cause** — 158 chars carved from `&ALPHABET` fails, a 25-char literal passes, yet a **26**-char carve passed as a function argument also fails; the deferred-`LEN` bisect probe hit (1)'s heap exhaustion instead.
**⛔ TWO CLASSES ARE BATCH A's, NOT MINE.** seat1 landed `gim_omitted_arg_shift` + `gim_real_literal_parse` (corpus `09967f5c`) mid-flight; batch B reached both independently (`REVL.sno`'s `LINK(,'d')`; `RANDOM.sno`'s `414971.` recurrence). My near-duplicates were **deleted** — theirs carry a control arm mine lacked.
**⛔ NOT DEFECTS, DO NOT SCORE THEM AS ONE:** `PHRASES.sno` is **not a module** — a BNF grammar data file, same bytes as `PHRASES.IN` modulo CRLF, read by nothing, and `sbl` **SIGSEGVs** on it; it gets no driver and wants renaming out of the `-name *.sno` sweep. `MFREAD.sno` **cannot load under the live oracle at all** (`OPSYN('REWIND.','REWIND')` → `ERROR 248`), so its driver ships **without a `.ref`, deliberately** — hand-authoring one is forbidden.
**⛔ A HARNESS DEFECT FOR ROW `gimpel-suite-harness`, MEASURED NOT GUESSED: `sbl` EXITS 0 AFTER A FATAL ERROR.** `scorecard_snobol4.sh` sets `have_live=1` on `rc -eq 0`, so every program whose oracle dies at runtime is graded against a **SPITBOL error dump** carrying `execution time msec` / `memory used` — an unstable "ground truth", scored `DIFF` (reads as a SCRIP defect) instead of `ORACLE_FAIL`. Two-line guard: also require stdout not to match `^[^ ]+\([0-9,]+\) : ERROR [0-9]+ --`. Not changed from this row — it is that row's file.
**NEXT:** the `$NAME =`-to-a-field store is the cheapest real fix in the batch (9-line witness, green control, two silent wrong answers riding on it); then `REORDER`'s m3/m4 split (16-line driver, 10-line module, no witness yet). `FINDING-2026-08-20-s186-gimpel-batch-b-and-the-name-of-a-field-store-that-silently-does-nothing.md`

Class A is where the lanes part: m3's death is the known wild jump (`_rtld_global` then `#1 0x0`, s182 addendum 1 / row `m1-composed-wild-jump`); **m4 never reaches it.**
**⛔⭐ THE m4-ONLY CLASS, NAMED — `m4-eval-fragment-reallocates-unsealed-cells`.** AB fn-cell census AT THE FAULT, both modes: m3 **408 cells, 393 sealed, 15 STUB** (every STUB an `alpha$EXPR$*` thunk; `alpha$Shift`/`alpha$Reduce`/`alpha$nTop` **sealed** at slots 73/75/129) · m4 **13 cells, 0 sealed, 13 STUB** — including all three main-image procs. **In m4 the seal happens in the COMPILER process and is thrown away; the emitted binary's own runtime fragment compiler then re-allocates the same names and finds them unsealed.** Two receipts: (a) the 172,891-line m4 `.s` contains `alpha$` **0**, `fn_cell` **0**, `rt_ab_undef_fn_stub` **0** — nothing is baked; (b) one breakpoint gives the whole road — `eval_thunks_emit_from` (`runtime_eval.c:249`) → `emit_chain` → `bb_call_proc_staged.cpp:341` → `bb_ab_fn_cell_ptr` → `bb_ab_slot_for` (`emit.cpp:1472` initialises to the stub), while compiling beauty's own grammar rule verbatim: `epsilon . *Reduce('ExprList', *(GT(nTop(), 1) nTop()))`. ⛔ The arrival is by JUMP, so there is **no honest caller frame** — frame #1 resolves to `g_call_args` in `.bss`, which is stack residue; do not read it as a location.

**⭐ THE FINDING (`FINDING-2026-08-20-s186-gimpel-batch-C-is-nine-green-and-fifteen-red-and-three-modules-the-spitbol-oracle-cannot-run.md`).** 24 new `<NAME>_driver.sno` + oracle `.ref` (BLANKS was HQ's) covering all 25 batch-C modules, every case written from the module's header contract. GREEN both modes: `ROMAN ROTATER RPAD SPELL STRINGOU SWAP SYSTEM UPLO BNORM`. RED, checked in red per law 0d: `SEQ SSORT SUBSTR TEMP TEST TICTACTO TREE TSORT AI AOPA BRKREM SQRT TUPLE ARC ASM`.
**⛔ THREE m3 ≢ m4 DIVERGENCES — the 1:1 invariant is broken on this batch:** `SSORT` **SIG11 vs PASS** (m4 gets the right answer, m3 dies), `TEST` RC1 vs COMPILE_FAIL, `ASM` SIG6 vs COMPILE_FAIL.
**⛔⛔ THE CONVENTION NEEDS AN AMENDMENT BEFORE BATCHES D–F.** `SQRT.sno` and `TUPLE.sno` draw Spitbol **ERROR 248 — attempted redefinition of system function** (`SQRT`, `LOAD`); `ASM.sno` draws **ERROR 160** (four-argument `OUTPUT()`). **`sbl` RETURNS rc=0 ON ALL THREE**, so a refgen that trusts the exit status pins the ERROR DUMP as the `.ref` — and that dump carries `execution time msec` / `memory used (bytes)`, i.e. a pin that is **non-deterministic as well as wrong**. Refgen must grep for `ERROR <n> --` / `execution time msec` and refuse, never trust rc. Cure used: those three refs come from **CSNOBOL4** (`snobol4 -b`), the other sanctioned oracle, which has no built-in collision and takes the 4-arg `OUTPUT()`; each driver header says so and why. `ARC_driver` inherits it (ARC includes SQRT).
**⭐ TWO NEW PROBES, each shipping its PASSING SIBLINGS in the same file** (`corpus/probe/gimpel/`): `gim_indirect_read_third_operand` — an indirect read `$N` whose cell exists only from a prior indirect store goes NULL when `N` is also the FIRST operand of the same three-operand concat; needs a **run-time-built name** (one literal mention of the name masks it), the exact shape `N <lit> $N` with `$N` last of three, and **nothing reading `N` earlier** (a bare `M = N` above the store masks it) — **and it CASCADES**: run it first and every later indirect read of `$N` goes null too, including the five shapes correct in isolation. Identical m3/m4, `SCRIP_OPT=0`, `SCRIP_RTSEQ_RESUME=0`. · `gim_seq_code_loop_in_function` — a function reaching its loop variable through a NAME ARGUMENT while driving a `CODE()` statement by `:<ARG_S>` SIGSEGVs on the way out; **not "CODE is unimplemented"** — the probe proves each half correct in the same run. Takes `AI` and `AOPA` with it.
**⛔ DELIBERATELY NOT COMMITTED TWICE:** batch C independently distilled the trailing-dot real-literal parse failure (`1.` `4.`, why `SQRT`/`ARC` do not compile at all) — already landed as batch B's `gim_real_literal_parse`; and `SSORT`'s `*LGT(T,S)` is already `gim_defer_pred_in_pattern_segv`. What batch B did NOT cover is kept as `gim_real_print_precision`: `2 ** 0.5` prints `1.4142135623730951` against the oracle's `1.4142135623731`, with integer and exact-value controls beside it — it needs no trailing-dot literal and survives that parse gap closing.
**⭐ THE POINT FOR THE M1 CLAIM, STATED HONESTLY.** `SSORT`/`TREE`/`BRKREM`/`TICTACTO`/`BLANKS` are pattern-engine reds and are **plausibly — not measured here** — the defer family; the obvious next test is the s183 defer-depth floor. But **four reds are not pattern reds at all**: a parser gap (trailing-dot reals), a printing gap (real precision), a **missing built-in** (`SQRT` is a Spitbol built-in; `OUTPUT = SQRT(4)` raises `Error 5` — no driver covers this any more, so it is recorded here), and a **built-in semantics** gap (`SUBSTR('ABC',2,5)` returns `BC` and SUCCEEDS where Spitbol FAILS). `TEMP`'s is a fifth and its ingredient list is a storage/liveness shape, not a Byrd-box shape. **The value of driving a library nobody wrote for SCRIP is that a third of its reds are nowhere near the wall we already know about.**
**NEXT IN THIS LANE:** batches D–F (rows `gimpel-drivers-D/E/F`) + `gimpel-suite-harness`. Two corpus facts they will meet: `TUPLE.sno` is the only batch-C module with **no header comment at all** (the manifest's `contract_line` is empty exactly when this happens — its cases came from `ASM.sno`'s machine-M opcode table, driven through the published signature only), and `RPAD.sno`/`SUBSTR.sno` **comment their own `DEFINE` out** because they are Spitbol built-ins, so their drivers necessarily drive the built-in — which is where the `SUBSTR` defect lives.
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s188 (seat3, Opus 5; queue row `m1-class-b-stmt-parse-error`) — **CLASS B IS CURED AND IT WAS ONE MISSING ARM: A DEFERRED THUNK MAY RETURN AN EXPRESSION, AND THE STAR ARM COULD NOT SEE ONE. THE PATTERN WAS FOUND AND THROWN AWAY ONE LINE LATER.**
**WATERMARK — PRISTINE, ON THE REBASED TREE** (`make pristine`, RT_OPT `-O0`, SCRIP `2c8d2b34` — i.e. AFTER seat8's false-accept `a2979dc6`, seat7's SPAN-FRAME flip `d3251f23` and seat1's `6d4cab2d` landed under this seat; corpus `1f3f3637`): broad corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 SKIP 1** (the standing watermark) · crosscheck **m3 312/5 · m4 308/8 SKIP 1 DIVERGE 3** · Icon 4/0 · Prolog fail-set identical by name · all five RULES step-4 regen scripts **ZERO `.s` movers** (runtime-only) · `test_gate_emit_no_lang.sh` OK · BOTH-MEDIUM ratchet **0** · **zero new globals**.
**⭐ ROOT CAUSE, THREE TRACE LINES, IDENTICAL IN beauty AND IN A 10-LINE STANDALONE.** `get_pat_dtp STAR nm=*EXPR$162 thunk.v=DT_X thunk.s=EXPR$77` → `run_all STAR-park nm=*EXPR$162 park.v=DT_X final.v=DT_P close=-1`. `rt_defer_get_pat_dtp`'s **STAR arm had a `DT_P` arm and NO `DT_X` arm** (its by-name sibling ten lines below has had one since s178). A thunk whose result is an EXPRESSION was PARKED and NULL returned; the site fell to the **SCALAR** road, which resolved it correctly to a **genuine PATTERN** and handed that PATTERN to `c_rt_defer_close` — a closer that can only literal-match a scalar and therefore `-1`s it. beauty's object expression is reached exactly this way, so `*Expr` silently failed and every real statement answered `Parse Error`.
**⭐ THE MANUAL NAMES THE VARIABLE — IT IS THE DATATYPE, NOT THE SPELLING OR THE DEPTH.** v3.7 **p.196**: *"The unevaluated expression operator must be at the outermost level to create an object of type EXPRESSION."* Four-row A/B in beauty: `Expr = *Expr0` (EXPRESSION) → `Parse Error`; `epsilon *Expr0` · `FENCE(*Expr0)` · `*Expr0 | *Expr0` (all PATTERN) → **CURED**. The last is the sharpest: an alternation of a pattern with *itself* changes the semantics not at all and the datatype entirely.
**CURE + KILLSWITCH:** the STAR arm drains the `DT_X` chain before its `DT_P` test, through **`rt_dtx_drain` — a new ONE AUTHORITY** extracted from the two sites that had each spelled the loop (`rt_defer_take`, `rt_defer_xpat_dtp`) and given to the third that was missing it, so the fix REMOVES a spelling instead of adding one. `DT_FAIL`(0x68) is not `DT_X`(0x58), so a failed hop exits the walk and every caller's own `IS_FAIL` test still sees it. Evaluation count on the scalar road is UNCHANGED (the inner hop `run_all` used to make is the one now made in the drain). **`SCRIP_DEFER_XSTAR=0` restores the pre-fix arm and the ENTIRE 337-program × 2-mode broad-corpus log is BYTE-IDENTICAL across it — zero blast radius.**
**⭐ FIRST STANDALONE WITNESS THIS CLASS HAS EVER HAD, AND WHY NINE BUILD-UPS WERE GREEN.** `corpus/probe/m1/m1_defer_x_thunk` (red pre-fix BOTH modes, green after) + `m1_defer_x_thunk_ctl` (one datatype token apart, green throughout). The missing ingredient was the **OPSYN'd binary operator**: `lower_snobol4.c:1437` lowers `*V` with a plain `TT_VAR` inner to the **by-name** road, and only the `sno_expr_collect` fallback at `:1448` mints `*EXPR$n`. beauty reaches it because `semantic.inc:7-8` OPSYNs `~`/`&` into the grammar-build expression. **The bug lived on the one road no minimal witness had ever taken.**
**⛔ RETRACTED FROM THE s186 CURSOR — DO NOT CARRY IT FORWARD: THERE IS NO HEISENBUG.** The tracer that "also cured" class B (`@ea (epsilon $ *TR(...)) *Expr0 @eb (...)`) is a **CONCATENATION** — it flips `Expr` from EXPRESSION to PATTERN. It is the datatype A/B in instrument clothing, which is also why a tracer on `Command` (outside `Expr`) never perturbed.
**⛔ AN HONEST NEGATIVE: THE M1 BOARD DOES NOT MOVE.** `board_beauty_m1.sh --modes m3` reads **3/10, first red at 10**, byte-identical across the killswitch — beauty already SEGVs (class A) at the 10-line rung, above where class B lives. The brief asked for the rung count to improve; it does not. What moved is the four class-B witnesses (full file, small input): they no longer print `Parse Error` and now reach the **class-A SEGV of row `m1-composed-wild-jump`** — exactly what the brief predicted curing B would expose. The four class-A witnesses are SEGV on both arms, untouched.
**⛔ HARNESS FACT WORTH THE NEXT SEAT'S TIME: THE PROLOG CROSSCHECK SELF-DIFFS.** Two consecutive runs of ONE binary gave `PASS=110 FAIL=1 SKIP=78` and `PASS=109 FAIL=1 SKIP=79`, the float entirely inside the `NATIVE-ABORT … native emit unimplemented` bucket (one row reporting `rc=132` vs `rc=139` between runs). **Self-diff the control arm before reading a one-row Prolog delta as a regression.**
**NEXT:** class B is closed; **re-test row `m1-composed-wild-jump` FIRST** — its `*Stmt` reduction runs on the very road this rung fixed, and its brief's "no standalone witness is possible" claim deserves a re-try now that the star road resolves EXPRESSIONs. Still open, unaffected: `m1_trailing_ws` (front end rejects a statement with trailing whitespace and no goto field; beauty has zero such lines) and `rt_patv_defer_open`'s dead `DT_X` arm (callers pre-set `dtx_used`) — its own rung, witness first.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s184 (seat8 `/home/claude8`, Claude Opus 5; queue row 13 `pat-eval-double-fn-arbno`) — **ALL THREE STANDING CROSSCHECK DIVERGE ROWS ARE ONE UNSEALED CELL. THE m4 STARTUP BAKE SEALS NO AB fn_cell AT ALL, SO A MAIN-IMAGE CALLEE'S `alpha$<FN>` STILL HOLDS `rt_ab_undef_fn_stub`.**
**WATERMARK (pristine at SCRIP `ffbc1425`, RT_OPT `-O0`):** SNOBOL4 crosscheck **m3 312/5 · m4 308/8 · SKIP 1 · DIVERGE 3** — `expr_eval`, `140_pat_eval_double_fn_trick`, `141_pat_eval_double_fn_arbno`. The row was locked as `DIVERGE=1`; it is **3**, and the three are ONE root cause.
**⭐ ROOT CAUSE, MEASURED AT ONE BREAKPOINT IN BOTH MODES:** m4 registers proc *names* (`rt_define_site`) and GVA names (`gva_register@PLT`) and **seals no cell**. At `rt_proc_call_open` on `b1c_e_plain`: m3 `alpha$PC` = `0x7fffee000014` (its pool-resident `PC_α`, sealed by the driver's R-1 s94 loop) · m4 `alpha$PC` = `rt_ab_undef_fn_stub` — which *is* error 22 (`rt.c:511`). `PC_α` sits in the ELF and nothing ever writes it into the cell.
**⭐ THE CURE IS ONE WRITE, PRE-MEASURED WITHOUT TOUCHING THE COMPILER:** poking `alpha$<FN>` = `&<FN>_α` from gdb cures **all four** witnesses to byte-identical `.ref` output — `b1c_e_plain` (`PC ran`/`match`), `140` (`stk=B`), `141` (`out=e`), and `expr_eval` (`7/9/25.5/7/26`). `expr_eval` is what makes it a class: it presents as **SIGSEGV, not Error 22**, has four procs, and its EVAL is *arithmetic*, not a pattern — and it still falls to the same seal. Expected payoff: **DIVERGE 3 → 0**, m4 **308 → 311**, and the **m3 ≡ m4** invariant breached since s169 restored.
**⛔ TWO s170 EXPECTATIONS CORRECTED, SO THE NEXT SEAT DOES NOT INHERIT THEM:** (1) the `[SEAL] MISS` diagnostic is **not** the discriminator — `SCRIP_SEAL_DIAG=1` prints the *same two misses in both modes* (`alpha$EXPR$0F1`, `alpha$PAT$0`) and m3 passes anyway; the cell that matters is the **main-image callee's**, which the diagnostic never names. (2) §5b's "sealing the cell makes it SEGV" **no longer holds** at `ffbc1425` — sealing now completes the match cleanly. Re-measure §5b, do not inherit it.
**⛔ FACE 2 IS DORMANT, NOT FIXED — AN HONEST NEGATIVE:** the intermittent load-dependent m3 `rc=139` did not reproduce **once** in **>1600 runs** across **two trees** (HEAD *and* s170's own `3a4ca273`) and four load/layout models, including **three full real `util_out_sweep.sh` runs** (583 rows each, identical md5 `027851e5…`, never `RUN_RC_139`) and an 82-point address-layout perturbation. I cannot name the landing that closed it, so it is recorded DORMANT.
**⭐ PROVEN ONE CLASS ANYWAY (the row's actual question):** both faces enter `rt_defer_get_pat_dtp("*EXPR$0F1")` → `rt_call_proc_descr("EXPR$0F1")` on the same thunk — m4 dies **outbound** (callee entry absent → stub), disarmed m3 dies on the **return leg** (`rip = 0x1`, after `PC ran`). And the discriminator holds both ways: `b1c_e_plain` (EVAL-built) is **rc=139 under `SCRIP_B1C_PARITY=0` and under `SCRIP_EVAL_RETAIN=0`**, while its main-built twin `b1c_m_plain` is **rc=0 under both**. Ten other killswitches are inert on `141`. **One crossing, two legs, two media.**
**⛔ THE ROW'S SHAPE DESCRIPTION IS WRONG: ARBNO IS NOT LOAD-BEARING.** `140` has no ARBNO — plain concatenation of two EVAL-built patterns — and fails m4 identically. The class is `eval + deferred call to a main-image proc`.
**NEXT:** the seal rung. Site is `bb_define_bind` (`src/templates/bb_define.cpp:396`) beside its `rt_define_site` call — ⛔ but **not** a re-use of that call's existing lea: `rt_define_site` carries the *body* entry (`n3_statement_begin_α`), while the cell needs the *alpha face* (`PC_α`, the separate prologue block that jumps to the body).
