## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s184 (seat3, Opus 5; queue row 2 `zsm-all-perturbation`) — **THE INSTRUMENT DEFECT IS CURED AND IT WAS THE s179 CURE ITSELF: `call_bare`'s BINARY TRAMPOLINE IS `movabs rax / call rax`, SO THE EVENT DESTROYED rax AT THE CALL SITE. ZSM-ALL IS NOW PROVEN TRANSPARENT; LON'S AUTOMATIC BUG FINDER IS BACK ON THE M1 HUNT.**
**WATERMARK (cured build):** corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11 · SKIP 1** — the s183 watermark exactly, m3 fail-set identical by name · `.s` blast radius on the DEFAULT arm **0 movers / 286 programs** with the hold-the-arm-fixed control also **0** · all five RULES step-4 regen scripts `changed=0` (623+22 programs) · BOTH-MEDIUM bb_*.cpp ratchet **0**, `test_gate_emit_no_lang.sh` OK.
**⭐ THE DEFECT, IN ONE SENTENCE:** s179 moved the ZSM event off `x86("call")` to escape the veneer's GLOBAL `rtccb` bank — correctly — but the veneer was doing TWO jobs, and the second one was saving **rax** across its own call stub; `x86_call_ro`'s bare BINARY stub is `48 B8 <imm64> / FF D0` = `movabs rax,ptr ; call rax`, which kills rax **in the caller's instruction stream, before `rt_zdp_ev`'s own `push rax` can bank it**. The residual perturbation was never "elsewhere" — **it was inside the s179 cure**.
**⭐ CURE (`x86_asm.h`, `x86_zsm_ev`, +5 −3, one file):** bank rax at the call site, **twice**, so the eight-push `rsp mod-16` parity is unchanged and the rdx addend moves with the pushes (64L → 80L), per that function's own header law. Pure `x86(...)` (TEMPLATE-ONLY), no `MEDIUM_*` conjunct (BOTH-MEDIUM), **zero new globals**, no per-op filter. Default arm unreachable ⇒ byte-identical by construction AND by measurement.
**⭐ MEASURED, NOT ARGUED — 896-PROGRAM SWEEP, DEFAULT vs `SCRIP_ZSM=1 SCRIP_ZSM_ALL=1`:** **87 → 23** divergent, and the class that matters, **silent wrong answers, 65 → 0**. The 22 survivors are the SAME 22 `rt_bomb` rows as before (the instrument's own verdicts, not perturbation); the 23rd, `cf_goto_computed.sno`, **self-diverges at a FIXED arm with no instrument at all** (`rc=133,139,132,139` across four default runs) and is noise in both arms. Beauty on `m1_min.in`: **bit-identical, rc=139 both arms**; beauty **self-host** (`beauty.sno < beauty.sno`, 259 bytes, md5 `e883e4b862ba`): **IDENTICAL both arms**. DONE-WHEN branch 1 met; the fence-it-off branch was not needed and `util_autobug.sh` needed no banner edit.
**⛔ THREE NEGATIVES KEPT SO THEY ARE NOT RE-DERIVED:** (1) **The row's own hypothesis is FALSE** — the witness's TEXT `.s`, normalised with the 158 event blocks removed by shape, diffs **0 lines** against the default: no GVA arm, no optimizer arm, nothing `MONITOR_BIN`-like. (2) **Not the stack** — a tunable guard moved every byte the block writes (and the C frame under it) down **128 / 512 / 4096 / 65536 / 1048576** bytes: wrong at every depth, 1 MB included. (3) **Not the sink** — `rt_zdp_ev` is hand asm that saves rax, rflags, r8–r11, rdi/rsi/rdx/rcx, rbp; the block **minus only the call** is CORRECT. ⭐ AND THE GENERALISABLE LESSON: **an asm diff is only evidence about the medium it was taken in** — TEXT emits `call …@PLT` and touches no register, so mode 4 could not have shown this defect at all.
**NEXT, IN ORDER:** (1) **the 22 BOMB rows** — now the only thing between ZSM-ALL and a trustworthy census; several convict PASSING programs (smallest: `probe/b2c/b2c_eval_pat_release.sno`, 129 bytes, oracle `match`, killed by `ZSM β … FRAME LOST`), and s179's ruling says an instrument that convicts passing programs cannot testify about failing ones — each is either a real law-0b violation or a further over-reach, and that is a row of its own; (2) re-run `util_autobug.sh` on beauty on the cured instrument and watch the divergence step move (s183 seat1's owed item, now unblocked); (3) the three sibling instruments (`rt_zdp_anchor`/`origin`/`probe`) are safe from THIS defect (`x86_rtcc_clob` → `RTCC_C_ALL` banks all nine GPRs) but still carry the s179 global-`rtccb` reentrancy hazard, untouched here.
**FINDING:** `FINDING-2026-08-20-s184-the-zsm-all-perturbation-is-the-bare-calls-own-rax-trampoline.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s184 (seat4, Opus 5; queue row 1 `m1-composed-wild-jump`) — **THE STORED-PATTERN MEMO COMPARED A DANGLING FRAGMENT-AST POINTER AGAINST ITSELF. ONE MEMO HAD THE SALT GUARD AND ITS TWIN NEVER RECEIVED IT — s183'S SHAPE, ONE FUNCTION OVER. BEAUTY STILL SIGSEGVs; THE ROW IS NOT FINISHED.**
**WATERMARK:** corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11**, fail-set **byte-identical across the killswitch A/B**, re-proven at the post-rebase HEAD `30a705de` · passthru board **m3 130/139 · m4 117/139** (was 127/139 m3 — **+3, pure cure, removals only**) · blast radius **0 movers / 1254** mode-4 `.s` (the 1 differing row self-differs in the control arm; all five RULES step-4 regens report `No changes`) · pristine build rc=0 before every verdict.
**⭐ THE DEFECT, IN ONE SENTENCE:** `sno_pat_collect` (`lower_snobol4.c:1929`) memoised stored patterns with `sno_expr_eq` **alone**, while its twin `sno_expr_collect` (`:86`) has guarded the same memo with `salt == g_sno_expr_salt` since the `EXPR$<n>F<salt>` naming landed — so a `g_sno_pats[]` row's `pat` is a **DANGLING pointer into an EVAL fragment AST `ast_tree_free_dyn` already released**, the arena hands the 5th fragment the 1st's addresses, and `sno_expr_eq`'s opening `a == b` declares two unrelated patterns identical **without reading one field**.
**⭐ MEASURED, NOT INFERRED:** on `ptw_min_epsshift8` fragments 5..8 arrive at `0x43e180/0x43dd70/0x43e0b0/0x43f300` — byte-for-byte the four pointers rows 0..3 hold — `npat` FREEZES at 4, and the match-time capture records read `*EXPR$0F1..*EXPR$3F4` **twice**. Every layer below the memo was already correct: 8 distinct `EXPR$kF<salt>`, 8 distinct `OPQ$k`, 8 distinct DTPs, 8 textually distinct EVAL cache keys (all miss, all compile fresh).
**⭐ THE CURE — PATSALT (SCRIP `30a705de`):** the salt guard transplanted verbatim + a `salt` field on the EXISTING `g_sno_pats[]` row. **Zero new globals** (row widening = the `g_sno_seal` `val` precedent; killswitch = function-local `static`, the `rt_defer_xpat_on` construction). Static patterns are all collected at salt 0, so the guard is **inert without EVAL** and static emission is untouched. **Killswitch `SCRIP_PATSALT=0`.** Removes **12 false pattern-identity collisions in beauty** (npat 74 → 86 on the one-byte input).
**⛔ THE ROW IS NOT DONE — SAY IT PLAINLY.** Beauty is **still rc=139** in both arms. The brief's DONE-WHEN (a standalone oracle-refed witness reproducing rc=139 that is GREEN under `SCRIP_RTSEQ_RESUME=0`) is **NOT met**: every witness minted this session is a wrong answer, not a crash, and every one is red in **both** arms of RTSEQ_RESUME, i.e. pre-existing rather than s183's rung.
**⭐ THE NEW HANDLE ON BEAUTY'S CRASH — THREE INPUTS BRACKET IT:** `\n` · `X\n` · `\n\n` all **SIGSEGV**; `  A = 1` answers `Parse Error` (rc=0); `* hi` is **correct**; empty input is correct. So the crash road is **`Stmt`'s empty alternative** — the four consecutive `epsilon ~ ''` this rung's witness class was minted from.
**TWO SEPARATE CLASSES FILED, BOTH OWED** (witnesses corpus `cdcebe25`): (1) **a generator in ARGUMENT position dies** — `mk(ARBNO('a'))` nomatch, `A = ARBNO('a'); mk(A)` match, and SPAN/LEN/BREAK/ARB/alternation in the same slot all GREEN (`ptw_min_argpat_arbno` + 2 controls); (2) **the OPSYN'd binary operator loses the whole blob** — `ARBNO('a') reduce("'A'",1)` match vs `ARBNO('a') ("'A'" & 1)` nomatch, and the **asm diff** shows the red arm emits **no `PAT$` blob at all** (`ptw_min_opsyn_elem` + `ptw_min_opsyn_named_ctl`); (3) incidental: `ptw_min_opsyn_named_ctl` is **m3-green m4-red**, an m3≢m4 design-invariant divergence.

A census of all **158** `jmp rcx` sites in the emitted `.s` (78 set `rax,rdi` = γ · 78 set `eax,104` = ω · `rax` at the crash is `0xffffffff`, which NO site sets) leaves only the global `RETURN: pop rcx; add rsp,8; jmp rcx` trampoline. **A match-state save frame sits exactly where the function-call return record belongs** — an RSP-depth accounting error of ONE SPINE CELL PER COMPOSED ELEMENT, which is why the crash is element-count sensitive.
**⭐ THE REDUCTION — `scripts/util_beauty_m1_reduce.sh` (`--green` for the control), THREE grammar lines, a ONE-TOKEN green/red pair:** `Label = epsilon ~ 'Label'` · `Stmt = *Label *Label` (RED 139 5/5; **`*Label` alone GREEN 0 5/5**) · `Parse = *Stmt ("'Stmt'" & 7) nl`. ## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s183-HANDOFF (HQ, Fable 5; Lon-signalled) — **THE M1 WALL FELL. BEAUTY DID NOT MOVE. M1 IS NOT EARNED.**
**WATERMARK (verified at HEAD by HQ, not quoted from a seat):** corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11** · passthru combo board **m3 147/164 · m4 133/164** · beauty_suite m3 **17/17** · smoke **7/7 both modes** · **M1 ladder m3 3/10, first red at line 8** (`board_beauty_m1.sh`).
**⭐ THE M1 WALL IS DOWN (`10d0bdbb`).** The JIT builder `sno_pat_tree_graph_rt` published a scaffolding `IR_GOTO` as its resume surface, which `branch_chain` chased to `IR_SUCCEED`, so every runtime-composed pattern conceded wholesale at β. **The cure was deleting a second spelling**, not adding a rule.
**⛔ AND BEAUTY IS UNMOVED.** The fleet then cured the rank-0 **defer-depth floor** (a pattern reached through TWO levels of `*defer` failed; one level worked — a 3-line witness, `G1='aa'` · `G0=*G1` · `P=*G0`). **9 of 10 defer witnesses went green and the beauty ladder did not move one rung.** `ptw_min_defer2_hang` still hangs, which that row's DONE-WHEN required to clear. **Do not read a cured witness list as progress on M1 — read the ladder.**
**⭐⭐ THE METHOD THAT NOW FINDS BUGS (Lon's diagnosis, s183):** *"I see they all work. And beauty does not work … the BB probes are NOT good enough tests."* A **168-combo shallow grid scored 167 AGREE**. Deep chains (3/5/7/9 levels), beauty-shaped Expr grammars, beauty's measured vocabulary, and a **random grammar fuzzer** then produced **9 distinct oracle-differential classes** from ~2,000 programs — including the campaign's **only FALSE ACCEPT** (`ARBNO('a') LEN(1)` anchored: oracle nomatch, SCRIP match; **no ALT, no defer**).
**INSTRUMENTS (all committed, all refuse to run without the oracle):** `board_beauty_m1.sh` · `util_beauty_override.sh` · `util_pattern_fuzz.py` · `util_beauty_m1_reduce.sh` (seat).
**⛔ TWO TRAPS RECORDED SO NOBODY REPEATS THEM:** an input without an `END` is **not a program** — beauty parses a whole program and the ORACLE ITSELF returns empty output on a fragment, so verdicts built on one are meaningless. And **building a standalone witness UP does not reproduce beauty's class-A crash** (HQ failed nine times; seat7 hit the identical wall from the other side — *"transcribing the shape is not transcribing the program"*). Reduce beauty **downward**.
**FINDINGS:** `…s183-m1-wall-down-the-jit-road-spelled-the-resume-surface-twice.md` · `…s183-the-defer-depth-floor-two-levels-of-defer-fail.md` · `…s183-gimpel-becomes-a-test-suite-and-finds-two-defects-on-module-two.md`.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s183 (HQ, Fable 5, max effort) — **THE M1 WALL IS DOWN. BEAUTY'S WALL MOVED FROM `Parse Error` TO A WILD JUMP. M1 IS NOT EARNED.**
**WATERMARK:** corpus **m3 332/5 · m4 325/11** (fail-set bit-identical to s182 — this rung is a PURE CURE) · passthru combo board **m3 119/120 · m4 111/120** (s182 read 108/112 · 100/112) · beauty_suite m3 **17/17** · 7-mover fence class green both arms · **ZERO `.s` movers** (10-witness md5 incl. beauty.sno).
**⭐⭐⭐ WHAT LANDED — RT-CARRIER / RTSEQ-RESUME (`10d0bdbb`, merge of HQ's cut with seat1's `943e404a`).** s182 root-caused the wall in one sentence — *a runtime-composed pattern has no β path from its right element back into its left* — and s183 names the mechanism and kills it. `sno_pat_tree_graph_rt` (the **JIT** pattern-graph builder, reached by `pat_cat` → `TT_SEQ` recipe → `dtp_fn_of`) published `gp->all[before_pat]` **RAW**; for a multi-element SEQ that is a leading argument-less `IR_GOTO` relay, which `branch_chain.c:59` then chased to the **`IR_SUCCEED` terminator** — a node not even in the emitted set, so the β dispatch defaulted to `lbl_ω` and the blob **conceded wholesale**. Measured: JIT `body_root_op=116 (IR_SUCCEED) tier=0 in_nodes=0` vs static twin `op=72 (IR_MATCH_LIT) tier=2 in_nodes=1`.
**THE CURE IS A DELETION.** Two sites spelled "which node is this blob's resume surface" (the s68/s70 **spelled-twice disease**) and the JIT copy was missing every law the static one earned since s121 — nary `out_rtail` capture, FENCE-RESUME tier-1 narrowing, right-sealed refusal, GOTO-relay skip. Both roads now call ONE AUTHORITY each: `sno_pat_carrier_build` + `sno_pat_publish_body_root`. `SCRIP_RTSEQ_RESUME=0` reverts verbatim.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-20 s174 (seat2 `/home/claude2`, Claude Opus 5, CN front; queue row `cn-oracle-rulings`) — **ROW CLOSED, BOTH RULINGS DELIVERED. `$()` IS NOW A DISJOINT NAMESPACE IN BOTH DIRECTIONS AND FOR EVERY STORE — AND THE SPLIT NEEDED NO NEW STATE AT ALL, BECAUSE `NV_t.is_const` WAS ALREADY EXACTLY THE SET OF KEYWORD-SPACE CELLS.**

Record: `FINDING-2026-08-20-s174-dollar-indirect-is-a-disjoint-namespace-and-is_const-is-the-tag.md` (this ruling) + `FINDING-2026-08-19-s173-eval-fails-not-aborts-and-the-dollar-indirect-premise-is-falsified.md` (the EVAL half and the falsification receipts; its §2 "BLOCKED" is closed and banners across). A/B baseline: origin HEAD SCRIP `bd183811` / corpus `09542e6f`.

**HQ-61 RULED (i) FAITHFUL, ON THIS SEAT'S PROBE:** *"your probe governs — ORACLE-FAITHFUL CONFIRMED … BOTH halves; drop 341-on-indirect-write; the ARCH-SN4-CONSTANTS `$()` clause goes VACUOUS … a probe that falsifies a ruling's premise supersedes the ruling's letter."* Delivered same-seat next session.

**THE MECHANISM, AND WHY THE OBVIOUS ONE IS WRONG.** A mangled keyword key (`"&&N"`, `"&\x01N"`) is a *convention*, not a property: `$()` names variables with ARBITRARY strings — `beauty.sno:104` is literally `$'$' = *White '$' *White` — so every mangled key is a string some program can type. `NV_SET_fn` sealed on the leading `'&'` and **nothing else in the tree sets the bit**, so `is_const` was ALREADY the tier-3 set: promote it from seal to NAMESPACE TAG and the split is one predicate, `_nv_ordinary(e) = !(e->is_const && _nv_kwsplit())`, applied in the five ORDINARY walkers (`NV_GET_fn`/`NV_SET_fn`/`NV_EXISTS_fn`/`NV_PTR_fn`/`NV_bind_gva`), with `NV_KW_GET_fn`/`NV_KW_SET_fn` taking the complement and owning the 341 seal outright. Two entries may now share a name in one bucket chain — that IS the two cells. **No new global, no new struct field, no key mangling.** Killswitch `SCRIP_KWSPACE_SPLIT=0` = exact pre-ruling regime (both `NV_KW_*` delegate verbatim to their ordinary twins on the OFF arm).

**⛔ THE ONE NON-OBVIOUS EDIT IS A SILENT-WRONG-ANSWER TRAP, AND IT IS THE THING TO REMEMBER:** `NV_CONST_ASSIGNED_fn` returned `e->is_const` **of the first name match**, and the bucket chain PREPENDS — so the moment an indirect write to `"&N"` lands in front of the constant, the 342 predicate answers "never assigned" for a constant that *was* assigned. Wrong diagnostic on correct code, reachable only after this ruling, and invisible to any witness that does not write BOTH cells. Now a keyword-space scan (`name && is_const`).

**THE SECOND PINNED GATE EVENT FIRED — TWICE IN A ROW NOW.** s153 minted `cn_indirect_seal.sno` as a RULING REQUEST pinning the then-current table (`indirect=42`, 341 on the indirect write) *so the ruling would be visible rather than silent*; it went RED on the first run after the flip, exactly like `cn_t1_eval_undecl.err_sno` did last session. Rewritten to the ruled table (`direct=42 · indirect=[] · write=[99] · rewrite=[100] · direct-after=42`) and **ORACLE-LAW-DERIVED, not an oracle run** (UDC is oracle-fail by construction), with the law oracle-run byte-for-byte in its siblings. **The convention is earning its keep: keep minting deliberate pins for anything a ruling might move.**

**NEW ORACLE WITNESS — `probe/cn/cn_indirect_rewrite.{sno,ref}` (`.ref` = live `sbl` stdout).** It covers the half the s173 sibling structurally could not see: that witness writes each indirect cell exactly ONCE, and SCRIP's seal only bit on the SECOND write. Oracle: three successive `$('&ZED')` writes all accepted (1/2/3), `DATATYPE` INTEGER, two `$('&ANCHOR')` writes with the keyword still reading 1, and `$N` reaching the same cell through a computed name.

**MEASURED, A/B against origin HEAD `bd183811`:** SNOBOL4 crosscheck **identical row-for-row** (m3 308/9, m4 306/10/1, DIVERGE 1, same names) · CN-4 gate **32 → 40 PASS, 0 FAIL** · CN-13 const-graph gate green · SNOBOL4 smoke 6/1 both modes (`define` PRE-EXISTING) · Icon smoke 14/14 + Icon crosscheck 4/4 · Prolog smoke 3/2 · Prolog crosscheck ×2 per arm (112/2/75 · 112/1/76 baseline vs 111/3/75 · 111/1/77 — one name, run down below) · **`.s` md5 0 movers / 527 comparable (529 rows) — no artifact regen owed.** Revert probe: `SCRIP_KWSPACE_SPLIT=0` drives the CN-4 gate to **8 FAIL** (both witnesses × both media × both the `.ref` and the stderr-SILENT assertion).

**⛔ THE PUSH REBASED ONTO seat1's s183 RT-CARRIER AND THE VERDICT WAS RE-TAKEN AT `ffbc1425`.** The crosscheck moved under this seat (m3 308/9 → **312/5**, m4 306/10/1 → **308/8/1**, DIVERGE 1 → **3**: `expr_eval` · `140_pat_eval_double_fn_trick` · `141_pat_eval_double_fn_arbno`). It is entirely upstream's — the SAME binary with `SCRIP_KWSPACE_SPLIT=0` reads **312/5, 308/8/1, DIVERGE 3, identical name-for-name**, so this row contributes ZERO rows at the new HEAD in either arm; CN-4 gate 40/0 there too. **A same-binary killswitch A/B is the only control that separates your rows from a concurrent seat's, and a rebase is exactly when you need it.** (141 at the rebased HEAD: 20 reps, m3 20/20 PASS, m4 20/20 FAIL.)

**⛔ `nm` WILL LIE TO YOU ABOUT A RUNTIME-ONLY EDIT — MEASURE THE `.s`.** Neither new symbol appears in `nm scrip`, which invites "runtime-only, codegen cannot move"; but `core.c` is linked into the compiler too and the `scrip` md5 DOES change (`3efc9203` → `b89974b3`). The 0-mover claim above is stash → rebuild → sweep → `diff` → pop, not an inference.

Four unconditional O(nblk) walks → two.
⛔ **THE CEILING IS PROVEN, NOT ASSUMED.** The index walk must complete BEFORE mark (it clears `HBF_MARK`/`HBF_PIN`, zeroes `fwd`, and builds the index mark navigates by); the forwarding walk must run AFTER mark (it reads the marks). They are separated by the phase they exist to bracket, so no restructuring that keeps mark in the middle can fuse them. **2 is the minimum this collector shape admits.**

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-19 s169 (seat3 `/home/claude3`: Claude Opus 5, PT front — queue row 6 `pt-json`, rung PT-J) — **json HAS NO PERFORMANCE CURVE. IT HAS TWO WALLS, AND ">60 s" WAS NEVER THE PROBLEM — IT IS NON-TERMINATION.**
SCRIP `a78b39fb` (read-only this rung) · corpus `a3604cc9` · .github this commit. Record: `FINDING-2026-08-19-s169-ptj-json-has-no-curve-it-has-two-walls.md`. **Instrument-only per the PT charter — NO fixes, no codegen touched, so no `.s` regens owed.** All numbers RT_OPT=`-O0`.

### THE VERDICT PT-J ASKED FOR — A NEGATIVE ONE, AND IT IS STRONGER THAN A SLOPE
- **⛔ THE 631K→64K→8K→1K BISECT CANNOT BE RUN, AND THAT IS THE FINDING.** json's cost is not a function of input SIZE — it is a step function of input SHAPE, and both steps sit three orders of magnitude below the smallest rung the brief names. **Neither linear nor superlinear: there is no curve.**
- **⛔⭐⭐⭐ WALL 1 — THE FIRST `ARBNO` ITERATION OVER A DEFERRED (`*`) BODY NEVER RETURNS.** `[]` · `["s"]` · `{"a":["s"]}` (zero ARBNO iterations) all complete in ~1.1 s. `["s","s"]` · `{"a":"s","b":"s"}` · `{"a":["s","s"]}` (ONE iteration) never complete. Controlled ladder `{"a":["s" × n]}` puts the wall between **n=1 (12 B, 1 ms)** and **n=2 (17 B, no completion)**; every row is **5 of 5 clean runs**. ⛔ **SCOPE IT:** this is `json.sno`'s CAPTURING grammar — the pure-grammar sibling `json-match.sno` matches `["s","s"]` fine and fails differently (SIGSEGV on any non-empty object). Reduction to a small witness FAILED this seat; `probe/ptj/ptj1` carries the whole grammar and shows both halves in one run. **It is a control-flow loop, not allocation growth: still spinning at 4 min 11 s with RSS FLAT at 28 MB; 420 s budget, no completion.** Every real JSON document has 2+ members somewhere, so the benchmark never runs at ANY size.
- **⛔⭐⭐ WALL 2 — ANY NUMBER WITH A LEADING DIGIT 1–9 SIGSEGVs.** `0`, `0.5`, `0.087` fine; `1` `2` `9` `12` `123` `10` `100` `-1` `1.5` `1e5` `1.5e-3` all SIGSEGV. The grammar splits exactly there: `('0' | ANY('123456789') (SPAN('0123456789') | ''))` — the `'0'` arm is safe, **the `ANY(...) (SPAN(...) | '')` arm faults.**
- **`json-match.sno` (the PURE-MATCH sibling, the one PT most wants) IS WORSE:** `{}` ok, but `{"a":[]}` · `{"a":"s"}` · `{"a":1}` all SIGSEGV, and it SIGSEGVs at **every rung** of the 409 B→631 KB ladder.
- **⭐ ONE MINIMAL WITNESS, AND IT IS A WRONG ANSWER NOT A CRASH:** `elem = *val` / `val = ('"s"' | '[' elem ARBNO(',' elem) ']')` on `["s","s"]` — **SCRIP prints FAIL, SPITBOL prints MATCH**, 9 ms, no crash. Self-recursive and non-recursive spellings both match correctly. **This is json's exact shape** — `json-match.sno` defines `jmember`/`jarray` against `*jelement` while `jelement` is assigned LAST.
- **⭐ WITNESSES MINTED — `corpus/probe/ptj/`, oracle-ref'd, every one VERIFIED TO ACTUALLY REPRODUCE.** ⛔ My first `ptj1` and `ptj2` were WRONG (self-recursive spelling / lifted-out `jnumber`) and both matched correctly — witnesses that witnessed nothing. Caught only by running them; rebuilt against the real grammars. **A probe that does not reproduce retires a defect on paper — run every witness you mint.**
- **⛔ ALL OF IT PREDATES seat3's OWN FZ-3 COMMIT — PROVEN, NOT ASSUMED.** Identical on a pristine build of `f0ae498d` (the parent of `a78b39fb`). `json-match.sno` has ZERO fences and FZ-3 ships disarmed. The oracle is correct and 0 ms on every one of these.

### ⛔⭐⭐ NEXT SEAT — PICK UP EXACTLY HERE
1. **STRIKE json FROM PT'S MEASUREMENT SET until the three defects land** — it is not a performance workload today, it is three correctness defects wearing a stopwatch. **treebank remains the workhorse; PT-0/PT-1/PT-2/PT-3 are unaffected and still unrun.**
2. **REPAIR RANK BY BLAST RADIUS:** (a) ARBNO-first-iteration-over-deferred-body non-termination — the one that makes the benchmark unrunnable and the likeliest to be hurting the real grammar corpus; (b) the `ANY(...) (SPAN(...) | '')` SIGSEGV; (c) the forward-deferred-reference wrong answer. **⛔ (a) and (c) may be one defect — do not assume they are two.**
3. **A THIRD, SMALLER OBSERVATION, HONESTLY BOUNDED:** `{"a":"s"}` under the stock harness exhausts the 512 MB arena on **4 of 5 clean runs** (intermittent, shape-sensitive) — but it is NOT simple per-iteration leakage: 400 separate `ZBODY(1)` calls AND a single `ZBODY(10000)` both complete cleanly. Whatever accumulates is in the harness calibrate/measure path.
4. **⛔ MEASUREMENT HYGIENE, RECORDED BECAUSE IT BIT ME:** my first construct table ran while a 420 s background job held a core and reported HEAP rows the clean re-run does not reproduce. **It is retracted in the FINDING.** A benchmark measured under concurrent load is a measurement of the load — `ps` for stragglers before every timing table on this front.

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ LIVE CURSOR — 2026-08-19 s154 (local seat `/home/claude3`: Fable 5 → Opus 5, BENCH front) — **THE BENCHMARK SUITE WAS MEASURING A GC STALL, AND THE DEMO BOARD DATES A FENCE REGRESSION**
SCRIP `3e2a915ca`+ · corpus `ce6404fe7`+ · .github this commit. **No codegen touched** — corpus + instrument only, so no `.s` regens owed (RULES step 4 N/A).
Records: `FINDING-2026-08-19-s154-gc-stall-is-the-benchmark-and-collect-cannot-fix-it.md` (BM-3) · `FINDING-2026-08-19-s154-bm4-workload-family-fence-is-inert-on-scrip.md` (BM-4).

### WHAT LANDED
- **BM-2/BM-3 — the microbenchmark instrument was lying twice.** `NOISE-FLOOR.tsv` was keyed to pre-promotion `*_t` names at `nohuge=0` while the runner looks up classic names at `nohuge=1`: **every lookup missed silently.** Re-baked. And the allocating rows were reporting **one GC stall, not throughput** — `table_access` published 0.23× of the oracle when the truth is 0.48×, `array_sum` 0.34× when the truth is **0.94× (parity)**. The runner now sizes the arena past the window (`HEAP=1024`) and **counts and prints regenerations per row**; revert-probe at `HEAP=512` turns exactly the two allocating rows red. Suite: **12/12 check-green, gc 0, m3≡m4 0.98–1.08×**, every elapsed on budget.
- **BM-4 — the WORKLOAD family**, `corpus/benchmarks/snobol4/demo/`, 15 programs, 5 grammars × {plain, -match, -match-fence}, shared `harness.inc`, driven by the same runner via `BENCH_DIR`. `check:` is the **deserialization census** (claws5 tokens built · json `objects/arrays/strings/ints/reals/bools/nulls/maxdepth` · calculators the sum of every expression evaluated · porter total stem length), so it witnesses the computation, not just "the match succeeded". Originals in `programs/snobol4/demo/` **untouched** — the scorecard `demos` suite (weight 15) globs them.

### ALSO LANDED — THE FENCE FRONT (second half of the seat)
- **FENCE-SPLIT — `IR_MATCH_FENCE0` is its own kind.** FENCE0 and FENCE1 are DIFFERENT CONSTRUCTS WITH OPPOSITE β SEMANTICS (manual Ch.4/18 vs Ch.19: backup through bare FENCE **aborts the attempt**; backup through `FENCE(P)` **passes through**), and they were sharing one opcode discriminated by `ival==0`. That conflation had already cost: emit.cpp's NOWHACK ledger carried a blanket exclusion **s67 had to split by arity** after measuring it mask a real `frame_need_of==1`; the ζ grant comment had to explain FENCE0 "takes this quad grant uniformly but its template arm never reads it". Now: own kind, own `bb_match_fence0.cpp`, own dispatch/drive/grant/audit entry. **Proven inert:** 862 programs swept vs a worktree built at origin — 823 byte-identical, 39 movers (all fence-bearing), **all 39 behaviourally identical**; crosscheck identical by set (m3 307/10 · m4 306/10 · DIVERGE=0).
- **⛔ FENCE0 FLOOR WHACK — IMPLEMENTED, MEASURED, FALSIFIED.** Lon's directive is that FENCE0 must WHACK. Restoring `rsp:=rbp` (the match standing floor, deeper than the UCLAIM claim, so immune to the ZWS-FENCE0-UCLAIM double-release) **cores `treebank-match-fence` at every stack size**, where disarmed it runs in 256 KB. ⛔ **The crosscheck gate did not catch it** — 306/10, DIVERGE=0, FAIL-set identical in both arms; only a demo workload failed, which is the case for BM-4 existing. **ROOT CAUSE, retiring the whole floor-restore family: FENCE0's cut kills BACKTRACKING, not DATA.** Capture cells, ARBNO instance records and cursor state left of the fence are read on the FORWARD path and at MATCH_END, so ANY bulk floor restore — `___` or `rbp` — frees live storage. Changing which floor only moves the crash. FENCE1 is no precedent: its watermark frees only what P carved after the bank, provably dead at commit.
- **FZ-1 — the planner rung that replaces it (ships DISARMED, `SCRIP_FENCE0_WHACK`).** The ζ-SPINE is LIFO ⇒ a release can only pop a CONTIGUOUS TOP region, so the planner measures how much of the frontier is provably dead rather than picking a floor. `fence0_release_bytes()` walks left in carve order accumulating `zd_k` over SPAN/BREAK/BREAKX/TAB/RTAB/REM/BAL — leaves whose 16B cell is their own cnt/cur retry state, dead once the cut forbids re-entry, result delivered in r13/r14/r15 not the cell — and stops at the first carving node that is anything else, or the bracket. `ASSIGN_SAVE` excluded BY NAME (its cell is "the SAVE cursor the paired COND/IMM reads back"). Staged as `op_fence0_release`; the template spends the count, never computes it. **Measured:** witness `SPAN FENCE SPAN SPAN` emits the release and matches the oracle on BOTH arms incl. the cut arm; armed crosscheck 307/10 · 306/10 · DIVERGE=0 identical to disarmed; treebank-match-fence correct armed (its fences correctly bill 0).

### ⛔⭐⭐ NEXT SEAT — PICK UP EXACTLY HERE
1. **⛔ FZ-1's ONE UNRECONCILED NUMBER, before anything else on this front.** The witness should bill **16** (one MATCH_SPAN between MATCH_BEGIN and the fence in the IR dump) and bills **32**. Either the `all[]` index the scan walks is not the dump's slot numbering, or a node sits between them the dump does not show. Output is correct and gates are clean, **but an OVER-release is exactly the class that cored the floor attempt**, so reconcile the count against the emitted carve before arming by default. Next instrument: print `(ni, j, op, zd_k)` per step under a diag env and diff against the `.s`. Flagged in `bb_match_fence0.cpp`.
2. **FENCE went INERT and it is DATED.** Fenced ≡ unfenced on SCRIP across calculator-1/-2/treebank while the oracle moves 1.14×–2.3×. README's s34 table (`a5c2264`, 08-09) records FENCE buying SCRIP **+49% on treebank** (1.57× vs 1.05×); today **0%**. ASM-DIFF-FIRST already run — the two calculator-1 `.s` are NOT byte-identical (210 lines, fenced 5,179 B smaller, 4 fence sites), so FENCE lowers and emits; the no-op hypothesis is dead. **`a5c2264` → HEAD is a bisect window.** ⛔ Note calculator-1/-2 emit **ZERO** fence nodes (5 and 3 in source), so for them the seal is edge-rewiring only — FZ-1 cannot help there until they mint boxes.
3. **`claws5` ×3 SIGSEGV in m3 and the ORIGINALS do too** — HQ D-2 owns it; README's claws5 rows marked ⚠ this seat.
4. **Error 22 "undefined function called"**: `porter` m3+m4, `calculator-1/-2` **m4 only** (MODE34 violation). Reproduces the **s156 B1 byname-dispatch class on real programs and extends it to m3**. Isolated: array access and literal argument both exonerated — it is the CALL. HQ D-9/D-10.
5. **`json` ×3 exceeds 60 s on SCRIP** where the oracle does 1.0K iters/s — hang vs >1000× slowdown want different hunts. HQ D-8.
6. **The GC is the biggest single perf item and is unowned**: O(blocks ever allocated), 7,352,520 walked to retain 1,548, `reclaimed 0`, **65–70% of wall clock at EVERY heap size**. Oracle runs the same workload in **2.8 MB RSS vs SCRIP's 593 MB** — its collector is O(survivors), ours O(garbage). `COLLECT()` cannot fix it (tested); `SCRIP_HEAP_MB` is a measurement device, not a fix.
7. Owed: demo-family `NOISE-FLOOR.tsv` unbaked (min-det reads `-`); timed family still not wired into `scorecard_snobol4.sh` (reweights META — Lon's call); `bb_match_begin/end`'s whack-hazard scan still lists FENCE0 for byte-identity and is probably vacuous (flagged in both files).

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐ HQ-21 CONTESTED — DOES NOT REPRODUCE AT ORIGIN HEAD (s149, measured three ways; READ THIS BEFORE ACTING ON THE BANNER BELOW)
**s149 re-measured every HQ-21 claim at origin HEAD `f80c3f4d`, baseline built in its own worktree at the named pushed commit `bc0eeff4`:** probe/cn **28/28 PASS** (every witness × arm 0/1 × m3/m4 — the `&W = "hi"` 342 does not occur in any cell) · UDC gate **12/12** · `.s` byte-identity **0 movers / 527 comparable** · by-set board **318 rows IDENTICAL** for base-vs-HEAD default, default-vs-armed m3, and default-vs-armed m4. The repair HQ asked for is **already present and was present at `746f0757`**: `sno_kw_static_slot()` returns −1 on an `rt_kw_index()` miss, so every tier-3 user constant keeps the by-name `SNO$KWSET` route — the new kind is minted only when ARMED **and** the block names the keyword.
**MECHANISM THAT MANUFACTURES HQ-SHAPED NUMBERS, MEASURED:** a fresh driver paired with a **stale `out/libscrip_rt.so`** yields **40 spurious `.s` movers**. The emitter lives in the `.so`; `make scrip` alone leaves the old one in place. RULES' "swap the `.so`, not the driver" has a second edge — **swapping the driver WITHOUT the `.so` is equally vacuous and INVENTS movers.** (40 ≠ 529, so this is the only reproducible mechanism found, not a full account.) ⛔ **CN-4/CN-4b/CN-5 are therefore NOT "unverified riders on a broken write path."** ⭐ **SEPARATE KILLSWITCH, SEPARATE VERDICT — DO NOT CONFLATE THE TWO (s149 correction):** the coarseness finding above concerns **`SCRIP_CONST_STATIC`** (CN-3 and CN-4b share `sno_const_feature()`, so its flip cannot isolate them). **`SCRIP_KW_STATIC` is a different switch and isolates PRECISELY — measured:** default-vs-armed `.s` moves **47 of 527** programs, **47 of 47 referencing a `&` name (zero false positives)**, the other 480 byte-identical. The KW killswitch touches keyword-using programs and nothing else; nothing here bears on the `SCRIP_CONST_STATIC` verdict, which stands as written and remains untested. Record: `FINDING-2026-08-19-s149-HQ21-does-not-reproduce-and-a-killswitch-too-coarse-to-isolate.md`. **Disposition owed by HQ: re-measure on a clean pair (driver AND `.so` from one commit) or strike the banner.** The D-3 repair brief below is retained verbatim as history, NOT as a live instruction.

**⛔ HQ-24's ESCALATION (`7a498ec6`, landed mid-s149) ALSO DOES NOT REPRODUCE ON MAIN — tested, not assumed:** `corpus/probe/arbnostore/` reads **10/10 at BOTH `bc0eeff4` and `f80c3f4d`, in BOTH modes** (HQ-24 reports 10→0), and the `SNO$MKPAT` empty-blob-name wall **never fires** (the path at `by_name_dispatch.c:6920` is present but unreached). arbnostore is the s122 **Milestone 1 blocker class**, so a true 10→0 would be the gravest claim on the board — it does not occur on main. **Hypothesis, not fact:** HQ measures on a tree carrying its own B2 campaign (origin now has `hq-b2`), so its numbers may describe a **B2×KW-3b interaction** or a driver/`.so` pairing — either is a real finding, but neither is *"KW-3b breaks the default arm"*, which is what this banner tells seats to repair. **On main, by every test either party has named — 342-on-write, UDC, 529 md5s, 318 board rows, arbnostore, MKPAT — KW-3b measures clean.**

## ⭐⭐⭐⭐ BENCHMARK-PROPER — TIMED LOOPS BESIDE ITERATION LOOPS (Lon 2026-08-19 in-chat, verbatim in substance: *"get benchmarks working as TIMED loops versus ITERATION loops … industry standard technique of time limiting versus count limiting"* — the 3rd concurrent GOAL-SNOBOL4-100 session)
**THE MODEL:** every benchmark splits into KERNEL (the work, an `-INCLUDE`-able body) + two thin DRIVERS: the **iteration driver** (count-limited, fixed N — DETERMINISTIC output, stays a correctness row and the cross-engine comparability anchor) and the **timed driver** (industry shape: WARMUP window, then run the kernel until `TIME() - t0 >= MEASURE_MS`, count completed iterations, report THROUGHPUT `ips:` — iterations/second). Timing lines are NEVER correctness: the scorecard's `norm=ms` precedent extends to `iters:`/`ips:` lines (rewritten on both sides before diff). The oracle runs the SAME drivers — that makes the throughput table the direct instrument for PLAN.md's product claim ("Ten times faster"): ips(SCRIP m3/m4) ÷ ips(sbl), per kernel.
**LAWS THAT BIND:** ⛔⛔ **NO `-O2` BUILDS. EVER (Lon, 2026-08-23 s262 FACT RULE — this SUPERSEDES the O0-DEV-O2-BENCH text that stood here, which is DEAD and must not be followed).** `RT_OPT` is `-O0` for development AND benchmarks AND demos. Never pass `RT_OPT="-O2 …"`, never build an `-O2` RT_TAG, never quote an `-O2` number as the current state. TWO REASONS, the second the stronger: cost (~9m30 vs ~1m40 per template-touching rebuild, paid on every arm of a measure-and-cure loop), and ⭐ **it measures a compiler we are deleting** — the RT is moving to register-aware ASM (`GOAL-RTCC.md`, `src/runtime/rtx/*.S`) with C kept for at most a few large algorithms, so an `-O2` figure grades gcc's optimizer over code that will not exist. The LABELING duty survives and now reads `-O0`. Authority: `RULES.md` § NO `-O2` BUILDS (every number labeled with its RT_OPT level) · median-of-3 minimum, min/median reported, single runs flagged as noise · `&STLIMIT` pinned high in timed drivers (a statement limit silently truncates a timed loop — measure the default, then set it explicitly) · TIME() semantics PINNED on both engines before any number is quoted (granularity, cpu-vs-wall, manual §TIME vs measured binary — the manual-is-stale law applies) · perf is a LANE BESIDE the META score, never inside it (weights untouched).
**RUNGS:** **BM-0** TIME()/&STLIMIT semantics pinned by oracle probe both engines + survey of the 23 existing benchmarks (which are count-limited today; s91: 12/15 of 23 scoreable) · **BM-1** the harness convention — `bench.inc` timing scaffold (warmup/measure/emit), the two-driver split, scorecard norm extension · **BM-2** convert the 23 (kernel extraction; iteration drivers stay correctness rows; timed drivers added; oracle-verified) · **BM-3** `scripts/bench_report.sh` — the throughput table sbl vs m3 vs m4 with ratio column, median-of-3, environment header (RT_OPT, host) · **BM-4** scorecard PERF lane wiring + the standing "10×" watermark tracked per session.
## ⭐⭐⭐⭐ WIRE-ORDER — THE γ/ω PAIR PROTOCOL (Lon 2026-08-19 in-chat, verbatim in substance: *"R10 (GAMMA) and R11 (OMEGA) stored on the stack together as the TWO POSSIBLE return locations. I LOVE THAT... ensure that EVERYWHERE the protocol ALWAYS pushes those in the EXACT order. And also store them in FRAMES in a consistent order for ALL FRAMES... an order of SAVING which I also want to be CONSISTENT EVERYWHERE."*)
**VERIFIED BY CENSUS (HQ, same day, zero deviants):** ONE memory order everywhere measured — **γ(r10) at the LOW address (+0), ω(r11) at +8** — across frame stores (24/24 pair-sites `FRQ(op_off)/FRQ(op_off+8)`), stack pushes (4/4), and the RTCC call-spill slots (`rtccb+56`/`+64`, ascending). ⛔ **THE PUSH-SEQUENCE TRAP, now law:** to produce γ@+0 on a downward stack the SEQUENCE is `push r11; push r10` — sequence is the inverse of layout BY NECESSITY. Any new site pushes ω first, stores γ low. Gate (D-14 mints it): greps for `push r10`-before-`r11` and for `FRQ(op_off)` paired with r11 == 0 forever.
**THE ALWAYS-LIVE QUESTION (Lon, same message: *"should we keep R10 and R11 always LIVE for every BB in every landing, since of course those two are saved for every activation?"*):** the register contract already declares R10/R11 LIVE-never-scratch, and RTCC already spills/restores the pair around C calls — the HOLES are paths that skip the restore (the s44 WIRE-RESTORE bug class: "without this, x86_omega reads r11 == this node's own dead L(5)"). Lon's proposal upgrades the contract to an ENFORCED INVARIANT: r10/r11 equal the running BB's staged wires at EVERY port landing. HQ position: YES as instrument-then-repair — mint a ζ-SM-style WIRE CANARY (compare r10/r11 at each port against the staged wires under an env gate), census the violations corpus-wide, repair the holes the census names, then the canary becomes the standing gate. Cost of blanket restore-at-landing is measured by the census before any blanket decision.
## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ FOUR PORT NAMES ONLY — NEVER NAME A BB PORT ANYTHING BUT α β γ ω (Lon in-chat, s133)

**A BB HAS EXACTLY FOUR PORTS: `α` fresh entry · `β` resume/backtrack-retry · `γ` success/advance · `ω` fail.** RULES.md already says *FOUR PORTS = FOUR GREEK NAMES ALWAYS* — this is that law's MEASUREMENT-SIDE corollary, earned because a session violated it while believing it was obeying it.

⛔ **THE INTERNAL GLUE OF A BOX IS NOT A PORT.** The n-ary ALT/ARBNO success- and fail-glue defines (`na_s`/`na_f` at `emit.cpp:1391-1394`, whose edge tags are spelled `0xcf83`/`0xcf86` in `IR_ref_t.sz` beside β's `0xceb2`) are LABELS A BOX DEFINES FOR ITS OWN INTERNAL DISPATCH. They are not entry ports, no template treats them as ports, and nothing may ever present them in a port column, a port grid, or a port-to-port transition table.

⛔ **CONVICTION (s133):** a seat censused 16,082 edges and reported a SEVEN-ROW grid with `γ→σ`, `γ→φ` and `ω→φ` as rows beside `γ→α` and `ω→β`. Lon rejected it on sight — *"I did not like your grid because I see more than FOUR ports. So that is invalid."* The grid was arithmetically correct and architecturally false: it manufactured a five- and six-port machine that does not exist, and it buried the one fact that mattered (γ never lands at β) among rows describing glue.

⭐ **HOW TO SAY IT INSTEAD.** When an edge lands on a box's internal glue, name it in four-port terms and say whose box it re-enters: *"the body's γ returns into the enclosing ARBNO box, which then either re-enters the body's α for another iteration or takes ARBNO's own γ to advance"* — NEVER *"γ→σ"*. The tag bytes stay in the code as an implementation detail of the edge encoding; they never reach a document, a census header, a finding, or a sentence.

**ENFORCEMENT:** any port table, census output, or FINDING naming a fifth port is REJECTED. A census that must distinguish glue edges reports them as *"returns into <box>"* rows, not as port rows.

---

## ⛔⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ THE STALL IS ROOT-CAUSED (s132, Lon in-chat) — `x86_rsp_slide_known() { return 1; }` — THE DEPTH LATTICE HAS NO ⊤, SO EVERY ADMISSION GATE IN THE TREE IS A HAND-WRITTEN ESTIMATOR OF THE MISSING PREDICATE. FULL ARGUMENT: `FINDING-2026-08-17-s132-the-stall-is-no-unknown-in-the-depth-lattice.md`.

**Lon's model, and it is the standard static-stack-height result:** offsets are `(depth at use) − (depth at allocation)`, total wherever depth is path-invariant, failing in exactly TWO ways — (a) an unknown COUNT of allocations between a RESULT's write and read (backedge accumulation), (b) a β label reached with an unknown accumulation, so a LOCAL is across an unknown number of boxes (resume at foreign depth). **No third case, and BOTH have the SAME cure: address off a base that does not move.** One analysis, one exception handler — not a taxonomy.

**THE ONE LINE:** `x86_asm.h:582` `inline int x86_rsp_slide_known() { return 1; }`. The predicate answering "is depth statically known here?" is hardwired YES, so `x86_frame_off`'s `-1` unknown-depth branch is **DEAD CODE**. Its own comment records the s53 ruling that did it and names Lon's two classes verbatim — *"unbounded ARBNO instance growth, suspend re-entry at foreign depth) now fail HONESTLY at run time and go on the failure list"*. **Every session since has worked that list one entry at a time. This inverts that ruling: the list becomes the code.**

**WHY IT EXPLAINS THE CADENCE:** with no ⊤ computable, "does this cell need an immovable base?" is APPROXIMATED per construct, by hand, once per session — `leaf_frame_candidate()`, `blob_choice_rbp_scan()`, s128's 5-term admission, the s130 8-byte window law, the s131 BAL refuse, s127's retracted shape-framing, s131b's reverted re-base. Each is right on its measured corner and wrong on the next, **because none is the property**. One corner per session is the arithmetic of that, and working corners faster cannot fix it.

**THE CURE IS ALREADY IMPLEMENTED — FOR ONE CONSTRUCT.** `op_stmt_dyn` (`emit.h:624`) IS case (a), modelled correctly (*"makes the statement extent COMPILE-TIME INDETERMINABLE"*) and cured correctly with a depth-immune base (`lea rsp,[___+op_zgpop]`, *"restores the pre-claim frontier at ANY dynamic depth"*). Defect is SCOPE: a per-construct latch, not a graph property. ⛔ **AND THE β SIDE HAS NO VERDICT AT ALL** — `op_stmt_dyn` reaches exactly two files; nothing at a β port ever asks. That is why `IR_MATCH_DEFER` β is a bare `jmp [rsp]` (witness `probe/deferclob/`).

**⛔ THIS RETIRES ζ-ONE U-2..U-5 AS WRITTEN** (migrate families under op-filters = more of the disease; the op-filters ARE the estimators). U-0/U-1 STAND as necessary plumbing — `zone_ref` is already the single place the tier decision is spelled and needs NO change, since it keys off `rbp_off != -1`; **the PLANNER supplies the verdict.** Replacement ladder + the honest sequencing risk (s131b's standing-carve geometry is a PREREQUISITE, not a follow-up, or ⊤ classifies more cells RBP than the frame is shaped to hold) is in the FINDING's NEXT RUNG section.

## ⭐⭐⭐⭐ ζ-ONE — THE UNIFIED RSP/RBP OFFSET AUTHORITY (design of record; Lon in-chat s132, verbatim: *"many of the bugs are caused by having two disagreeing authorities on calculating RSP offsets and RBP offset. Why not make it ONE authority so that we will not have to play WHACK-a-MOLE... one function, one data structure, one pass, one whatever"* + *"Make a global variable if you can fix this mess with it"*)

**⛔ THE DIAGNOSIS, MEASURED (`scripts/census_zeta_offset_authorities.sh`, s132 baseline):** raw `[rsp` literals **250** · raw `[rbp` literals **35** · `FR(` 186 · `FRQ(` 989 · `LFC(` 67 · `LFCQ(` 2 · `x86_scratch_off` 49 · `capture_frame_slot` 16 · `arbno_frame_slot` 16 · `frame_slot_scan` 18 · `sn4_choice_rbp_off` 6 · `blob_frame_bytes` 16 · `zd_` 200. Top raw-literal files: `emit.cpp` 52 · `x86_asm.h` 46 · `xa_flat.cpp` 32 · `bb_match_arbno.cpp` 29 · `emit.h` 25 · `bb_call_proc_staged.cpp` 21. **285 raw spellings + 7 partial accessor families = the whack-a-mole backlog, quantified.**

**⭐ THE CORRECTED FRAMING — WE ARE GENERALISING A PROVEN PROTOTYPE, NOT WRITING A NEW AUTHORITY.** Two things already exist and must be built ON, not replaced: (1) **`x86_frame_off` (`x86_asm.h:583`) IS the ONE offset function for RSP** and says so — "Nothing else may add a frame displacement"; its sole consumers are `x86_frame_modrm` (BINARY modrm), `x86_frame_text_mem` (TEXT), and `FR`/`FRQ`. (2) **`LFC`/`LFCQ` (`x86_asm.h:1184-85`, s131) IS ALREADY THE TWO-ARM SHAPE ζ-ONE NEEDS** — `LFC_ON() ? RDD("rbp", op_leaf_frame_off + d) : FR(x86_scratch_off + d)` — one accessor choosing an RBP home or an RSP home for one cell family, with a `-1` sentinel making every legacy path byte-identical. **THE DEFECT IS SCOPE, NOT ABSENCE: that authority is RSP-only, RECORD-BLIND, and PER-FAMILY.** RBP has no counterpart, so RBP homes are spelled by four unrelated registries (`frame_slot_off` 32:64/40:72, `sn4_choice_rbp_off`, `LFC`'s rbp arm, `blob_frame_bytes`); runtime RECORD layouts are hardcoded per template (the s132 `deferclob` corpse: `IR_MATCH_DEFER` β = `jmp qword ptr [rsp]`, zero-offset no-record, three lines from `IR_MATCH_ALTERNATE` β = `mov rax,[rsp+8]` — two private layout assumptions on one shared region); and the s97 push/pop dodge exists precisely BECAUSE templates route AROUND the compensator rather than through it.

**⭐ THE THREE THINGS `zd_plan` IS BLIND TO** (each is a named corpse, not speculation): runtime records (s97 dodge) · blob activation frames (s128, verbatim: *"the zd cross-blob pricing must learn `blob_frame_bytes` first"*, unbuilt) · the standing carve (s131b re-base blocker, `trueinline_grn` regression). **Every corpse of s127-s132 sits on a seam between two regimes.** Each session unified one fact; the moles are the remainder.

### THE SHAPE — one structure, one function, one pass
- **ONE DATA STRUCTURE — `ZOne_t`, per-graph, riding `g_emit`** (stage/dispatch/clear discipline at `emit.cpp:447-454`; the `xa_strtab_*` precedent, Lon s122). Every storage word has exactly ONE canonical home `{owner node, tier, width, index}` where tier ∈ **SPINE(rsp) · STANDING(rbp) · ACTIVATION(rbp) · RECORD-FIELD**. ⛔ **RECORD LAYOUTS BECOME REGISTRY-DECLARED FIELD TABLES** — `{resume, entry_cursor, saved_rbp@+24}` spelled ONCE, so no template may ever privately assume a record shape again. **NO NEW FILE-SCOPE GLOBAL** (grant banked, unused unless the arena needs backing storage — then named in the commit); `PEERS RULE` untouched, nothing added to `BB_t`/`IR_t`.
- **ONE FUNCTION — `zone_ref(class, idx, width)`**, sited in `x86_asm.h` beside `FR`/`FRQ`/`LFC`/`LFCQ` (s131's "THE ACCESSOR LEFT THE TEMPLATE" law), the only thing a template may say to address storage, BOTH MEDIA. Internally: SPINE → today's `x86_frame_off`; STANDING/ACTIVATION → the RBP registry; RECORD-FIELD → the field table. **It is `LFC`'s two arms generalised from one family to all.**
- **ONE PASS — `zd_plan` extended** to assign every home BEFORE emission, finally seeing records, blob frames, and the standing carve. **Tier rule:** statically-known depth+lifetime → SPINE; must survive dynamic carving/suspension/re-entry → ACTIVATION; match-lifetime → STANDING. ⛔ **REFUSAL IS THE SAFETY PROPERTY:** where static depth is unknowable the pass REFUSES SPINE and homes on RBP — s131's BAL refuse made structural instead of per-rung judgement.

### ⛔ THE MIGRATION LAW (this is what keeps ζ-ONE from becoming retraction #4)
**EVERY RUNG'S FIRST CUT RETURNS TODAY'S LEGACY SPELLING BYTE-IDENTICALLY, PER CELL CLASS** — the exact discipline s131 used migrating `bb_match_span` onto `LFC` (26 refs, `-1` sentinel). So the corpus-wide md5 manifest holds at EVERY rung and each family migration is a op-filter change in ONE file. **Precedent for why: s127's wide cut produced five OFF-PASS→ON-SEGV movers and was retracted same-session; s130's one-constant re-base broke the `trueinline_grn` green control and was reverted same-session. Both were caught ONLY by the full battery.** ζ-ONE lands as byte-identical plumbing FIRST, families move through it under gates SECOND. ⛔ A rung that cannot show byte-identity on the default arm DOES NOT LAND.

### RUNGS AND STEPS
- **U-0 — CENSUS + RATCHET ✅ LANDED s132 (SCRIP `5e35e6c2`).** `scripts/census_zeta_offset_authorities.sh` (advisory default; `--strict` fails if any file in `scripts/zone_migrated.list` still carries raw `[rsp`/`[rbp`). List empty at U-0. **Ratchet direction: raw counts → 0, migrated list → every file in the top-files table.** STEP: run it at every ζ-ONE handoff, paste counts into the cursor; the number must never rise.
- **U-1 — THE DISPATCHER ✅ LANDED s132 (SCRIP `5e35e6c2`).** `zone_ref(rbp_off, spine_base, d, w)` in `x86_asm.h` is THE SOLE PLACE A TIER DECISION IS SPELLED; `LFC`/`LFCQ` re-expressed on it. **GATE MET: manifest 62/62 byte-identical across BOTH arms (default AND `SCRIP_SPAN_FRAME=1`) — baseline from a git-stash PRISTINE rebuild (non-vacuous), re-proven after pop; leafsib 6/8 == s131 watermark; greps 0.** ⛔ REMAINING U-1 WORK, NOT DONE: the `ZOne_t` structure + per-graph registry in `zeta_storage.{c,h}` is DESIGNED, NOT BUILT — U-1 landed the dispatcher half only, which is what the byte-identity gate could carry at this seat's budget. The structure is U-1b and is the prerequisite for U-2's field tables. ORIGINAL STEPS (b/c done, a outstanding): (a) `ZOne_t` + cell-class enum in `src/contracts/zeta_storage.{c,h}` (existing depth-model home). (b) `zone_ref()` in `x86_asm.h` dispatching SPINE→`x86_frame_off`, RBP tiers→registry, RECORD→field table. (c) **Re-express `LFC`/`LFCQ` in terms of `zone_ref`** — the proof the generalisation is faithful, since LFC's behaviour is already gated and measured. GATE: default-arm md5 manifest byte-identical corpus-wide; `SCRIP_SPAN_FRAME=1` arm ALSO byte-identical (LFC's ON behaviour preserved); leafsib 6/8 unmoved; template-only + both-medium greps 0.
- **U-2 — RECORD FIELD TABLES (this is `deferclob`'s cure).** Declare ALT / DEFER / ARBNO / capture-save record layouts in the registry; convert `IR_MATCH_DEFER` β off its bare `jmp [rsp]` onto the declared field, and `IR_MATCH_ALTERNATE` β off its private `[rsp+8]`. Killswitch `SCRIP_ZONE_REC=1`, default OFF byte-identical. GATE: `probe/deferclob/deferclob_stmt_alt_red` GREEN both media · `deferclob_stmt_standalone_grn` unmoved · clobarm/altdepth/leafsib/arbnostore/seam/arbnofence unmoved · crosscheck full A/B zero movers · beauty re-measured. ⛔ Expect a further blocker behind it; re-measure, do not assume last.
- **U-3 — RBP REGISTRY UNIFICATION.** Fold `frame_slot_off`, `sn4_choice_rbp_off`, `LFC`'s rbp arm and `blob_frame_bytes` into ONE registry with ONE floor convention. **This is where the s131b "never pass a pointer into the middle" re-base belongs** — re-derive the standing head layout from `emit_match_begin_rbp`/`emit_match_begin_frame_extra` and widen/shift the standing carve IN THE SAME EDIT. GATE: `clob_altarm_trueinline_grn` ON-arm (the named discriminating witness s131b failed) + leafsib + clobarm + scorecard. - **`fc_tables_reset` widening** (14 tables unreset) — INERT on A/B, reverted. **`g_chain_entry_key[65536]`** — gates anchor pull-in, not arming. **Source-line attribution** — comment lines kill it.

- **s102's "monitor is dark for DEFINE-bearing programs" applies to `MONITOR_BIN` only** — `test_monitor_2way_spitbol_vs_run.sh` (spl↔scr, no csnobol4 build) runs clean.
- **TOP-PORTS hoist (s118b): built twice behind a killswitch, measured broken twice, reverted.** Three facts not to rediscover: (1) `main_α_body` FALLS THROUGH into the first-emitted node — anything inserted there runs at startup; (2) label/pool ids (`.Lx`, `.Lrkfnzd`) come from ONE `g_flat_node_id` stream consumed at emission time, so early emission renumbers everything after it (ports-only hoist broke `fibonacci` m3 rc=1 ⇒ some consumer treats those ids as more than local); (3) RETURN/FRETURN floaters are `IR_DEFINE` ival=1/2 = `zd_sr_role`'s SAVE_RESTORE citizens — emission-order staging mutation is the prime suspect for the `cap_imm_nret` Error-22-at-statement-0 death. Detail: `FINDING-2026-08-16-s118-top-ports.md`.
- **A passing sibling is NOT evidence an offset is right** (CLIMB s23/s24 convictions). An instruction byte-identical in the passing sibling IS exonerated.

- **⛔ THE DEFER-β FIX: TWO STATIC/AMBIENT SHAPES ARE FALSIFIED, NOT MERELY UNTRIED (s140).** Widening the γ records to 24B with `rbp` as a 3rd quad, read at β via a baked `RDQ("rsp",16)`, is measured broken; and β cannot trust ambient `rbp` **AND** a raw stack-pushed record at a baked displacement cannot replace it. The shape that remains is **U-2, the record-field**. Method rule bought with it: **test all 5 `probe/m1` witnesses after every build, before beauty** — that seat's own falsified hypothesis looked "fixed" on a single witness.
- **⛔ A GLOBAL-LIFO α:β BANK IS THE WRONG MODEL, AND IT MANUFACTURED A RETRACTION (s141).** The reported "38/37 MISMATCH + 1437 UNDERFLOW" on beauty was **VOID**: the instrument assumed α:β pairs 1:1. **α runs ONCE, β re-fires MANY times.** Any port-balance instrument built on pairing is measuring its own assumption.
- **⛔ "THE INSTRUMENT IS SILENT IN MODE 3" (s135) IS REFUTED (s137).** `rt_zdp_anchor` is not missing from the link — `scrip` links `libscrip_rt.so` **DYNAMICALLY** (`-L out -lscrip_rt`), so a stale `.so` beside a fresh binary reads exactly like a dead instrument. Rebuild the `.so`, then re-ask. Fix shape (A) in its broad form is separately **refuted by measurement** (s137, landed OFF).
- **⛔ A ONE-KERNEL NOISE FLOOR IS NOT A GLOBAL CONSTANT (s149).** `arith_loop`'s cv 1.3% was about to be written into the benchmark runner as a global "~4%". Per-row dispersion differs by kernel; a floor measured on one row and applied to all manufactures both false movers and false stability. (Same family as the `util_out_sweep` false-mover class — see the s185 FINDING.)
- **⛔ A CURSOR IS NOT A MEASUREMENT (s155, and it cost two sessions).** s153 wrote "EXECUTED / VERIFIED: 12/12" for work that was never committed and s154's HQ review repeated it; all 5 claims were void at HEAD. Sibling law to PRISTINE-BUILD-BEFORE-VERDICT: **read the tree, not the prose** — the fleet-era restatement is HQ LAW 10/11 (FETCH-IS-NOT-CHECKOUT · REVIEW-MEANS-OPEN-THE-ARTIFACT).
- **⛔ TWO PUBLISHED "THE ROAD IS DEAD" VERDICTS WERE RETRACTED BY THEIR OWN AUTHORS (s167 CONST-GRAPH `proc_PAT$N` unreachable; s182 the "five refusing blobs" read, falsified by `SCRIP_FENCE_IGNORE=1`).** Both were reached from an instrument reading rather than an ablation. **A negative verdict on a road needs the same ablation a positive one does.**
- **⛔ GC-R IS ANSWERED **NO**, AND THE KNOB IS CONFOUNDED BY CONSTRUCTION (s169).** `SCRIP_GC_BUDGET_MB` forces `g_alloc_detax=-1` and disarms the inline allocation fast path (`gc_heap.c:219–220`), so it cannot price the cadence hypothesis at all; it helped in 1 of 10 measured cells and hurt in the rest (`array_sum` @512 −44%). **Do not expose it as a tuning knob**; if revived, decouple the cadence trigger from `g_alloc_detax` first. `FINDING-2026-08-19-s169-gc-r-the-budget-knob-cannot-price-the-cadence-hypothesis.md`.
- **⛔ DO NOT RE-DERIVE (banked, each already paid for):** the collector is **O(garbage) vs the oracle's O(survivors)** — 65–70% of wall at EVERY heap size, 593MB vs 2.8MB RSS, and `COLLECT()` cannot fix it (s154/s166) · **PT-0/PT-1/PT-2 are DONE** (`FINDING-…-s168-PT-0-1-2-treebank-baseline…`) · **M1-R0 is DONE** (SCRIP `281040ef`, `FINDING-…-s145`).

## ⛔ REPEAT SETUP TRAPS — hit for the 5th+ session each; read before spending a measurement on them
Process substitution `<(...)` fails under `sh` — pipe to files · `sort -t$'\t'` fails under `sh` — use `TAB=$(printf '\t')` · `join -j2` puts the KEY FIRST, so the second file's columns shift by one (an uncorrected join reported "384 movers of 384 rows") · `gawk` required for `scorecard_snobol4.sh report` · `corpus` and `x64` need `git config --local` identity (a plain `git commit` in corpus SILENTLY fails) · `sbl -I` is not a flag — run the oracle from the include dir · backgrounded builds die in this container — build in foreground under `timeout` · C++ template externs need the C-linkage block.

## ⭐⭐⭐ DESIGN OF RECORD — RESCUED FROM PRUNED CURSORS (s138; these outlive the sessions that measured them)

### ⭐ STANDING FINDING (independent of instrument validity)
`x86_rsp_slide_known()` (x86_asm.h:582) is hardwired `return 1`, asserting depth is *"ALWAYS knowable — there is no other base."* ZDP measures that false at ~7.38% ACTIVATION. The compiler addresses through an assertion nothing checks. The anchor instrument is the thing that settles it — which is why this rung outranks everything else on the file.

### ⭐⭐⭐ LON'S UNKNOWN-DEPTH TAXONOMY (design of record, in-chat s133) — **FIVE SOURCES, TWO CLASSES, ONE PREDICATE**
**(A) UNKNOWN PATTERN** — the carve's IDENTITY is a runtime value: (1) `*P` pattern defer (`IR_MATCH_DEFER`) · (2) `P()` pattern-returning function.
**(B) UNKNOWN NUMBER OF SUBSEQUENTS** — the carve's COUNT is a runtime value: (3) ARBNO's β (unbounded iteration) · (4) FENCE1 · (5) ALTERNATES after their γ (which arm ran is unknown, so the accumulated carve is).
⭐ **BOTH CLASSES ARE ONE PREDICATE** — they are the two ways a meet disagrees, which is Lon's own s132 result ("no third case, and BOTH have the SAME cure: address off a base that does not move"). The taxonomy is a SOURCE list, not two analyses. ⛔ **IT IS UNVALIDATED.** The validating experiment is CHEAP and is the next rung: a corrected ZDP flags exactly these five and clears everything else. If it does, the SPINE/ACTIVATION tier rule writes itself and `zone_ref` needs no change (it already keys off `rbp_off != -1`; the PLANNER supplies the verdict).

### ⛔ MEASURED PORT LAW (s133, 16,082 generated edges / 180 programs) — supersedes any prose guess
- **γ→β CROSS-BOX = 0. NOT RARE — STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE** (Lon's argument, verified): the target's α never ran, so no cell exists for β to read. Sole hit in the corpus is a `FAIL` SELF-EDGE (`057_pat_fail_builtin`, `srcidx==tgtidx`, `rawsz=ceb2`, not goto-chased) where the box's own α provably just ran. A self-loop is not a transition between boxes.

- [ ] **⛔⭐⭐ R-8 · OPSYN OPERATOR IDENTITY + THE COERCION SHAPE REVERSION (Lon in-chat 2026-08-15 s105; two halves of ONE fact: an operator's meaning and its coercions belong to the OPERATION, never to the call site).** Opened on a measured census, not a hypothesis. **THE LAW (manual pp.115-116, Ch.15 pp.181-183, VERIFIED against live `sbl` this session):** SPITBOL splits the operator space in two and the halves get OPPOSITE treatment. (i) The USED operators (`+ - * / ** | . $ @ ? =` …) **CANNOT be OPSYN'd** — `OPSYN('+','PLUS',2)` is **ERROR 156** *"OPSYN first arg is not correct operator name"* — and the manual states the reason verbatim: *"by not allowing basic system functions and operators to be redefined, SPITBOL is able to optimize the code it generates."* Static arithmetic identity for these is therefore SANCTIONED BY THE LANGUAGE, not a shortcut. (ii) The UNUSED operators — binary `& @ # % ~`, unary `! % / # = |` — are OPSYN-able **at runtime, repeatedly**, to any built-in or program-defined function. They therefore carry **ZERO** static identity: no arithmetic kind, no coercion, no precedence-implied meaning. ⛔ **MEASURED DEFECT TABLE (s105, oracle-diffed, repros in `/tmp` → mint to `corpus/probe/opsyn/`):** with `OPSYN(<op>,'cat',2)` and `cat(a,b) = a b`, oracle prints `abcdef` / `x5` for every row. MEASURED against the minted witnesses (`corpus/probe/opsyn/`, all 8 RED at SCRIP `ba53a207`+MV-glue): `&`→**SIG11** · `@`→**SIG11** · `~`→**SIG11** (these three DO reach the correct `TT_OPSYN` arm, `snobol4.y:129/138/164` — the dispatch itself is broken) · `#`→**exit 0, prints `0`** · `%`→**exit 0, prints `0`** (silent WRONG ANSWER, the worst class) · `#`→`DIFFER` builtin target→**rc=1** · unary `!`→`ANY`→**rc=1** · rebind-twice→**SIG11**. `#`/`%` are wrong because **the grammar hard-wires them to ARITHMETIC**: `snobol4.y:145` `T_2POUND → expr_binary(TT_MUL)`, `:154` `T_2PERCENT → TT_DIV`, `:176` `T_1BANG → TT_POW`, `:177` `T_1PERCENT → TT_DIV`, `:178` `T_1SLASH → TT_DIV`, `:179` `T_1POUND → TT_MUL`. Six arms, filled in from the precedence table (unused `#` sits at priority 7 beside `*`, `%` at 10 beside `/`), mistaking SAME PRIORITY SLOT for SAME OPERATION. The `0` is the coercion signature: both strings coerced to numeric 0 and multiplied. Conversely SCRIP **silently accepts** the illegal `OPSYN('+','PLUS',2)` and prints `3` where the oracle raises 156 — permissive where it must be strict, static where it must be dynamic. **Blocks `semantic.sno` (`OPSYN('~','shift',2)`, `OPSYN('&','reduce',2)`) ⇒ on the beauty path, R-2-adjacent.** ⛔⭐⭐⭐ **s192 (seat5, queue row `opsyn-3arg-ruling`) — HALF OF THIS RUNG IS CLOSED AND THE OTHER HALF'S DEFECT TABLE IS TWO-THIRDS STALE. DO NOT PLAN OFF THE s105 NUMBERS.** **CLOSED:** the sentence *"SCRIP silently accepts the illegal `OPSYN('+','PLUS',2)` and prints `3`"* is cured — `opsyn()` opened `(void)type;` and **discarded** the kind selector, so every form was a function alias; it now raises **156** (bad operator for the kind), **152** (arg 3 not integer) and **153** (negative or > 2^24, bisected — the same bound KW-7 found for 210), routed through `kwb_error` so `&ERRLIMIT` converts them exactly as 208/209/210. Contract pinned in 13 oracle-exact lines at `probe/opsyn/opsyn_kind_selector.sno`. ⛔ **AND THE LAW ABOVE NEEDS ONE CORRECTION FROM THE LIVE ORACLE: arg 3 is NOT restricted to {0,1,2}.** The SIL adds the undefined-binop count to `i` and compares against the undefined-unop count, so **only 1 reaches the unary table and EVERY other non-zero reaches the binary one** — `i=3` and `i=99` are legal binary opsyns (measured). A `kind == 2` test is stricter than SPITBOL. **RE-MEASURED s192, pristine, both modes, on the eight witnesses s105 minted:** `&`/`@`/`~` were **SIG11** and are now **PASS/PASS**; `opsyn_rebind_twice` was **SIG11** and is now **m3 PASS / m4 FAIL**; `#`/`%` still print `0`, and `opsyn_builtin_target` / `opsyn_unary_target` / `d_unary` **fail m3 and do not COMPILE in m4**. **Four of eight cured, three SIG11s gone.** The survivor is exactly the class named above — the grammar's arithmetic hard-wiring of the unused operators — and it is now the WHOLE of R-8's remaining work. ⛔ **PLUS TWO m3 ≠ m4 SPLITS, ONE OF THEM IN NO LEDGER: `opsyn_rebind_twice` and `f_apply` (m3 PASS, m4 SIGSEGV).** Both pre-existing; the `SCRIP_OPSYN_KIND=0` A/B moves neither. **R-8(c) IS ANSWERED BY A THIRD OPTION:** `probe/opsyn/unstable-oracle/` was parked because the ERROR-156 banner is unpinnable and the choice was posed as "normalise the grader OR leave it manual" — `&ERRLIMIT` + `&ERRTYPE` makes the WITNESS catch the error and print its CODE, so the oracle emits clean diffable lines and no grader change is needed. See `FINDING-2026-08-20-s192-opsyns-third-argument-is-a-kind-selector-and-scrip-was-discarding-it.md`. ⛔⭐⭐⭐ **2026-08-27 (seat06, queue row `conform-opsyn-operator-rebind-gz5-fatal`) — THE SURVIVOR NAMED ABOVE (grammar hard-wiring of the unused operators) IS FIXED, ALL SEVEN REMAINING ARMS. 17 OF 19 R-8 WITNESSES NOW PASS BOTH MODES; FULL SNOBOL4 CORPUS 365/365, ZERO REGRESSIONS.** `snobol4.y`+`snobol4.tab.c`: binary `#`/`%` and unary `!`/`%`/`/`/`#`/`=` now all build `TT_OPSYN` nodes (matching the already-correct `&`/`@`/`~`/`|` sibling lines) instead of `TT_MUL`/`TT_DIV`/`TT_POW`/`TT_ASSIGN`. This is what the split-off row `conform-opsyn-operator-rebind-gz5-fatal`'s FATAL actually was — a parser bug, not a `lower_snobol4.c` GZ#5 dispatch gap as that row's brief assumed (unary `!` built a one-child `TT_POW`, tripping the binary-arity check). ⛔ **STILL RED, NOT THIS FIX, NOT A REGRESSION (both already-red at s105, re-confirmed):** `opsyn_rebind_twice` (m3 PASS, m4 empty output — a runtime call-dispatch bug for OPSYN-established names in compiled mode) and `opsyn_builtin_target`/`opsyn_unary_target` (OPSYN targeting a builtin, and `!` used as a pattern operand — the latter now a silent wrong answer instead of a FATAL, since the pattern-building dispatch has no `TT_OPSYN` admission arm yet, same family as the `comment.sno` fix). Full detail: `FINDING-2026-08-27-seat06-opsyn-unary-gz5-fatal-fixed-r8-class-closed-17-of-19-rebind-twice-is-separately-broken-and-nondeterministic.md`. R-8 is NOT closed — `opsyn_rebind_twice`/`opsyn_builtin_target`/`opsyn_unary_target` remain, and the latter two are genuinely new investigations, not sibling fixes. ⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CORRECTION, later same day (seat06):** the "memory-layout-sensitive/nondeterministic" characterization above was a testing artifact (retracted, not real — see the FINDING). Went further on `opsyn_rebind_twice`'s actual root cause: `g_rt_gen_procs` (mode-4's by-name dispatch table) never gets an entry for ANY OPSYN-established name — `register_fn_alias`/`_func_buckets` is a different, unread table, and is itself incomplete for aliasing a statically-compiled target. A fix was written, verified to make the by-name dispatch work, then found to introduce a SIGSEGV via a SECOND, separate by-name dispatch path (`rt_call_arr_bl`→`APPLY_fn`→`_usercall_hook`, `driver_hooks.c`) that also touches the same broken placeholder state — REVERTED, not landed; the reverted fix's code and the crash trace are both kept in `FINDING-2026-08-27-seat06-opsyn-rebind-two-dispatch-paths-disagree-attempted-fix-reverted.md` for whoever continues this. ⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLOSED, SAME DAY (seat06) — R-8 IS NOW FULLY CLOSED, 18/19.** The revert's own root-cause paragraph was itself wrong about which line of `f14_opsyn.sno` failed (`double(21)`, not `!21`) and about the fix shape needed. Real cause: THREE gaps, all upstream of `g_rt_gen_procs`/`_func_buckets` disagreeing — `g_user_call_hook` is never installed for standalone mode-4 binaries at all (only `scrip.c`'s driver `main()` sets it); `rt_proc_call_open`/`rt_proc_fn`/`rt_proc_jmp_entry` (a family always invoked together) had no alias fallback and fixing only one crashed on a NULL from another; `rt_proc_epilogue_named` (the name-based RETURN read) resolved the alias name instead of the real target's own name. Fixed via one shared `rt_proc_find_alias` helper redirecting through the EXISTING `entry_label` field, duplicating no state — `g_rt_gen_procs` never gains alias entries, unlike the reverted attempt. `opsyn_rebind_twice.sno` now PASSES both modes and is ALSO the proof hq_C's re-pointed "must resolve through `rt_call_arr_bl`/`APPLY_fn` too" concern is satisfied (`&`→`cat`/`rev` are static targets going through exactly that path). 18/19 opsyn probes pass; the survivors (`opsyn_builtin_target`/`opsyn_unary_target`, OPSYN-to-a-BUILTIN — a different, still-unopened gap) confirmed pre-existing via `git stash`, not this fix. A SEPARATE, pre-existing `APPLY('alias',...)` SIGSEGV was found and minted (`corpus/probe/opsyn/opsyn_apply_alias_sigsegv.sno`) but NOT fixed — own future row. Full detail: `FINDING-2026-08-27-seat06-opsyn-alias-dispatch-fixed-mode4-had-no-user-call-hook-and-two-more-gaps.md`.
  - **(a) GRAMMAR — the eleven OPSYN-able operators lower to `TT_OPSYN` and nothing else.** Delete all six mis-mapped arms above; every unused unary/binary symbol becomes `expr_unary/expr_binary(TT_OPSYN, …)` carrying its symbol in `v.sval`, exactly as `&`/`@`/`~` already do. Precedence/associativity rows stay as the manual pins them (binary `&` 2 left · `@` 5 right · `#` 7 left · `%` 10 left · `~` 13 right; unary all equal, right-to-left) — precedence is SYNTAX and is fixed; only the OPERATION is dynamic. **Regen law: `bison`/`flex` output is the committed artifact — regen and confirm byte-identical BEFORE the grammar edit, then edit and regen (REPO-SCRIP.md).** GATE: `--dump-ir` on each of the eleven shows a dynamic dispatch node, ZERO `IR_BINOP`/`IR_COERCE_*`.
  - **(b) DISPATCH — fix the `TT_OPSYN` runtime path that SIG11s even where correctly wired** (`lower_snobol4.c:386`). An OPSYN'd operator is a by-name call to a target that may be a built-in, a program-defined function, or another operator, rebindable between any two statements: it must ride the SAME by-name machinery s104 built for computed targets (`rt_dyn_alpha_fn`, sealed-alpha cells, `rt_call_proc_descr` dyn arm) — ONE mechanism, not a second. Operand count comes from the OPSYN third arg (1/2), NOT from the symbol. No new global (registry rides the existing proc registry).
  - **(c) ERROR 156 — reject OPSYN of a used operator, first-arg check, at the `OPSYN` builtin** (`lower_snobol4.c:2410` is the compile-time arm; the check is RUNTIME because the prototype string can be computed). Oracle-pinned text; witness both modes.
  - **(d) ⛔ COERCION SHAPE REVERSION (Lon ruling, verbatim in substance: *"revert IR_COERCE_NUMERIC anyway, it produces code bloat for not much gain … we're back to just a straight RT call for each"*).** The s211 design (`DESIGN-SN4-NUMERIC-COERCION.md`) hoisted coercion OUT of the operation into per-operand-edge `IR_COERCE_NUMERIC` nodes; SNOBOL4 shipped that hoist ONLY on the relop/predicate path (`sx_pred_cmp`, `lower_snobol4.c:181-182` — two coerce nodes per `GT/LT/EQ/NE/GE/LE`). **UNWIRE IT: one RT call per operation, coercion folded inside.** The RT call does its own tag test with the bit trick the design doc already proved free (§3.1): `DT_I=6=0b110`, `DT_R=7=0b111` differ in exactly bit 0 and are the only numeric tags, so `mov eax,[a_tag]; or eax,[b_tag]; test eax,1` decides int-vs-real in two instructions — **no conversion call when none is needed, and NO four-way INT/REAL combinatorial inlining (Lon: "too much")**. ⛔ **DO NOT touch `lower_icon.c:342/343/368`** — Icon's arithmetic legitimately uses these nodes and is a different language with a different goal file. `IR_COERCE_NUMERIC` + `bb_coerce_numeric.cpp` (65 lines) survive for Icon; only the SNOBOL4 edges unwire. ⛔ **PRESERVE THE POSITIONAL ERROR CONTRACT** — the hoist's whole correctness payload: each operand validates SELF and raises ITS OWN oracle-pinned code, **first position first** (`GT('x','y')` → **111** *"GT first argument is not numeric"*, never 112; manual p.271 error list, one code pair per relop). The folded RT call must test operand 0 and raise before it looks at operand 1; a witness per relop × per position, both modes, or this step is not done. GATE: killswitch byte-identity is N/A (shape change) ⇒ md5 blast radius over crosscheck+patterns+probe with a BY-SET old-vs-new, ZERO PASS→fail; `.text` size delta reported (the bloat claim is the rung's premise — MEASURE it, do not assert it); relop witnesses green both modes.
  - **(e) WITNESSES + GATE SCRIPT.** Mint `corpus/probe/opsyn/` with a `.ref` beside each from live `sbl`: the eleven operators × {program-defined target, built-in target}, rebinding mid-program (same operator OPSYN'd twice), the illegal-`+` 156 case, arity mismatch, and an OPSYN'd operator whose operands are non-numeric strings (the coercion trap — this is the `#`→`0` witness). Add `scripts/test_gate_opsyn_no_static_identity.sh`: greps `snobol4.y` for any unused-operator symbol mapped to a `TT_` arithmetic kind == 0, and runs the probe set both modes.

**PARKED — PERFORMANCE / ARCHITECTURE, AFTER 100% (law + history in git; do not open before DoD 1-2 are green unless Lon directs):** RTX asm runtime ladders (RTX-4/6/7/9/10/12, RTX-FUNC-1..11 zero-C call path, `bench_rtx_3arm.sh` min-of-N rail) · RTCC RC-5/6/7 (`GOAL-RTCC.md`, kept) · LADDER AB flip (AB-3..6: `jmp [fn_cell]` call sites, runtime-DEFINE generic twin, static-direct fold; the RTX-FUNC-8 expression-position-DEFINE hatch) · LADDER KW native keywords (KW-0..6, `rt_keyword_read_snobol4` → emitted statics) · M-SLEN (populate `DESCR_t.slen`, `rt_match_enter` deletion, register-only MATCH_BEGIN α) · RBX inline bump-alloc arms (X-3) · OS-0..OS-FINAL ONE-SYSTEM addressing consolidation (24 `fc_*` + compensation terms — collapses naturally as R-4 re-homes storage; finish as hygiene) · MECH M-4 glue unification · `-O2` runtime pass (⛔⛔ **NO `-O2` BUILDS. EVER (Lon, 2026-08-23 s262 FACT RULE — this SUPERSEDES the O0-DEV-O2-BENCH text that stood here, which is DEAD and must not be followed).** `RT_OPT` is `-O0` for development AND benchmarks AND demos. Never pass `RT_OPT="-O2 …"`, never build an `-O2` RT_TAG, never quote an `-O2` number as the current state. TWO REASONS, the second the stronger: cost (~9m30 vs ~1m40 per template-touching rebuild, paid on every arm of a measure-and-cure loop), and ⭐ **it measures a compiler we are deleting** — the RT is moving to register-aware ASM (`GOAL-RTCC.md`, `src/runtime/rtx/*.S`) with C kept for at most a few large algorithms, so an `-O2` figure grades gcc's optimizer over code that will not exist. The LABELING duty survives and now reads `-O0`. Authority: `RULES.md` § NO `-O2` BUILDS).
